# Muktā-caritram \[BS\]

## Story of the pearls

*namaḥ śrī-gāndharvikā-giridharābhyām*  
*kandarpa-koṭi-ramyāya sphurad-indīvara-tviṣe \|*  
*jagan-mohana-līlāya namo gopendra-sūnave \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to the son of Nanda, whose pastimes enchant the universe, whose complexion is that of a shining blue lotus, who is as attractive as ten million Cupids.**

*kraya-vikraya-khelābdau muktānāṁ majjitātmanoḥ \|*  
*mitho jayārthinor vande rādhā-mādhavayor yugam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa, absorbed in being victorious in the pastimes of buying and selling the pearls.**

*nijām ujjvalitāṁ bhakti-sudhām arpayituṁ kṣitau \|*  
*uditaṁ taṁ śacī-garbha-vyomni pūrṇaṁ vidhuṁ bhaje \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I worship the full moon who has arisen in the sky of Śaci’s womb, in order to offer to the earth the nectar of shining bhakti (mādhurya-rasa) to himself.**

*nāma-śreṣṭhaṁ manum api śacīputram atra svarūpaṁ*  
*śrī-rūpaṁ tasyāgrajam uru-purīṁ mâthurīṁ goṣṭhavāṭīm \|*  
*rādhā-kuṇḍaṁ girivaram aho rādhikā-mādhavāśāṁ*  
*prāpto yasya prathita-kṛpayā śrī-guruṁ taṁ nato’smi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I offer respects to the best name, to the mantra, to Lord Caitanya, to Svarūpa Dāmodara, to Rūpa Gosvāmī, to Sanātana Gosvāmī, to the great city of Mathurā, to the cowherd forests, to Rādhā-kuṇḍa, to Govardhana, and to Śri guru, by whose mercy I have attained aspiration for Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa.**

*hari-caritāmṛta-laharīṁ vṛndā-vipināmbu-rāśim bhūtām*  
*rasavad vṛndāraka-gaṇa-paramānandāya santanumaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I produce sweet waves of the Lord’s pastimes, arising from the abundant waters of Vṛndāvana, for giving the highest bliss to the best devotees who are full of rasa.**

*ekadā kiñcin-mātra-śruta-pūrva-vṛttāntayā satyabhāmayā kṛṣṇaḥ sākūtaṁ pṛṣṭaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**One day Satyabhāmā, who had heard a little of his previous activities, inquired with purpose from Kṛṣṇa.**

*latās tā madhurāḥ kasmin jāyante dhanya-nivṛti-nātha mat-kaṅkaṇa-nyastaṁ yāsāṁ muktāphalaṁ phalam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**O Lord, covered in wealth! Where did the sweet creepers whose fruit are the pearls set in my bracelet arise?**

*Tatas tad-vṛttānta-smaraṇât kṛṣṇaḥ svāntar-glānir bahir vihasyāha \|*  
*gataḥ sa kālo yatrāsīt muktānāṁ janma valliṣu \|*  
*vartante sāmprataṁ tāsāṁ hetavaḥ śukti-sampuṭāḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Remembering that incident, Kṛṣṇa, internally sad, externally laughed and spoke. “The time has passed where pearls arise from creepers. The cause of pearls is now the oyster.”**

*tatas tad apūrvaṁ śrutvā sa-viśeṣa-śravaṇotkaṇṭhitayā tayā bāḍham āmreḍitaḥ kṛṣṇaḥ punar āha \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this remarkable statement, she insistently repeated her question with longing to hear the details. Kṛṣṇa again spoke.**

*ekadā kārttike māsi gokule govardhana-girau dīpa-mālikā-mahotsava āsīt \| tatra sarvo jano vicitra-nepathya-sāmagrīṇāṁ saṁskārāya sādhanāya ca paramāśakto babhūva \| gopās tu svaṁ svaṁ bhūṣayanto’pi viśeṣataḥ gavādi-paśūnāṁ prasādhanārtham udyuktā āsan \| gopyaḥ kila prasādhita-sadanā ātmanāṁ bhūṣaṇārthaṁ nānālaṅkāra-saṁskāra-sādhana-parā babhūvuḥ \| rādhā tu mālya- haraṇākhya-saras-tīropānta-mādhavī-catuḥ-śālikāyāṁ sva-sakhī-samudayena paramottama-muktābhir vividha-bhūṣaṇāni racayitum ārebhe \|*

**Перевод:**

**At one time during Kārtika month, at Govardhana in Gokula, there was a great lamp festival. Everyone was completely absorbed in cleaning and preparing the collection of colorful decorations. Having decorated themselves, the cowherds were especially intent on decorating their cows and other animals. The cowherd women, after decorating their houses, were busy in cleaning and making various ornaments to decorate themselves. Rādhā began making various ornaments out of the finest pearls with all her friends in a cottage of mādhavī creepers, composed of four rooms on the bank of the lake called Mālya-haraṇa.**

*mayā ca vicakṣaṇa iti yathārtha-nāma-kīra-kumāra-mukhatas tan niśamya sa- kautukaṁ tatra gatvātipremāspadasya haṁsī-hariṇīti nāmno dhenu-yugalasya*  
*bhūṣaṇautsukyena tāsāṁ sakāśe mauktikāni prārthitāni \|\| tataḥ suvividha-vaidagdhya- parimala-parilasita-sāmi-nimilita-vāma-nayana-nīlotpala-dalāñcalena sāvahelanam iva manāg eva mām avalokya muni-vrata-jatu-mudrita-hasita-hīrakānarghya-mahā-ratna- bahiḥ-prakāśaṁ nirbharāveśa-viśeṣeṇa hārādi-gumphana-vilāsam eva vitanvatīs tāḥ prati sasmitam idam aham ābhāṣitavān \| abhinava-yauvanā-mūlya-cintāmaṇi-labdhi- vivardhitottuṅga-garva-mahā-parvatāvaguṇṭhita-karṇyaḥ ! priya-vayasyasya mama prārthite kṣaṇam api karṇān udghāṭayantu bhavatyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**I heard this from the mouth of a young parrot appropriately named Vicakṣaṇa (the wise one). Going there with joy, eager to decorate my two cows named Haṁsi and Harinī, who were objects of my love, I requested some pearls from the gopīs. They glanced at me for a second with contempt, out of the corner of their half-closed left eyes, which were comparable to blue lotuses, radiating the fragrance of various types of cleverness.**  
**With a smile I spoke to them, as they continued their activities of stringing their necklaces with the greatest absorption, while revealing the great, priceless diamond of their smiles, covered by the lac of their determined silence. “O women whose ears are covered by the great mountain of swelling pride increased from attaining the valuable touchstone of new youth! Please lend your ears for a moment only to my request, since I am your dear friend.”**

*tatas tāsām īṣat smitvānyonyam ālokayantīnāṁ madhye pragalbhā lalitā vihasya saroṣam iva vyājahāra \| aye nāgara ! etāni bahu-mūlyāni mauktikāni rāja-mahiṣī- yogyāni \| tava mahiṣīnām evālaṅkriyāyai satyam abhirūpāṇi kathaṁ na dāsyāmaḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**As they glanced at each other while smiling slightly, bold Lalitā laughed and spoke as if angry. “O lecher! This multitude of valuable pearls is suitable for a queen (*****mahīṣi*****). Yes, should we not give these beautiful pearls to you, just to decorate your female buffaloes (*****mahiśī*****)?”**

*tatas tad-vacaḥ śrutvā mayā kautukenāviṣṭena sāmnā punaḥ punar idam evoktam \| bhoḥ priya-bhūṣaṇāḥ ! sarvāṇi yadi na deyāni tadā mad-atyanta-priya-dhenu-yugala- śṛṅga-paryāptāny evāvaśyaṁ dīyantām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing her words, I spoke again with equal humor. “O nicely decorated women! If you do not give all the ornaments, then you should at least give enough to decorate the horns of my two dear cows.”**

*tato lalitā sarvāsāṁ mauktikāni punaḥ punaś cālayantī bhūyaḥ prāha \| he kṛṣṇa ! kiṁ kartavyam ? tava dhenu-yogyam ekam api nāsti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Moving all the pearls around, Lalitā again spoke. “O Kṛṣṇa! What can I do? There is not even one pearl suitable for your cows.”**

*tadāham uktavān \| ayi parama-cature lalite ! tiṣṭha tiṣṭha \| ahaṁ kārpaṇya-bhâk paścāt tvayā vaktuṁ na śakya ity upālabhya satvaram ambāṁ vrajeśvarīm āgatya mātar dehi me mauktikāni \| ahaṁ tu tāni kṛṣṭa-kedārikāyām eva vapsyāmīti punaḥ punar avocam*  
*\| tato mātrâ vihasyoktaṁ \| vatsa na mauktikāny uptāni prarohanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then I said, “O most clever Lalitā! Please stop this! You will not be able to call me a miser later on.”**  
**Scolding her, I quickly went to mother and repeatedly said, “O mother! Give me some pearls.” I will plant them in the ploughed field.”**  
**Mother then laughed and said, “O son! Pearls are not sown and grown like plants.”**

*tadā* ***mayoktaṁ*** *\| mātar avaśyam eva dīyantāṁ dina-trayābhyantare prarūḍhāni bhavaty eva sākṣât kartavyānīty asman-nirbandha-parihārāsamarthayā tayā dattāni bahūni mauktikāni \| mayā gokula-jalāharaṇa-ghaṭṭa-samīpa-vartini yamunopakaṇṭhe pauruṣa-trayam avagāḍhāṁ kedarikāṁ niṣpādya sahāsaṁ paśyantīṣu kāsucid gopikāsu tany uptvā sā punar apūri \| parito vṛtiś ca kāṣṭhair niviḍā kṛtā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then I said, “O mother! You must give me the pearls. In three days, they will sprout. This will visibly happen.”**  
**She could not avoid my insistent demand, and gave me many pearls. Making a low field the length of three men in a garden on the bank of the Yamunā near Jala-haraṇa-ghaṭta in Gokula, I planted the pearls while some gopīs looked on, laughing. I filled up the holes with earth. I make a thick fence out of sticks.**

*tatas tāsāṁ mauktika-prārthana-vacanāsvara-nirāsārthaṁ kedārikāsekāya vayasa-dvārā gavyeṣu prārthyamâneṣu solluṇṭhaṁ vihasya tāḥ procuḥ \| tan-mauktika-kedārikāseko’smākaṁ gavyena katham ucitaḥ \| yat priya-go-bhūṣaṇārthaṁ kṛta-mauktika-kedārikāsekas tāsāṁ gavāṁ dugdhenaiva kriyatām \| tat-kedārikotpadyamāne phale nāsmābhir abhilāṣaḥ kartavyaḥ \| iti śrutvā mayā svagṛhāṇāṁ gavāṁ bahubhiḥ payobhireva tā darśayatā pratyahaṁ sekaḥ kriyate sma \|*

**Перевод:**

**Through a friend, I asked them for some milk for watering the field, so they could not later plead for pearls. They laughed sarcastically and said, “Why should our cows’ milk be used for watering the field of pearls? The field should be watered by the milk of your dear cows for whom you have made the pearl field. We do not have a desire for the pearls produced from that field.”**  
**Hearing that, I daily watered the field with lots of milk from my own cows. This was done while showing the fields to the gopīs.**

*tataś caturtha-divasa eva mauktikāni praūḍhāni \| tad-darśanenollasitena mayā mātur añcalaṁ gṛhītvānīya tad aṅkurā darśitāḥ \| tān dṛṣṭvā tu mātā kim etad iti manasi vicārayantī sandigdhā satī vrajaṁ jagāma \| gopyas tu tacchrutvā hiṁsrā latāḥ prarūḍhā iti parasparaṁ sahāsam ūcire \|*

**Перевод:**

**On the fourth day, the pearls sprouted. Overjoyed on seeing this, I pulled mother by her cloth and showed her the sprouts. Seeing them, mother thought, “What is this?” She went to Vraja with a doubtful mind. Hearing of this, the gopīs spoke among themselves with laughter, “Only some weeds have grown up.”**

*tato nātivilambena mūrvākāriṇyas tā mauktikalatā vistāriṇyaḥ samālocya samīpavartināṁ kadambānām upariṣṭhāt krameṇāvarohitāḥ \| tataḥ katipayair divasais tāḥ saurabhonmādita-madhuvrataiḥ kusuma-nicayair gopīnāṁ camatkāram ātanvānāḥ sakalaṁ gokulam evādhyavasāyan \|*

**Перевод:**

**Very quickly the pearl creepers grew like hemp plants, spreading out everywhere. They began climbing nearby kadamba trees. In a few days the creepers astonished the gopīs by producing flowers which intoxicated the bees with their fragrance. All of Gokula came to examine the creepers.**

*tato’ṣṭa-vidha-yonija-mauktikebhyo vilakṣaṇāṁ śriyaṁ dadhati muktāphalāni tāsu jātāni tāni dṛṣṭvā vraja-vāsināṁ vismayo’tyarthaṁ jātaḥ \| viśeṣataś ca gopīnām \| tataḥ praty aham eva tad-darśanena jāta-lobhās tāḥ mantrayāñ cakrire \| bhoḥ sakhyaḥ bhadrena jñātam asmabhyaḥ kṛṣṇo mauktikāni sarvathaiva na dāsyatyeva \| bhavatu \| tat-kṛta-mauktika-kṛṣi-prakriyāsmābhir na dṛṣṭāstīti na syāt \| tatrānadhyavasāyaṁ tyaktvā tad-dviguṇikāyāḥ kedārikāyayā ārambhaḥ kathaṁ na kriyate ?*

**Перевод:**

**The pearls had more remarkable beauty than pearls arising from eight sources. The people of Vraja, especially the gopīs, were most astonished on seeing these pearls arise from creepers. Coming to see the pearls every day, the gopīs became greedy for them and began to discuss among themselves.**  
**“O friends! We very well know that Kṛṣṇa will not give the pearls to us at all. Let that be. It is not that we have not seen the method by which he cultivated the pearls. Let us not be lazy. Can we not cultivate a field twice as big as his?”**

*iti śrutvāticaturā lalitā prāha -- bhoḥ pavana-vyādhi-vyāpṛtā gopyaḥ ! govardhanoddharaṇādi-bhūmi-mauktikotpādanādikaṁ lokottaraṇām api duṣkaraṁ karma \| tena yad añjasā kriyate tat kila mahā-siddhato labdha-siddhauṣadhi-mantrādi- prabhāvād eveti samasta-vraja-janair niścitam eva \| anyathāsmad-vrajendra-gṛhiṇī garbha-sarovarotpanna-sukomala-nīlotpalasya jñāta-sva-gopa-jāti-kriyā-kalāpa- mātrasya gopālakasya tasya tat-tat-karaṇe katham etāvatī śaktiḥ svataḥ sambhaved iti jānantyo’pi bhavatyaḥ siddhauṣadhi-mantrādy-antareṇa yat-tat-karmaṇi pravartitum abhilaṣanti \| tat khalu pariṇatāvagādha-lajjā-parihāsa-sāgarāntaḥ-sva-patanāyaiveti satyam avadhāryatām\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, clever Lalitā spoke. “O girls inflicted with insanity! Making pearls grow from the land of Govardhana and other places is very difficult even for the best of men. What he did with ease was because of the power of mantras and herbs obtained from a great yogī. This can be confirmed by all the people of Vraja. Otherwise, how could this cowherd who knows only the activities of a cowherd, who appeared in the womb of Nanda’s wife, like a tender blue lotus in a pond, develop power to do this on his own? Knowing this, you desire to undertake this project without using powerful herbs and mantras. As a result you will be plunged into an ocean of mockery and great embarrassment. Please understand this.”**

*tatas tuṅgavidyā prāha -- asmābhir api śrī-bhagavatī-pāda-padma-siddha-mantra-śiṣyā- nāndīmukhī-sakāśāt siddha-mantram ekam ādāya kathaṁ na tathodyamaḥ kriyate ?*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā spoke. “Why can we not try this after taking an effective mantra from Nāndīmukhī, the student of effective mantras which she obtained from the lotus feet of Paurṇamāsī?”**

*sarvāḥ -- bhadraṁ vadati tuṅgavidyeti nirṇīya tat-pārśvam upetya sa-vinayam ātmābhilāṣaṁ nivedayāmāsuḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone said, “Tuṅgavidyā speaks wisely.” They then went to Nāndīmukhī and politely revealed their desires.**

*tato nāndīmukhī svagatamāha -- aye nija-nayana-yugma-dṛṣṭi-sāphalyāya cira-dinam asmad-vidhābhilaṣyamāṇa-kraya-vikraya-krīḍā-kutūhala-kalpataror bāḍham akasmād asmād dṛśāṁ bhāgyātiśaya-vaśāt sākṣād bīja-rūpo’yam avasaraḥ pratyāsanno babhūva \| tad-vidagdhānāṁ śiromaṇīr apy etās tathā yukti-sauṣṭhavātiśayena kṛtye’tra pravartayāmi \| yathā sa tarus tvaritam eva praūḍhaḥ phalavān bhaved iti manasi vicintya sāntarānandaṁ nāndī prāha -- bhoḥ sakhyaḥ \| satyaṁ mukundasya na*  
*mantra-kṛteyaṁ mṛdi mauktikā sṛṣṭiḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said to herself, “Ah! By great fortune, the seed of the desire tree of playful pastimes of buying and selling which I have desired for a long time--a great opportunity--has suddenly arrived to satisfy my two eyes. Since the gopīs are the height of cleverness, I will use skillful logic to accomplish this, so that the mature tree will quickly bear fruit.”**  
**Thinking in this way with internal bliss, Nāndīmukhī then spoke. “O friends! This is not the result of Kṛṣṇa’s mantras. The pearls were produced in the earth.”**

*sarvāḥ – tan nija-janena kāraṇa-śuktyādi-mātram antareṇa kathaṁ mṛttikāyām asambhaveyaṁ tad-utpattiḥ pratīyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone said, “It is impossible that pearls can come from the earth since you also know that pearls come from oysters only. Why do you say something else?”**

*nāndī -- asyā bhuvaḥ svābhāvikedṛk-prabhāvād eva \| yad vividha-ratna-jananīyaṁ vraja-vana-bhūr iti śrī-bhagavatī-pāda-padmaiḥ punaḥ punar nivarṇyate \| ṛtam apy anubhūyante tathā \| yataḥ pravāla-nava-pallava-marakata-chada-vajra-mauktika- prakara-koraka-kamala-rāga-nānā-phalādimanto hiraṇmaya-mahīruhāḥ sphuṭam atra jātā jāyamānāś ca dṛśyante \| ato’syām upta-muktāphalāni janiṣyante phaliṣyanti ceti kiṁ citram ? tad bhavatībhir api surabhitara-navanītādi-seka-pūrvakam atiprayatnena tathā tat-kṛṣiḥ kriyatāṁ yathā tato’py adhikatamāni paramottama-phalāni labhyante \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndimukhī said, “It is because of the natural power of this land. Paurṇamāsī has repeatedly said that the forests and lands of Vraja give rise to various jewels. This has been perceived to be true also since it is clearly seen that golden trees with fruits made of rubies, many diamond and pearl buds, emerald bark, and coral leaves have appeared in the past and are appearing at present. What is so astonishing if you plant pearls and they produce pearl fruits as a result? Therefore, you should cultivate pearls carefully, while watering them with the most fragrant butter. As a result you will obtain the most excellent pearls--better than Kṛṣṇa’s.”**

*iti tad-vacana-mādhurīm āpīya sa-santoṣaṁ sa-ślāghaṁ pratyayantyaḥ sarvās tām āliṅgya nijāṁ sthalīm āgatya spardhayā maj-jayāya samucita-vetanato dviguṇa- triguṇa-gorasa-pradānena karma-kārānānāyya sthāne sthāne kedārikāḥ kṛtvā*  
*peṭikāyām agrathitāni grathitāny aṅga-bhūṣaṇa-rūpāṇi ca yāvanti sthitāni tāvanti yathā-yuktam alpāny eva saṁrakṣyāṅgataḥ samuttāritāni ca mauktikāni niravaśeṣam uptvā pratyaham eva tri-sandhyaṁ go-dugdha-navanīta-suvāsita-ghṛtair eva sekaṁ kartum ārabdhavatyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing her sweet words, all the gopīs, believing her, embraced with satisfaction her while praising her. They returned home and, with desire to defeat me, made fields in various places by engaging workers, paying them double or triple the normal wages in milk. They planted pearls yet unstrung, which were in a box, and pearls which had already been strung, and as well took off the ornaments they were wearing, leaving only a few. Three times a day they began watering the fields with milk, yogurt, butter and fragrant ghee.**

*tatas tāsāṁ muktā-kṛṣi-karaṇa-śravaṇān mātsaryato lobhataśca candrāvalīprabhṛtayo’pi sarvā vallabyas tato’py adhikakedārikāḥ sthāne sthāne kṛtvā deha-gehayor ekam apy asaṁrakṣya samasta-mauktikāny uptavatyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing that they were cultivating pearls, Candrāvalī and others, all other gopīs, out of envy and greed, began cultivating bigger fields in various places, and planted all their pearls, not leaving any even on their bodies or in their houses.**

*tataḥ katipaya-dināntare sva-sva-kedārikāsu jātān hiṁsra latāṅkurān dṛṣṭvā antargarbhita-garvābhir vallabhībhis tābhiḥ sarvābhiś chalato mat-priya-vayasyāḥ parihasyante sma \|*

**Перевод:**

**Some days later, seeing sprouts of weeds in their fields, all these gopīs who were full of pride ridiculed my friends because of the fraud.**

*ekasmin dine go-rasānām atīvavyayaṁ svasvagṛhasya ca nimauktikatāṁ vīkṣya gopāḥ sasaṁrambhaṁ kāraṇaṁ papracchuḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the cowherds noticed that all their homes were without pearls and that there was much less milk. With anger they asked the reason.**

*tacchrutvā vṛddhā ūcuḥ \| bho āyuṣmantaḥ \| nānuyoga-viṣayo’yam ābhir bālābhiḥ kṛto mauktika-kedārikānāṁ nimittaṁ bahulo vyayo’cirād eva bahutara-lābho bhaviṣyati \| yat Kṛṣṇa-kedārikāyāṁ raja-dārāṇām api durlabhāni muktāphalāni kila dṛṣṭāni santīti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing the cowherds, the elder gopīs said, “O long-living men! You should not criticize this. This has been done by the young gopīs. For making fields of pearls they have used up many pearls and lots of milk. But soon there will be abundant gain, since we have seen the pearls of Kṛṣṇa’s field, rare even for queens.”**

*athaikadā viśākhayā sva-kedārikāsu tān aṅkurâ nnirīkṣya kāsāñcit karṇe nibhṛtam idam uktam \| bhoḥ sakhyaḥ \| Kṛṣṇa-kedārikāmadhye mayā yādṛśā aṅkurā dṛṣṭā ete tu tādṛśā na dṛśyante \| na jāne paścāt kiṁ bhavet \| Kṛṣṇa-vayasya-dṛṣṭi-nivāraṇārthaṁ chalataḥ suṣṭhu veṣṭyantām \|*

**Перевод:**

**One day Viśākhā, inspecting the sprouts in their fields, spoke in the ear of some gopīs in private. “O friends! I do not see sprouts similar to those in Kṛṣṇa’s field. I do not know what will happen next. Slyly, we must hide them in order to prevent Kṛṣṇa’s friends from seeing them.”**

*atha katipaya-divasais tāsāṁ rādhādīnām anyāsāṁ ca kedārikāsu kaṇṭakādi-cihnena latābhir nijarūpe prakāśyamāne gopī-kedārikāsu hiṁsrā latā jātā iti sakalagokula eva khyātam \|*

**Перевод:**

**After some days, all of Gokula knew that weeds had grown in the gopīs’ fields when they saw thorn bushes in Rādhā’s and other gopīs’ fields.**

*etad ākarṇya vayasya-dvārā gāndharvā-goṣṭhyāṁ mayedaṁ sotprāsaṁ vijñāpitaṁ śrutaṁ bhavatīnāṁ kedārikāsu bahūni muktā-phalāni jātāni santi \| ahaṁ tāvat snigdhas tat sa-vayasyāya mahyaṁ prathama-phalāni dīyantām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this through friends, I announced derisively in the gopīs’ assembly, “I have heard that in your fields many pearl fruits have grown. I am a great friend. You should give the first pearl fruits to me, your friend.”**

*tatas tābhir uktaṁ \| yadi vayaṁ kṛṣim akariṣyāmas tadā sakala-goṣṭhaṁ mauktikamayam abhaviṣyat \| paśupālya-svadharmam apahāya sa iva kaḥ kilāsa-vṛttimāśrayet \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs said, “If we had taken to ploughing fields, all of the village would be full of pearls. Giving up our occupation of protecting the cows like him, who would take shelter of a leprous occupation like him?”**

*tad ākarṇya mayā nikhila-vayasyāḥ savatsā gāvaḥ śakaṭa-vahā mahiṣyaḥ, savarkarā ajā, vṛndāvana-vartinyaś ca mauktika-mayair mālādy-alaṅkārair maṇḍitāḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**On hearing this, with ornaments and garlands made of pearls, I decorated all my friends, the cows with their calves, the buffaloes, oxen pulling the carts, and the goats with their young which dwelled in Vṛndāvana.**

*tataś ca gopyo lajjayā sva-bhūṣaṇa-vyatirekeṇa bahu-dhana-vināśena gopa-bhiyā ca kim atra yuktam iti mantrayitum ārebhire \| aye gṛhīta-kṛṣṇa-pakṣayā dhūrta- nāndīmukhyā’bhadreṇa pratāritāḥ sma ity ābhāṣya sarosaṁ tan-nikaṭam āgatya tat-*  
*kathana-pūrvakaṁ bahuśas tābhir bhartsitā nāndīmukhī prāha \| bhoḥ tapobhyaḥ śape na mayā sarvathaiva pratāritāḥ stha, kintu yuṣmābhir eva tat sarvaṁ vināśitam \|*

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs began discussing what to do, out of shame and out of fear of the cowherd men, because they had lost so much wealth and even their own ornaments.**  
**“We have unfortunately been cheated by deceptive Nāndīmukhī, who is siding with Kṛṣṇa.”**  
**Going to her house in anger they scolded her heavily, while relating what had happened.**  
**Nāndīmukhī then spoke. “Oh! I swear on my austerities that I have not cheated you. Everything has been destroyed by you alone.”**

*sarvāḥ \| kapaṭini kathaṁ-kāram \|*

**Перевод:**

**The gopīs said, “O deceptive Nāndimukhī! How did this happen?”**

*nāndī \| yato garvitābhir yuṣmābhir ḍhakkā-vādyavat kolāhala-prapañcena savayasya- gaṇasya tasya śravaṇa-kuhare suṣṭhu-gocarīkṛtya kedārikāsu muktāḥ sphuṭam uptāḥ tathā ko’py ekaḥ prahariko’pi tatra na rakṣitaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndimukhī said, “You openly planted the pearls in the fields with loud announcements as if beating drums, so that Kṛṣṇa and his friends could clearly hear. And not even one guard protected the fields.”**

*sarvāḥ -- etāvatā kiṁ na jātam ?*

**Перевод:**

**They said, “Why did nothing come up from doing that?”**

*nāndī saroṣam \| yaj jātaṁ tac caturaṁ manyābhiḥ śrūyatām \| yuṣmān vijetum abhīṣṭa-miṣṭānna-pradānena suṣṭhu pralobhya dhūrta-guruṇā yuṣman-nāgareṇa tena prerita- lolupa-bhaṇḍa-madhumaṅgalenāti-nirbaddhato vicitya kiñcij jātāṅkurāḥ sarvā muktā niravaśeṣaṁ samādāya tatra tatra hiṁsrāvallī-kadambān samāropya kayatyaḥ pṛthak svakṛta ekasmin kedāre pratnataḥ saṁropitāḥ \| tathānyāsāṁ tu tathaivānīya kālindī- gabhīra-nīrāntaḥ prakṣiptāḥ \| iti mayā sudṛḍhaṁ jñātam asti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī spoke with anger. “Hear what happened. You think you are clever. Making Madhumaṅgala greedy by giving him sweet rice, your lecher, guru of rascals, in order to defeat you, had that greedy buffoon gather up with great effort all the pearls which had just sprouted and had him plant lots of weeds in their place. Some of the pearls were carefully planted in his field. The rest were taken and thrown in the depth of the Yamunā. I know this for certain.”**

*iti niśamya sarvā āhuḥ \| ayi kūṭatva-nāṭaka-naṭana-prakaṭanaika-kāryā-nindya- mahā-nandi \| ayi bhaṇḍa-madhumaṅgala-guruprāya-mahā-satīrthe \| ayi vrajaprathita- śaṭha-naṭa-sahita-nāṭya-yogye \| tat-priyatame naṭi \| ayi kaliyuga-tapasvini \| tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti sabhrūbhaṅgaṁ tām ākṣipya sva-geham āgatya punaḥ punas tad eva vicārayantī tāsu rādhā bhoḥ sakhyaḥ nāndīmukhī vā pratārayatu sa dhūrtas tathā vā karotu adhunā tad-vicāreṇa ko lābhaḥ \| sāmpratam anyad duḥkhaṁ na gaṇyate \| gurvādito bhayam eva naḥ khedayati \| tad vayaṁ tu tebhyo mauktikeṣu darśiteṣv eva śithilī bhavet \| tādṛśāni sarvathaivātra sudurlabhāni kintu kṛṣṇād eva mūlyena yathā gṛhītāni syuḥ \| tatropāyaś cintyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, the gopīs said, “O great director of despicable dealings! You have revealed yourself to be a dancer in a drama of crookedness! O guru of the buffoon Madhumaṅgala! You are his co-conspirator! O most suitable woman to dance with the cunning dancer of Vraja! O dearest dancer of Kṛṣṇa! O renunciate woman of Kali-yuga! Stop speaking!”**  
**Scolding her in this way with frowns, they returned home and then again began to reflect upon the matter.**  
**Among them, Rādhā spoke. “O friends! Maybe Nāndīmukhī tricked us, or maybe the rascal did it. What do we gain from deciding this? Another problem you are not considering at this time. Fear of our elders gives us pain. If we show them the pearls their anger will subside. Such pearls are very rare here. We could however get some from Kṛṣṇa for a fair price. We should think of a method of doing this.”**

*tataḥ sarvābhir vibhāvyoktam \| candramukhī caturā bhavati \| suvarṇāni gṛhītvā gatvā samucita-mūlyena mauktikāny ānayatu \|*

**Перевод:**

**They all thought about this and then said, “Candramukhī is clever. She can take some money and go to him. Paying a proper price, let her bring the pearls here.”**

*tatas tayoktam \| sampraty asmābhir upālabdhasya tasya samīpam aham ekākinī gantum aśaktāsmi \| kāñcanalatā mama saṅge samāgacchatu \| iti sarvāsām anumatyā bahu-suvarṇāni gṛhītvā mauktika-vāṭikā-samīpaṁ te ājagmatuḥ \| tatra tad- vāṭikādhikāriṇaṁ subalaṁ mayā saha niviṣṭam abrūtām \| he subala śrutam asmābhir bhavadbhir navya-muktāni vikrīyante \| tad imāni śuddha-suvarṇāni gṛhītvā samucita- mūlyena pravīṇa-pravīṇa-muktā-phalāni dīyantām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Candramukhī then spoke. “I cannot go alone since we have scolded him. Kāñcana-latā should go with me.”**  
**With everyone’s permission the two took a lot of money and went to the pearl garden. They spoke to Subala, the superintendent of the garden, who was sitting with me.**  
**“O Subala! We have heard that you are selling the newly grown pearls. Take these pure gold coins and for a proper price give us the best pearl fruits.”**

*tataḥ smitvā mayoktam \| tadānīm anekadhā prārthyamānaṁ naikam api mauktikam asmabhyaṁ dattam \| asmat-kedārikā-sekārthaṁ dugdha-pāli ca bhavatībhir na dattā \| asmābhir varaṁ kālindī-madhye prakṣeptavyam \| tathāpi bhavatīnāṁ gṛha- sarvasvenāpi paṇīkṛtena nikhila-mauktika-vṛndādapakṛṣṭam ekam api mauktikaṁ sarvathā na dātavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Smiling I said, “Recently, though I requested countless times, you did not give even one pearl to me. You did not even give a pot of milk for watering my field. I would rather throw the pearls in the Yamunā. I will not give even the worst pearl, even if you offer all the wealth in your houses.”**

*tataḥ kāñcanalatayoktam \| mauktika-nimittaṁ paty-ādibhyo yadi tāsāṁ bhīr na bhavet tadāsya kadarthanoktim īdṛśīṁ kā nāma saheta \| bhavatu kiṁ kartavyam \| haṭṭa-*  
*prasārita-bahu-ratnā madhurā tāvad dūre tad adya bhoḥ subala svayam eva bhavatā madhyasthena bhūyatām \| ānyatrika-mūlyād asmābhir viśeṣo’pi dātavyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kāñcana-latā then spoke. “If the gopīs were not afraid of their husbands and elders because of the pearls, how could they tolerate your harsh words? In any case, what should be done? Mathurā has many jewels available in the market, but that is far away. At this time, Subala, you should be the negotiator. We will pay more than the rate elsewhere.”**

*tataś ca viśeṣa-śabda-śravaṇān mayā vihasyoktam \| bhavatu nāma svabhāva-komalena mayā tu bhavatībhir iva kāṭhinyaṁ kartuṁ na śakyate \| adattvā vā anyat kiṁ kartavyam \| kintu mauktikārdhinīnāṁ sarvāsāṁ mauktika-mūlya-nirṇayaḥ kiṁ bhavatībhyām eva bhaviṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing that they would pay more, I began to laugh. “Let it be. I cannot be hard with you, as you have been with me, since my nature is very soft. And if I do not give the pearls, what will I do with them? Will you two determine the price of the pearls for all those who want pearls?”**

*tābhyām uktam -- atha kim ?*

**Перевод:**

**The two said, “Yes, then?”**

*mayoktam \| kas tāvad viśeṣaḥ \| tataś candramukhī kiñcid vihasya kāñcanalatām ālokitavatī \| kāñcanalatātha salajjaṁ subalaṁ prati vyājahāra \| sakhe subala svayam eva madhyasthena bhūtvā samādhāya samīcīna-yaśo-bhāras tāvad aṅgīkriyatāṁ bhavatā \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “What are the details of this transaction?”**  
**Candramukhī then laughed slightly and glanced at Kāñcana-latā. Kāñcana-latā then spoke to Subala with shyness. “O my friend Subala! Being the negotiator, by resolving this issue, you will receive your well-deserved fame.”**

*subalenoktam \| vayasya rahasyatayā bahu-mūlyatvaṁ kiyat kāñcanalatayā prakaṭīkriyate \| ātmano’bhīṣṭa-mūlyaṁ svayam eva sphuṭaṁ nigadya na kathaṁ gṛhyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “O my friend Kṛṣṇa! Secretive Kāñcana-latā is not revealing how much more they are willing to pay. Why don’t you just clearly state how much you want them to pay?”**

*tato’ham abruvam \| sakhe subala candramukhyā abhiprāyo jñātaḥ \| muktāphalāni grahītuṁ kāñcanalataiva vicāryānargha-mūlyatvena parikalpya rādhādibhiḥ prahitya mama dattāstīti \| kintu kāñcana-sañcayato’pi muktāphalānām adhikaṁ mūlyaṁ jagati prasiddham \| tasmād ekayaiva kāñcanalatayā katham etāsāṁ mūlya-paryāptiḥ \| yadi vā asyā vakṣasi svarṇa-sampūṭa-rūpa-phala-dvaye bahavaś cintāmaṇayo’pi santīti candramukhī vakṣati tathāpi na \| yato vaikuṇṭhanātha-kaṇṭha-sthita-kaustubhato’pi mamaitad ekaṁ muktāphalaṁ parama-parārdhyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**I then spoke. “O my friend Subala! I understand Candramukhī's intentions. Kāñcana-latā (golden creeper) has been sent by Rādhā, as they think she is priceless, and is to be given to me in order to receive the pearls. But it is well known in the world that the price of pearl fruits is greater than piles of gold. Therefore how can they make a sufficient price by offering just one golden creeper? If Candramukhī says that there are many cintāmaṇi gems in the golden caskets on Kāñcana-latā’s chest, it will not be sufficient, since one of my pearl fruits is millions of times more valuable than the Kaustubha on Viṣṇu’s throat.”**

*tac chrutvā bhrū-bhaṅgena mām avalokayantī kāñcanalatā saroṣam āha – nirbuddhike candramukhi ! tadaiva* ***mayoktaṁ*** *tasya dhṛṣṭasya savidhe mayā na gantavyam \| tathāpi tvayāham āgraheṇānīya kadarthitāsmi \| tvaṁ muktāphalāny ādāya samāgaccha aham itaś calitāsmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Kāñcana-latā, glaring at me with a frown, spoke in anger. “O foolish Candramukhī! I have just told you that I will not go near that rascal. But you insisted on bringing me, and now I am insulted by him. You should get the pearls and return. I am going from here right now.”**

*candramukhī āha – kāñcanalate ! satyaṁ kathayasi tat katham ekākinyā mayā mūlya- nirṇayo bhavatu \| kathaṁ vā nijane’tra sthātavyaṁ ekayoga-nirdiṣṭānāṁ saha vā pravṛttiḥ saha vā nivṛttir iti mayāpi gantavyam \| ity ubhe eva gamanodyate dṛṣṭvā mayoktam – subala ! tadaiva mayoktaṁ yad etābhyāṁ mūlya-nirṇayo na bhaviṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Candramukhī said, “O Kāñcana-latā! You are correct. But how can I negotiate the price alone? How can I remain here alone? Those who are assigned to work together should accomplish the task together or abandon it together. I will also leave.”**  
**Seeing them about to leave, I said, “O Subala! Just now I told you that these two could not negotiate the price.”**

*tac chrutvā tayoḥ savidham āgatya subalenoktam – sakhi candramukhi ! vayasyasya mūlya-viṣaye mahān āgraho dṛśyate \| tena priya-sakhī rādhā lalitādibhiḥ sahāgatya samakṣam eva samucita-mūlyaṁ pradāya svepsita-muktāphalāni gṛhṇātu \| tatra mayā madhyasthena bhūtvā sācivyaṁ karaṇīyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Subala approached the two and said, “My friend Candramukhī! My friend is very eager to settle on a price. Therefore our dear friend Rādhā along with Lalitā and others should personally come and, giving a fair price, should take the pearls they want. As the negotiator, I can assist.”**

*iti niśamya candramukhī-kāñcanalate tāsāṁ savidhe gatvā sa-roṣam iva sarvaṁ vṛttaṁ kathayāṁcakratuḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Candramukhī and Kāñcana-latā returned to the gopīs and angrily told them what happened.**

*tataś ca rādhā lalitādibhiḥ saha mauktika-bāṭī-prāntam āsādya candramukhī-dvārā subalam ākārya tam āha – priya-vayasya subala \| asmāsu bhavato niraṅkuśaḥ snehaḥ \| ataḥ svayam eva tathā vidhīyatāṁ yathāsmābhiḥ samucita-mūlyena muktāphalāni labhyante \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then Rādhā with Lalitā and others went to the pearl garden and had Candramukhī call Subala. She said to him, “O my friend Subala! You have unlimited affection for us. You should act so that we can obtain the pearl fruits at the correct price.”**

*tac chrutvā subalena mahyaṁ nivedya mad-vacanena mat-sannidhau tā lalitādayaḥ samānītāḥ \| rādhā tu mamātrāgamanaṁ tvayā mad-upadrāvaka-sva-priya-vayasyāya sarvathā na prakāśanīyam iti subalam ābhāṣya man-nikaṭa-stha-kadamba-kuñjāntar nikhila-vṛttāntaṁ niśamayantī nigūḍham āsthitā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Subala informed me and on my order brought Lalitā and others. But Rādhā said to him, “Do not tell your dear friend who harasses me that I have come.” She hid herself and listened to all the transactions from within a bower of kadamba trees near me.**

*tato mayā tāḥ sarvā nibhālya rādhā kathaṁ na dṛśyate ity ukte tuṅgavidyayoktam – gokula-yuva-rāja ! sā khalu sa-praṇayaṁ āryayā jaṭilayā kasyacid gṛha-kārya-viśeṣasya kṛte rakṣitā gṛhe vidyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**When I saw all the gopīs I said, “Why Rādhā cannot be seen?” Tuṅgavidyā said, “O price of Gokula! Respected Jaṭilā, out of affection, has kept her at home to do some special chores.”**

*tatas tad-avasara eva praviṣṭena madhumaṅgalena iṅgita-vijñāpita-rādhā-nigūḍha- man-nikaṭa-sthitim avadhārya kiñcid vihasya mayoktam – tuṅgavidye ! muktā- grahaṇecchā tasyā na vidyate iti dṛśyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**At that moment, Madhumaṅgala entered and signaled to me that Rādhā had concealed herself near me. Understanding that, I laughed and said, “O Tuṅgavidyā! I see that she has no desire to get the pearls.”**

*tatas tayoktaṁ – nahi nahi \| tan-muktā-mūlyam asmābhir eva dāsyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**She then said “That is not so. The price of the pearls will only be paid by us.”**

*tadā mayoktaṁ – viśākhaiva rādhā, rādhaiva viśākhā \| tatas tan-mūlyaṁ viśākhaiva dāsyatīti jñāyate \| bhavatu tatra samāgraho nāsti, kintu yā khalu svayam āgatya na gṛhṇāti tasyāś catur-guṇaṁ mūlyaṁ gṛhyatām \| mauktikāni ca sādhāraṇāny eva dīyantām iti me sarveṣāṁ sakhīnāṁ sudṛḍho nirṇayaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Viśākhā is identical with Rādhā, and Rādhā is identical with Viśākhā. Thus Viśākhā can pay the price. Let that be! But I am no so eager about this transaction. Whoever does not come personally to take the pearls must pay four times the price. And I will give them ordinary pearls. This is what all my friends have definitely decided.”**

*tad anu subalaṁ prati mayoktaṁ – sakhe subala ! avacita muktā-pūrṇa-sampuṭān ānīya purataḥ prasārya vicitya sarva-kaniṣṭhāny eva mauktikāni pūrvakṛtaṁ tat- kārpaṇyam apy avigaṇayya prathamaṁ tat-kṛte viśākhāyāṁ samarpya tat-sakāśāc ca tan-mūlyaṁ gṛhyatām \| yadi vā prastutaṁ dātuṁ na śaknoti tatas tad-abhinneyam \| tāvat puṣpa-pravāla-corikā gopa-kanyakā yatra rakṣyante tatraivārān mādhavī-kuñja- kārāyāṁ saṁbadhya rakṣyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then I said to Subala, “O my friend Subala! Bring the chests filled with the harvested pearls and spread them out in front. Pick out the worst pearls, and considering her previous stinginess, first give those to Viśākhā on Rādhā’s behalf and take the money. If she cannot give the required price, then imprison her, since she is non-different from Rādhā, in the mādhavī bower prison near here, where the gopīs who stole flowers and buds are retained.”**

*tac chrutvā madhumaṅgalenoktaṁ – priya-vayasya \| rodhe’pi para-rāmābhiḥ palāyana-vidyāḥ sphuṭam adhītāḥ santi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Madhumaṅgala said, “Dear friend! Though imprisoned, these women who enjoy with other men have clearly studied the art of escaping.”**

*tadā mayoktaṁ – vayasya ! mayāpy etad jñāyata eva \| kintu cintā nāsti \| yadyapi para- rāmā-sparśo lajjā-tyāgaś cāsmad-vidhānāṁ svapne’py atīvāyogyas tathāpi –*  
*sva-kāryam uddharet prājñaḥ kurvann api vigarhitaḥ \|*  
*tathā, āhāre vyavahāre ca lajjām api parityajet \|*  
*iti saṁhitā-vacana-balād aham eva prahariko bhavan sarvām eva rātriṁ jāgran nirantaraṁ nivasyāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I then said, “O friend! This I also know, but do not worry. Even though it is most inappropriate for me, even in dreams, to touch another man’s woman, giving up all shyness, it is said, ‘The wise man, though criticized, should attain his goals.’ Also it is said, ‘In eating and transactions, one should give up shyness.’ On the strength of these scriptural statements, I will remain here constantly, staying awake all night standing watch.”**

*iti niśamya subalaḥ sa-smitam āha – puruṣottama ! priya-sakhyā viśākhayā etāvati mahā-saṅkaṭe kiyantaṁ kālaṁ sthātavyam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this Subala said with a smile, “O best of men! How long will our dear friend Viśākhā remain in such great difficulty?”**

*tato mayoktaṁ – yad artham iyaṁ rakṣyate sā yāvat niḥśeṣaṁ mūlya-dravyaṁ prasthāpayati \| kiṁ vā kiyad-dravyaṁ gṛhītvā svayam evāgatya dhruvam asyāḥ snehād ittham atra sthitvā avaśiṣṭa-dravyānayanārtham enāṁ prasthāpayati \| tāvad anayā sthātavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “She will be imprisoned until the complete payment is sent. Or if Rādhā comes with partial payment, she can remain in her place out of affection for her and she can send Viśākhā for the remainder of the payment. She should stay here till the payment comes.”**

*iti śrutvā madhumaṅgalaḥ prāha – sakhe ! etad-goṣṭhīnām adhīśā sā sarvābhyo’pi sarva-karmaṇi vicakṣaṇā viśeṣataḥ palāyane gavya-ghaṭṭyāṁ dānādiṣu sarvair asmābhiḥ punaḥ punaḥ pratakṣīkṛtāsti \| tvaṁ tv aniśam udghūrṇase tena me mahatī cintā jñāyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Madhumaṅgala said, “O friend! Since she is the leader of all the gopīs, she is more skillful in actions than all others, especially at escaping. We have all seen that repeatedly in taking taxes at the milk station. And without sleeping, you will become dizzy. That worries me greatly.”**

*tataḥ smitam apavāryāham avadaṁ – sakhe ! alam anayā cintayā \| tan-nikaṭe mamodghūrṇā na janiṣyata eva \| yadi vā jāyeta tarhi mad-uttamāṅgasya tad-vāma- bhujā-mṛṇālīm upadhānīkṛtya tad-uras-talpollasita-pīta-paṭṭāmbara-vara- vidhūpadhānopari mad-vāma-kara-pallavam aruṇam abhinyasya tathā mauktika-paṇa- nimittaka-vāg-vilāsam ullāsayiṣyāmi \| yathā sukhena jāgaryām eva catvāro rajani-yāmā drutam eva viramanti \| athavā mad-uro-nāma-ghanāndhakāra-viṣama-kārāgārāntas tāṁ praviśya tat-pārśva-yugalaṁ kaṭhora-bhuja-gārutmatārgalābhyāṁ dṛḍhaṁ saṁruddhya sukhena nirātaṅkaḥ svapna-vilāsaṁ vitariṣyāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**With a smile I spoke. “O friend! Do not worry. I will not become dizzy while I am near her. If that arises, then I will make her left lotus arm into a pillow for my head. I will place the pink bud of my hand on the yellow cloth, shining like the best moon, which covers her bed-like breast. I will delight in talks concerning the selling of the pearls. The four night watches will quickly pass by staying awake in this comfortable manner. Or, I will make her enter the prison of my chest, full of impervious darkness. I will shackle her sides firmly by the emerald door bolts of my hard arms. Happily, without worry, I will enjoy my dreams.”**

*iti śrutvā sarvāḥ smayamānā babhūvuḥ \| rādhā tūdgrīvikayā māṁ viśākhāṁ sarvāś ca sakhīr avalokayantī āha – candrāvalī-keli-kuraṅga ! tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti anuccair māṁ tarjayantī susmitāsīt \| viśākhā tu kuṭila-dṛṣṭyā mām avalokayantī āha – vraja-dhūrta- dhṛṣṭa ! apehi apehīti vadantī sakhī-madhye līnā babhūva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, everyone began smiling. Rādhā (still hiding), straining her neck, looked at me and Viśākhā and all the sakhīs and said softy, “O pet deer of Candrāvalī! Stop this!”**  
**In this way she scolded me and smiled. Viśākhā glanced at me with a frown and said, “O lecher of Vraja! Go away! Go away!” and then disappeared among the sakhīs.**

*tataḥ sarvābhiḥ subalaṁ praty uktaṁ – subala ! vidūṣakatāṁ tyaja \| yadi bhavatāṁ vikrayecchā vartate, tarhi muktāḥ pradarśya samucita-mūlyena pradīyantām \| no ced vayaṁ gṛhaṁ gacchāmaḥ \| mathurāta eva mauktiāny ānayitavyāni \|*

**Перевод:**

**All the gopīs then said to Subala, “O Subala! Give up your antics. If you desire to sell the pearls, show the pearls and give them at the right price. If not, we will return home. It is better that we get pearls from Mathurā.”**

*iti niśamya subalena sampuṭānudghāṭya tābhyāṁ mauktikāni pradarśya māṁ praty uktaṁ –- priya-vayasya ! imāni mauktikāny amūlyāni ābhir gṛha-paty-ādikaṁ samasta-godhanāni ca vikrīyāpi ekasyāpi mūlyaṁ dātum aśakyam \| etāḥ khalu bhavat- snigdhāḥ sarvān upekṣya tvāṁ jānanti \| tasmād etat-pūrva-kṛta-kārpaṇyam api vismṛtya māṁ ca tvad-anugatam avekṣya yat-kiñcin-mūlyaṁ gṛhītvā vinā-mūlyena dattavad āsām abhīpsita-mauktikāni dātum ājñā kriyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Subala opened the box and showed Viśākhā and Candramukhī the pearls. He said to me, “Dear friend! These pearls are priceless. It is impossible for the gopīs to give the price of even one pearl even if they sell all the cows and wealth in their houses. All of them, having affection for you, giving up all else, know you. Therefore, forgetting their previous miserly actions, and considering that I am your loyal follower, taking whatever price, giving as if without price, you should give the order to offer them the pearls they desire.”**

*tato’ham avocam – sakhe ! nahi nahi \| vayaṁ vāṇijya-vyavasāyinaḥ \| bhavatu \| kiṁ kartavyaṁ \| bhavad-vacanaṁ ca rakṣaṇīyam \| tad yat kiñcid alpam eva mayā mṛgyate tad dāpayitvaiva dīyantām \| kiṁ ca utkocaṁ gṛhītvā bāraṁ bāraṁ mama ghaṭṭī-dāna- dravyāṇi bhavatā vināśitāni santīti mama kenāpi kathitam asti tasmān mūlya-dravyaṁ samakṣam eva mayaivābhyo grahītavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then I said, “O friend! No, no! We are dedicated merchants. Let that be. What should I do? – Also, I must follow your words. Charge them whatever small I amount I seek and give them the pearls. Someone has also told me that you are receiving bribes and that you have dissipated the taxes from my toll gate. Therefore I should directly take the money.”**

*subalaḥ kiñcid vihasyāha – bhadraṁ vacaḥ \| kiṁ ca etābhiḥ svīya-svīyābhīpsita- mauktikāni vicitya pṛthak pṛthak kuṭī-kṛtāni dṛṣṭvā bhavatāpi svābhīpsita-mūlyaṁ kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala laughed slightly and said, “Your words are auspicious. Moreover, after they have picked out the pearls they want, seeing the pile, you should tell them the price you want.”**

*tato’ham abruvam – bhadraṁ darśayantu sva-svābhīpsita-mauktikāni mūlyaṁ kathyate mayā \|*

**Перевод:**

**I then said, “Good. Let them show me the pearls they want. I will state the price.”**

*subalaḥ prāha – priya-vayasya ! etāḥ vinayena yan nivedayanti tat kṛpayāvadhārya bhavate yadi rocate tadā vidhīyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “Dear friend! They have made a request. Please understand it and if it pleases you, then do it.**

*tato’haṁ* ***–*** *subala \| kathyatāṁ kiṁ nivedayanti yuktaṁ cet kartavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Subala! Tell me their request. If it is reasonable, I will do it.”**

*subalaḥ prāha – evaṁ nivedayanti, madhu-purī tāvad dūre samagra-mūlya-dravyāṇāṁ ca samācayanaṁ dina-dvaya-madhya eva sampadyate \| gurukulaṁ tu mauktikālaṅkārādy-adarśanāt kṣaṇe kṣaṇe khidyamānaṁ bāḍham ākrośati \| ato’tisnigdhān bhavad-vidhān avagatya lajjām api parihāya nirjana-vanāntaram āgatānām asmākam ṛṇenaiva mauktikāni dāpayitvā drutam eva vidāyaḥ kriyatām \| mūlya-dravyāṇy asmābhir bhavatām abhīpsita-vṛddhi-sahitāni dina-dvayābhyantare pariśodhya praheyāṇi yady asmāsu bhavad-vayasyasya pratītir na jāyate tadā tvam evāsmākaṁ pratibhūr bhaveti \| tasmāt parama-pratīti-pātrābhyaḥ santata- satyavādinībhya etābhyaḥ pratītiṁ kṛtvā datteṣu mauktikeṣu mūlya-dravyam abhīpsita-vṛddhiś cācirāl lapsyate \| tathaitābhiḥ saha niviḍa-sneho’pi vardhiṣyata eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala spoke. “This is what they request. ‘Mathurā is far away. To collect all the money required for purchase requires two days. Not seeing the pearls and ornaments, the elders are at every moment disturbed and rebuke us. Knowing your affection, giving up all shame, we have come to this lonely forest. Penalizing us with debt, quickly give us the pearls. In two days we will clear the loan for the articles we already have with interest. If your friend does not trust us, you must be our security.’ Trusting the gopīs who always tell the truth and are most trustworthy, you will very soon obtain the cost of the pearls given, with the interest that you desire. Your intense affection for the gopīs will also increase.”**

*tato’haṁ vihasya – subala ! tvaṁ śuddha-buddho’si \| āsāṁ vyavahṛtiḥ kiñcin mātrāpi tvayā na jñāyate \| kuṭi-nāṭī-nāṭikā-nartakya imā mauktikaṁ gṛhītvā nija-nija-bhartṛ- mahā-durga-koṣṭhair veṣṭitān sva-sva-guru-kula-mahā-parvatān praviśya dravyam adattvā yadi khelyantyas tiṣṭheyus tarhi tvayā kiṁ kartavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Laughing I said, “O Subala! You have pure intelligence. You do not know anything about their dealings. They are crafty actresses. They will take the pearls. If they stay at home playing, surrounded by the great fortresses of their husbands and entering the great mountains of their elders, what will I do--after giving them the goods?”**

*subalaḥ prāha – sakhe ! maivaṁ bravīḥ \| etāḥ khalu naivaṁ kariṣyanti \| yadi vā kuryus tadojjvala-vasāntārjuna-kokilādibhiḥ saha tatra gatvā svayam ābhiḥ svīkṛtam \| svayaṁ grahāśleṣa-cumbana-svādhara-sudhā-pānādi-rūpa-mūlyam etāsāṁ bhartṛṣu saṁśrāvya tat-prāpty-arthaṁ tebhyas tathā bhayaṁ pradarśayiṣyāmi \| yathā ta eva drutam amūs tvat-samīpaṁ prasthāpya tad dāpayiṣyanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “O friend! Do not say this. They will not act in this way. Or if they were to do so, then when I go to their houses with Ujjvala, Vasānta, Arjuna, Kokila and others, they will accept their promise. Making their husbands hear that the price they agreed on was to receive embraces, kisses, and drinking the nectar of their lips, I will make them fearful, in order to obtain the money. Then they will come quickly to you and pay.”**

*tan niśamya madhumaṅgalena sa-krodham uktaṁ – re subala ! tvaṁ nāmnaiva subalaḥ pumāṁś cāsi \| vastutas tv abalākṛtir muhur dṛṣṭo’si \| yataḥ sāmpratam apy āsām abalānāṁ phutkāraṁ kṣudrād atikṣudratareṣu tad-bhartṛṣu kartuṁ yad icchasi \| tad-bhīru-svabhāvasya tavaitat samucitam eva \| tasmāt tvam evātropaviśya mayaiva vijayādidhāṭīṁ pragṛhya balād āsāṁ bhartṛ-sahita-go-mahiṣyādikaṁ veṣṭayitvā samānīya ruddhvātra nandīśvara-pure rakṣiṣyate \| tadā tā eva svayam āgatya sva-sva- dravyaṁ sutarāṁ dattvā svaṁ svaṁ patiṁ godhanādikaṁ ca mocayiṣyanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Madhumaṅgala said angrily, “O Subala! In name only you are the male named Subala (very strong). Actually, it is constantly seen that you are very weak, like a woman. It is just like you with a timid nature to want to hiss feebly at the very weak husbands of the girls. Therefore you should sit here. Taking up the offensive, aggressively surrounding the gopīs’ buffalos, cows and husbands, I will gather them and imprison them at Nandīsvara. Naturally the gopīs will come and give their payment. Then I will release their husbands and cows.”**

*tatas tac-chravaṇena janita-mahā-duḥkha-bhareṇaiva mayoktaṁ – prāṇa-sakha madhumaṅgala ! katham evaṁ tvayā mantryate ? vraja-vāsino bhilla-pulindādyā api me priyebhyo’py adhika-priyāḥ \| ete tu sa-gotrāḥ sahodarā mad-abhinnā eva \| tasmād*  
*etan-mantraṇam atīvānucitam \| subala-bhāṣitam eva kiñcid bhāti \| tathāpi priya- janaiḥ samam ādāna-pradāna-prayogeṇa rasa-rakṣā na jāyata eva \| tathā ca smṛtiḥ --*  
*naivādānaṁ pradānaṁ hi mitraiḥ saha vitanyate \| kṛte prītyā bhavel lopaḥ kalahas tad-anantaram \|\| iti \|*  
*tataḥ prastutam eva mūlyaṁ dattvā mauktikāni nayantu \|*

**Перевод:**

**On hearing this I became very distressed and said, “O my dearest friend Madhumaṅgala! How can you advise this? The people of Vraja, even the Bhillas and Pulindas, are dearer to me that my friends. And these persons you describe are my family members and brothers. They are not different from me. Therefore such advice is inappropriate. Subala’s words are more attractive. But bargaining with dear ones destroys relationships. The smṛti says, ‘One should not give or take among friends. If that is done, affection will be destroyed and quarrel will arise.’ Therefore after they have paid the mentioned price, let them take the pearls.”**

*tac chrutvā sa-krodham iva subalaṁ nibhālya – aye ! kauṭilya-pāraṅgata subala ! sarvair eva militvā viḍambayitum eva vayam atrānītā bhavatā \| tad yūyaṁ mauktika- vāṇijya-vyavasāyena rājyaṁ kuruta \| vayaṁ calitāḥ sma ity uktvā calantīnāṁ tāsāṁ savidham āsādya sauhārdam abhivyañjayan subalo lalitāṁ nīcaiḥ prāha – sakhi lalite ! ādāna-pradāna-vyavahārasya sneha-bhaṅga-kāritvāt kevalena tad-bhayenaiva priya- vayasyena mūlya-nirṇayaṁ prastuta-vitta-lābhaṁ ca vinā sarvathaiva mauktikāni na deyānīit sarva-prakāreṇāvadhāritam \| tasmāt samāgatya prathamaṁ tāvan mūlyam eva nirṇīyatām \| tad-dānopāyaḥ paścāc cintanīyaḥ ity anunayena tāḥ parāvṛtya mat- samīpam ānīya māṁ praty uvāca – vayasya ! narma hitvā mūlyam eva tāvat kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, the gopīs angrily spoke to Subala. “Ah! Subala is duplicitous! Gathering us together he brought us here to cheat us. All of you should make a kingdom by selling pearlsWe are going”**  
**When they said this, Subala approached them and, showing friendliness, said to Lalitā in a low voice, “O my friend Lalitā! Since buying and selling destroys affectionate relationships, with that fear only my dear friend had decided that the pearls cannot be given at all without fixing the price and getting the agreed money. Therefore, first you should together decide the price. Later, how you pay can be considered.”**  
**Changing their mood by his conciliation, he then approached me and said, “Friend! Give up joking and speak about the price.”**

*tato’ham* ***--*** *sakhe subala ! prathamaṁ tāvat kasya mauktikānāṁ mūlyaṁ kathanīyam ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “My friend Subala! Whose pearl price should be discussed first?”**

*subalaḥ prāha – etāsāṁ madhye lalitaiva mukhyā tad etad gṛhīta-mauktika-mūlyaṁ prathamaṁ nirucyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “Among them, Lalitā is the chief person. The price of the pearls that she took should be discussed first.”**

*tato’haṁ kiñcid vihasya – etad-vāhinīnāṁ pravīṇayā lalitayā samare pauruṣeṇa yadi mādṛśaḥ puruṣa-siṁhaḥ sakṛd api kuṇṭhitāstrīkartuṁ śakyate \| tadāsyāḥ samakṣaṁ sarvathaivāstrī na bhaviṣyāmi \| kiṁ vā santatam amuktāstrī bhūtvā etaṁ pauruṣam evānukīrtayann imām evānucariṣyāmīti idam eva yat kiñcin mūlyaṁ dattvā gṛhṇātu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Laughing slightly, I said, “If the lion among men can even once be made weaponless by the manly prowess of Lalitā, most skillful of the female army in battle, I will not bear weapons against her in the future. Not bearing weapons at all, glorifying her prowess, I will obey her. With that price, let her take the pearls.”**

*subalaḥ smitvāha – gokula-vīra ! sudīpta-darpa-bharair mahendra-garva-parvata- kharvanāya sapta-rātram atra vāma-kara-kamala-kaniṣṭhāṅguli-varāṭaka-śikharopari bhramara iva govardhana-girir yena vyadhāyi tenoccaṇḍena bhavatā samam abaleyaṁ kaumalya-lalitā katham iva samitim ativistārayatu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Smiling, Subala said, “Warrior of Gokula! To destroy the mountain of pride of Indra with your own brilliant pride, you held up Govardhana with the little finger of your left lotus hand for seven nights, as if it were a bee on a lotus pod. How can the tender girl Lalitā fight with a mighty person like you?”**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ prāha – subala ! taṁ kathaṁ pūrvam asya paugaṇḍa-kṛtaṁ darpam atiślāghase ? yad adhunā tāruṇyāmṛta-sekena sa darpaka-kalpa-taru-lakṣa-lakṣa- guṇaṁ pallavito’sti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala spoke. “O Subala! Why are you excessively praising his proud acts of the paugaṇḍa period? Now, he is blossoming with millions of qualities on his desire tree of pride (or Cupid) because of a sweet shower of youth.”**

*subalaḥ prāha – katham iti viditam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “How is this known?”**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ – yad anena pūrvārīṇāṁ sakalānāṁ pūrvaṁ yādṛśa-vaikalyaṁ kāritam āsīt, sāmpratam etad durgamaṁ tad-dhṛdayaṅgamān atanu-parvatān abhilīlayaiva kena ca nakharāstreṇaiva khaṇḍa-khaṇḍīkṛtya mahāmārādibhis tato’py atula-tara-vaikalyam āpāditam āste \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “Just as he has weakened all previously mentioned enemies, now he has playfully caused the greatest confusion with greater destruction, completely crushing the great mountains rising near the gopīs hearts with the weapons of his nails--- which is most difficult to do.”**

*tato’haṁ vihasya – subala ! satyam anyatra mayā tādṛśenaiva bhūyate \| na tv etasyāḥ purataḥ yasmāt tato’pi pravara-vigrahe vividha-vaicitrī-pravīṇayānayā bāraṁ bāraṁ bhrū-dhanuṣ-ṭaṅkāreṇaiva huṅkāreṇa helayā stabdhīkṛto’smi \| tat katham iyam abalā bhavatu \|*

**Перевод:**

**I then said with a laugh, “O Subala! This is true about me in other cases, but not in front of Lalitā, for Lalitā, with various wonderful skills, with the twanging of her bow-like brows, by her casual, contemptuous grunts, is able to stun me. How can she be a mere girl?”**

*ity ākarṇanena smera-mukhīḥ sakhīr avalokya svānandottha-vikārān avagūhya sakrodham iva lalitayoktaṁ – aye subala vidūṣaka ! madhumaṅgala-sahacarasya gokula-bhaṇḍasya bhaṇḍatā-devyā satyaṁ tvam apy āviṣṭo’si \| yad asmān etat- samakṣaṁ samānāyya viḍambana-samudre saṁpātayan kautukaṁ paśyann asa iti nigadya kuṭila-dṛṣṭyā mām ākṣipantīm āgacchata bhoḥ saralāḥ ! āgacchatety uktvā sarvābhiḥ saha gacchantīṁ tad avasara eva tatrāgatā bhagavatyāḥ paurṇamāsyā antevāsinī nāndīmukhī tad-gamana-vārtām akhilāṁ sarvābhyaḥ samavadhārya vyājahāra – sakhi lalite ! narma-śālino’sya śrī-vrajendra-nandanasya parihāsa-vāṅ- mātreṇaiva sva-kāryam upekṣya katham apayānty asi ? kṣaṇaṁ mayā saha nirvṛtya –*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, with smiling face, Lalitā, hiding the symptoms arising from her bliss, looked at the sakhīs and, as if angry, said, “O clown Subala! You have truly been possessed by the goddess of buffoonery worshipped by the rascal of Gokula, the friend of Madhumaṅgala, for you have brought us in front of him, thrown us in the ocean of insults, and observed it all as fun.”**  
**She scolded me with a crooked glance and then said “Come, innocent girls! Come!” But as she was leaving with all the sakhīs, Nāndīmukhī, the pupil of Paurṇamāsī, suddenly arrived. Understanding why they were leaving by the explanations of the sakhīs, she said, “My friend Lalitā! Are you leaving, ignoring your task, just because of the joking words of Nanda’s son, whose nature is to joke? Spend a few moments with me.”**

*apamānaṁ puraskṛtya mānaṁ kṛtvā tu pṛṣṭhataḥ \|*  
*sva-kāryam uddharet prājñaḥ kārya-dhvaṁso hi mūrkhatā \|\|*  
*ity ādi-nyāyena parihāsa-viḍambanāni soḍhvā sthairyam ālambya sva-kāryam uddhara*  
*\| kiṁ ca mayā śapathaṁ kṛtvā vyāhriyate \| asya narmabhir eva lagnakair iva*  
*mauktikāni dāpayitavyāni \| anyathā bhavat-pūrva-kṛta-kārpaṇyam anusmarato'py*  
*abhimāna-śālino'sya narma-prayogo na sambhavati \| tad anyasyā mauktika-mūlyaṁ*  
*śrutvā yat kṛte bhadraṁ syāt tad eva vyavahartavyam iti balād iva tāṁ haste gṛhītvā*  
*sarvābhiḥ saha muktākuṭa-nikaṭam ānīya māṁ pratyuktavatī gokula-yuvarāja ! tatra-*  
*bhavatyā bhagavatyā śubhāśīḥ śata-pūrvakaṁ bhavantaṁ prati kiñcit sandiṣṭam asti \|*

**Перевод:**

**She said, “ ‘The wise man, putting insult in front and praise behind, should attain his objective. It is foolish to destroy one’s objective (because of insult).’ You should tolerate insults and mockery, and remain steady. Attain your objective. I swear that he will be obliged to give the pearls because of his joking, which is like surety. Otherwise, remembering your previous miserly actions, he would not be able to joke, since he is very proud. Hearing the price of the pearls of the other sakhī, you should do whatever is best in that situation.”**  
**She then took Lalitā forcibly by the hand and brought her along with all the others to the pile of pearls. She said to me, “O prince of Gokula! Along with a hundred blessings Paurṇamāsī sends a little message to you.”**

*tato’haṁ* ***--*** *nāndīmukhi ! kuśalam āste tatra-bhavatī bhagavatī ? tat kathyatāṁ kim ājñāpayati \| tat-sandeśāmṛtenātmānam apyāyāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Nāndīmukhī! Is Paurṇamāsī well? Please say what she has ordered. Let me enjoy the nectar of her message.”**

*nāndīmukhī -- imā vatsā rādhādayo vraja-kumārikā asmākam atīva sneha-pātrāṇi āyuṣmati bhavaty api santataṁ paramānuraktāḥ \| tad asmān vīkṣya atyāgrahaṁ vimucya āsāṁ dātuṁ śakyamāna-mūlyam ādāya aṣṭa-nidhi-pater vrajendrasya kumāreṇa bhavatā etad abhīṣṭa-mauktikāni dattvā vayaṁ santoṣaṇīyā iti bhagavatyāḥ sandeśam imam avakalayya narmāṇi parityajya muktāphala-dānenaitāḥ santoṣya gṛhāya prasthâpayituṁ mahānubhāvāḥ sarva-gokula-sukha-kāriṇo bhavanta eva pramāṇam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “These girls of Vraja, headed by Rādhā, who are the objects of my great affection, are always attracted to you. Considering me, give up your stubbornness. Accepting the price which they can give, you, son of Nanda, lord of eight treasures, should give them the pearls they desire and satisfy me. Considering this message of Paurṇamāsī, giving up joking, you will become a great person, giving joy to all of Gokula, if you satisfy the gopīs by giving the pearls and send them home. This is the correct action.”**

*tato’haṁ sa-ślāghaṁ – nāndīmukhi ! pūrvaṁ subala-hastena tat-prahitājñā-kusumaṁ śirasi nidhāya samagram eva parityajya lalitā-mauktikānāṁ yaḥ kaścana mūlyābhāso’nayā saha nirṇīto’sti tam asyā mukhato niśamya tato yat tvayā tyājyate tad api mayā tyājyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**With praise I said, “O Nāndīmukhī! Receiving on my head previously through the hand of Subala the flower of the order she sent, I give up everything. If, after hearing of the small amount decided for Lalitā’s pearls, you reject even that price, I will waver that also.”**

*ity ākarṇya sakampādharaṁ bhruvaṁ kuṭilayantīṁ lalitāṁ sarvāś ca smita-mukhīr avalokayantī nāndīmukhī smita-pūrvakam evāha – vraja-yuva-rāja ! sa imābhyaḥ śruto’sti, kintu tādṛṅ narma parihāya mat-samakṣam anyāsāṁ sarvāsām eva yathā- yuktaṁ mūlyaṁ kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, seeing Lalitā frown with quivering lips, and seeing the others smiling, Nāndīmukhī said with a smile, “O prince of Vraja! The gopīs have also heard this. Give up this joking and state the correct price for all others’ pearls in front of me.”**

*tato mayoktaṁ – nāndīmukhi ! āsāṁ sarvato jyāyasī jyeṣṭhā tad asyā mauktika- mūlyam anayā saha vicārya tvayaiva kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Nāndīmukhī! Among the gopīs Jyeṣṭhā is the eldest. Decide the price of her pearls and tell me.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *vitta-svāminaiva mūlyaṁ prathamaṁ kathanīyam \| tat svayam eva bhavatā tat kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “First the owner of the wealth should say the price. You should say.”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *niśāpater mama hṛdayākāśa-vīthyām uditāyāṁ rādhāyām udayantyām anurādhāyāṁ sva-maryādām unmucya tayor madhye rāgeṇodayantī jyeṣṭhā man- mukha-candraṁ tābhyāṁ saha vā pṛthag vā manāg api sva-mukhena pariṣvajatu iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “I am the moon. Jyeṣṭhā, who is rising with color between Viśākhā (or Rādhā), who has already arisen in the sky of my heart, and Anurādhā, who is now rising, should immediately embrace the moon of my mouth, with her mouth, separately or along with the other two.”**

*tatas tan niśamya nikuñjāntaritāyāṁ rādhāyāṁ lalitā-viśākhā-jyeṣṭhāsu ca tisṛṣu bhrū- bhaṅgena krodham abhinayantīṣu* ***mayoktaṁ*** *– nāndīmukhi ! sva-sva-mahā-lābha- karam api bhāṣitam avadhārya katham etāḥ krudhyanti ?*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Rādhā, who was in the bower, as well as Lalitā, Viśākhā and Jyeṣṭhā, feigned anger by frowning. I said, “Nāndīmukhī! Why should they become angry on hearing what is spoken for their own great benefit?”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *sundara ! gokula-śyāma-niśāpater anya-parigṛhītānāṁ satīnām asmākam etat-para-puruṣasya satīnām asmākam etat para-puruṣasya mukha-candra- cumbana-karaṇaṁ dūre tāvad āstāṁ sparśo’pi mahā-pāpāyaiveti krudhyanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Beautiful Kṛṣṇa! What to speak of kissing the moon-like mouth of another man, for us chaste women married to someone other than the dark moon of Gokula, even touching him would be a great sin. Thus they are angry.”**

*ityādi vinoda-līlām ākarṇya satyabhāmā kṛṣṇam āha – nātha ! tārā-gaṇane rādhāyā viśākheti-prasiddham api nāma hitvā sākūtam anurādheti nāma prayuñjatas tan- narmālāpa-bhaṅgīṁ svasmin svasminn evāvadhārayantyos tayor vidagdha-rādhā- viśākhayor nyāyya eva roṣaḥ \| lalitā kathaṁ kupyati ?*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing these playful discussions, Satyabhāmā said to Kṛṣṇa, “O Lord! In enumerating the constellations, giving up using the name Viśākhā to indicate Rādhā you have used the name Anurādhā to indicate Rādhā. It is proper that clever Rādhā and Viśākhā, who understood the use of constellation names to indicate themselves would become anger. Why did Lalitā also become angry?”**

*kṛṣṇaḥ – priye ! anurādheti lalitāyā evāpara-paryāyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa said, “O beloved! Anurādhā is actually another name for Lalitā.”**

*satyabhāmā – yādavendra ! etad apūrva-līlā-kathā-śravaṇena mama manasi tṛptir alaṁ vṛttir nāsti tat kathaya kathaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Satyabhāmā said, “O king of the Yadus! Hearing this astonishing pastime, my mind is not fully satisfied. Please speak more.”**

*kṛṣṇaḥ – tato’haṁ smita-śavalita-campakalatā-vadana-candram ālokayaṁs tad gṛhīta- muktā-kūṭaṁ hastena cālayann idam avadaṁ – nāndīmukhi ! iyaṁ te priya-sakhī campakalatā kasmād api siddhāt prāpta-siddhir iti mayā bahu-dinam anumitam āste \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa continued to speak. Gazing at the moon-like face of Campakalatā who was smiling, moving the pearls she had chosen in my hand, I said, “O Nāndīmukhī! For a long time I have guessed that your dear friend Campakalatā has attained mystic powers from some yogī.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *katham anumitam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “How did you conclude this?”**

***mayoktaṁ*** *– yata iyaṁ campakavallī sthāvarā madhye bṛhat-phala-dvaya-bhārānatāpi līlayā caṅkramīti \| ato mudira-sundare mad-urasi campaka-mālā bhūtvā sva-saurabha- bhareṇa māṁ vāsayatu \| mayāpi sva-siddhi-balāt etad-ājñayaivāsyāḥ kaṇṭhe sūkṣmatara-marakata-maṇi-mālayā vakṣojayor antare ca mahendra-nīlamaṇi-nāyakena tvaritam eva bhavitavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “It is because this immovable campaka vine, though heavy with two big fruits, easily moves about. She should become a campaka garland on the beautiful cloud of my chest and perfume me with her strong fragrance. And I, by my own mystic powers, on her order, should quickly become a garland of fine emeralds on her throat, and a huge sapphire center jewel between her breasts.”**

***subalaḥ –*** *priya-sakhe ! caṅkramaṇādinā’syāḥ siddhiḥ sarvair anubhūyata eva \| tava tu kenāpi kadāpi kutrāpi sā naiva dṛṣṭāsti \| tasmād duṣkare’smin karmaṇi sahasaiva pravṛttena bhavatā yadi niṣpādayitum aśakyaṁ syāt tarhi vayam ābhir upahasiṣyāmahe*  
*\| ato vicāryaiva pravṛttena bhavitavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “Dear friend! Everyone has experienced the powers of Campakalatā, by seeing her walk about. But no one has ever at any place seen your powers. If you cannot display this difficult task by your powers immediately, we will become the laughing stock of the gopīs. Considering the matter, you should demonstrate your powers.”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *subala ! mama siddhiṁ paśyann api na paśyasi ? mayā kiṁ kartavyam ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Subala! Having seen my powers, do you still not see them? What should I do?”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *madhurāṅga \| kadā kutra kā siddhis tvayā niṣpāditāsti \| sā kathyatāṁ, sarve śuśrūṣavaḥ santi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Sweet Kṛṣṇa! Where and when did you get this mystic power? Tell us, for we are all eager to hear.”**

*tato* ***mayoktaṁ*** *– deva-yātrāyām ambikā-vane śaraṇāgatatvena tāta-pādayoḥ patitaṁ mahājagaraṁ pādāṅguṣṭha-sparśa-mātreṇaiva sarvālaṅkṛti-bhūṣito vidyādhara-rājo mayā vyadhāyi \| girīndra-rājo govardhanaś ca saptāham eka-kareṇaiva chatrākam ivānāyāsenaivādhāri \| kāliya-viṣa-jvālā-vimohitaḥ subalādi-vayasya-gaṇaḥ sva-dṛṣṭi- mātreṇaiva sa-cetanaḥ sphuṭam akāri \| bāraṁ bāraṁ mahādāvānalo’py amṛtīkṛtya sukhenaiva sphuṭam apāyi \| evam ādayo bahavaḥ siddhi-prabhāvā gokule kena nānubhūtāḥ santi ? tad ativistareṇālam \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “When a huge python had swallowed my father’s feet at Ambikā forest when we went on pilgrimage, I turned the snake into the king of the Vidyādharas, decorated with all ornaments, just by touching the snake with my toe. I easily held up Govardhana with one hand for seven days as if it was an umbrella. Subala and other friends, when overcome with Kāliya’s painful poison, were brought back to life just by my glance. I turned the great forest fire to nectar and comfortably swallowed it. Who has not seen the strength of my many mystic powers in Gokula? Why speak more of them?”**

*iti niśamya īṣat-smayamānā lalitā prāha – nāndīmukhi ! eṣa te durlalito nāgaraḥ sarvam etat satyaṁ kathayati \| kintu sa kālo gataḥ \| yatra brahmacarya-balena tat sarvaṁ niṣpāditam āsīt \| sāmprataṁ nṛśaṁsa-kaṁsa-sevaka-govardhana-malla- gṛhiṇyāḥ padmā-śaivyā-prabhṛtīnāṁ ca vrajāṅganānāṁ santata-sambhoga-vilāsena dūṣita-brahmacāritvād asyāntarhitāḥ sarvās tāḥ siddhayaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Lalitā, smiling slightly, spoke. “O Nāndīmukhī! Your rude rascal speaks the truth about all this. But that time has gone. This was all accomplished by the strength of his celibacy. Now all his powers have disappeared because his celibacy has been spoiled by constant enjoyment with Padmā, Śaibya, and the wife of Govardhana Malla, who serves cruel Kaṁsa.”**

*tato’haṁ kiñcid vihasya – lalite ! tejīyasāṁ na doṣāya vahneḥ sarva-bhujo yathā ity asya padyasyārtham abuddhyaiva tvayedam ucyate \| tat-tad-arthaḥ śrūyatāṁ \| yathā sarva-bhakṣaṇenāpi vahnes tejo-glānir na bhavet \| pratyuta tad-vṛddhir mahaty eva bhavati \| tathā bhavādṛśīnām uttama-varāṅganānām avirata-madhura-rasopabhoga- mādhuryeṇa mamāpi siddhir nirantaram ujjvalā bhavantī parama-vṛddhim evādhikatarām avāpa \|*

**Перевод:**

**I laughed a little and spoke. “O Lalitā! There is no fault in persons with power, just as there is no fault in fire which devours everything. You speak the meaning of this verse without intelligence. Please hear the meaning. Just as fire does not lessen its power though it devours everything, but rather increases its strength, my powers have constantly increased by the sweetness of enjoying conjugal rasa incessantly with you, the best of women.”**

*madhumaṅgalah -- lalite ! satyaṁ bravīti priya-vayasyaḥ \| siddhiś cen na vṛddhim āyāti tarhi muktaḥ kathaṁ bhūmau prārohanti ? prārūḍhāś ca kathaṁ sarvataḥ prasāriṇyā vallikā bhavantyaḥ pracurataraṁ phullanti phalanti ca ?*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “O Lalitā! My friend speaks the truth. If his powers have not increased, how could pearls grow from the earth? And after sprouting, how could they become creepers, spread everywhere and then blossom and bear fruit?”**

*lalitā vihasya – ārya madhumaṅgala ! tat kiṁ bhavat-priya-vayasyasya siddheḥ prabhāvaḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā laughed and said, “O Madhumaṅgala! Is this because of the mystic power of your dear friend?”**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ – tat kasya?*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “Whose power is it?”**

*lalitā -- vṛndāvana-bhūmer eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “It is the power of Vṛndāvana’s earth.”**

*rādhā svagataṁ – lalite ! etat kāminī-saṅgasya cety ucyatām iti rādhayoktaṁ viśākhāpy anubhāṣitavatī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā said softly, “O Lalitā! You should say the power comes from associating with young girls.” Viśākhā announced Rādhā’s words to everyone.**

***tato’haṁ –*** *tat katham atraiva bhavatībhir upta-muktā hiṁsrā jātāḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Then why did the pearls that you planted produce weeds?”**

***lalitā –*** *bho vidagdha-śiromaṇe ! muktāḥ kiṁ hiṁsrā bhavanti ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O crest jewel of the clever? Do pearls become weeds?”**

***mayoktaṁ*** *– tat kathaṁ muktā notpannāḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said “But why did pearls not appear?”**

***lalitā –*** *bhūmi-viśeṣasya bījasya ca vaiguṇyāt \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “There is some fault in the earth and the seeds.”**

*rādhā svagataṁ – lalite ! kṣetrasyāsya guṇād apy ucyatām ity etad viśākhāpy uktavatī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā said as an aside, “Lalitā, you should say it was the quality of that particular earth.” Viśākhā repeated this.**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *lalite ! satyam eva kathayati viśākhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “O Lalitā! Viśākhā speaks the truth.”**

***lalitā –*** *katham iva ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “How is that?”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *yasmāt sarvadā sarvāṅgiṇā mukti-viṣa-vallari-mahoṣara-bhūmau santata-paramānanda-kara-prema-bharojjvalita-bhakti-pīyūṣa-sura-valli-sarasatara- mahā-kṣetre’smin vṛndāvane dhruvam āgantukā ye kecana jantavo bhaktā eva bhavanti kathaṁ nu muktā bhavantu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “This great holy land of Vṛndāvana is always, in all places, barren soil for the poison creeper of liberation, but is a great fertile field for the divine nectar creeper of bhakti shining with intense prema, which produces the highest bliss. Certainly, beings who accidently come here become devotees. They become liberated (muktā).**

*lalitā vihasya – viśākhe \| sphuṭaṁ kāminī-saṅgasya iti bhavad-vāg-vilāso’pi sa- sandarbho bhaviṣyati tad vivṛtya kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā laughed and said, “O Viśākhā! You said that he gains his power from associating with girls. This needs some explanation. Please explain this in detail.”**

***viśākhā –*** *ārūḍha-yogo’pi nipātyate’dhaḥ-saṅgena yogī kim utālpa-siddhir iti nyāyenāsya kāye manasi gotre ca śyāmalatarasya sa-cchidra-vaṁśī-rasikasya kṣaṇa-mātra-saṅgena muktā api bhūmau garbha-vāsena jananam āsādya ca cchidrībhūya saṁsāra-guṇa-baddhā babhūvuḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Even a perfected yogī falls by bad association, what to speak of a person with lesser achievements. By even a moment’s association with the dark person who plays the flute with holes, through his body, mind or family connection, a liberated person ( or the pearl) must take birth on earth and, becoming full of faults (being pierced), becomes bound by the guṇas (or strung on a thread) of saṁsāra.”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *viśākhe ! nikhilam eva satyaṁ kathayasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Viśākhā! Everything you say is true.”**

***subalaḥ –*** *vayasya ! katham iva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “O friend, how is that?”**

*tato* ***mayoktaṁ*** *– mama kāma-vilāsādhyâpakasya saundarya-vaidaghdyādi-guṇān viśeṣeṇa nāradādi-mukhād ākarṇya daṇḍakāraṇya-vāsino jīvan-muktāḥ kaṭhora- tapasyākulam ācarya gokula-vane’smin pravara-gopa-gṛheṣu janim aṅgīkṛtya vraja- vilāsinyo bhūtvā susāra-vaidagdhyādi-guṇair āvṛtya etā iva mad-urasi sraja iva vilasanti \| tathaivāpare parama-muktā api mad-acintya-guṇair ākṛṣṭā asmin vraja-vane nitya-siddha-sthāvara-jaṅgameṣu paśu-pakṣi-bhūruhādayo bhūtvā mām ānandayantaḥ paramānandam āsvādayanto nandayanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Hearing from Nārada about my qualities such as beauty and cleverness, the sages of Daṇḍakāraṇya who were liberated, performing great penance, were born in the best cowherd houses in the forest of Gokula as gopīs and, covered by qualities like cleverness and competence, rest on my chest like garlands, just like these gopīs. Others, attaining the highest liberation, attracted by my inconceivable qualities, became birds, animals and plants in Vraja forest with the nitya-siddha plants and animals. Giving me joy and tasting the highest bliss, they rejoice.”**

***subalaḥ –*** *vayasya ! sādhu varṇitam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “O friend, you have described this nicely.”**

*lalitā kiñcid vihasya – mahā-siddhaś ced bhūmi-bheda-guṇaṁ vinā tava siddheḥ prabhāvān muktā jāyante tarhi parama-siddho’pi bhavān kiñcin-mātrādhika-dravya- lābhāya tad-vikraya-kṣudra-vṛttau kathaṁ pravṛtto’sti ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā laughed a bit and said, “If you have such great mystic powers and by that power pearls appear without considering the type of soil, then why are you, with such great powers, engaged in a the inferior occupation of selling pearls, in order to gain a few more commodities?”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *mūrkhe lalitike ! yauvana-dhana-garvitābhir bhavatībhir yathā sva- dharmaṁ parityajya itas tataś cañcalyate tathā svadharma-pariniṣṭha-vaiśya śiromaṇi- śrī-vraja-rājasya eka-putreṇa mayāpi svadharmaṁ parityajya kim ucchṛṅkhalena bhavitavyam ? yataḥ kṛṣi-vāṇijya-go-rakṣā-kuśīdaṁ tūryam ucyate iti vaiśyānām asmākaṁ catasro vṛttayo bhavanti \| āsām eka-vṛttyācaraṇenaiva sarva-siddher vṛddhis tac-catuṣṭayam ācarato mama punaḥ siddhiḥ parama-kāṣṭhām ārūḍhaiva paraṁ virājate \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Foolish Lalitā! Proud of your wealth and youth, you girls give up your dharma and wander about. Should I, the son of Nanda, who is the crest jewel of vaiśyas fixed in his dharma, also impetuously give up my dharma? We vaiśyas have four occupations: agriculture, trading, protecting cows and loaning money. All mystic powers increase by following one of these occupations. I perform all four, and thus my mystic powers have risen to the topmost level.”**

*nāndīmukhī sa-smitaṁ – svadharma-niṣṭha-yuvarāja ! bhavataḥ kṛṣi-vāṇijya-gorakṣā- tisro vṛttayaḥ spaṣṭam anubhūyanta eva vṛddhi-jīvikā tu kadāpi nāvakalitāsti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said with a smile, “O prince fixed in your dharma! We see that you engage in three occupations: agriculture, trading and protecting cows. But we have not seen living by making loans.”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *nāndīmukhi ! sāpy asmābhiḥ kriyamāṇāpi tvayā kiṁ na jñāyate ? sāmpratam api muktātyantābhāva-saṅkṣobhinībhir etābhiḥ saha saṁkrāmita-svadharmaṁ muktāpāra-vyāpāra-vṛddhi-vṛttiṁ vidhātum ārabdhavān asmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Nāndīmukhī! Do you not know that I do this? Even now, I have begun to work in the limitless loaning business of pearls, an approved occupation by which I meet with these women who are disturbed by the great lack of pearls.”**

*viśākhā kiñcid vihasya – subala ! yatra yo rajyati, sa khalu vinindyam api tad eva ślāghyatayotkīrtayati \| atas tāvat suṣṭhu adharmasyāpi śobhanatvena varṇanam asya tva-priya-sakhasya nāyogyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā, laughing a little, said, “O Subala! A person will praise to the heights something to which he is attached, even if it should be reviled. Thus it is not unsuitable for your dear friend to describe adharma in glowing terms.”**

*subalo vihasya – nāndīmukhi ! na kevalam asau dhanasya vṛddhiṁ labhate, kintv anyeṣāṁ padārthānām api kṣaṇe kṣaṇe vṛddhiṁ labhamāno’sti \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said with a laugh, “O Nāndīmukhī! Not only does he gain interest on loans, but also gains at every moment in relation to other objects.”**

*nāndīmukhī -- kasya kasya ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said “What are these items?”**

***subalaḥ –*** *prathamaṁ pratyaṅge manasija-koṭi-vijayi-nava-tāruṇyasya netrāñcale cañcala-kamala-vinindi-ghūrṇanasya bhāṣite ca sudhā-sārojjvala-mādhurīṇām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “He increases his new youth which conquers that of millions of Cupids in all limbs of his body. He increases the rolling movements at the edge of his eyes, which surpass the swaying of a lotus. He increases the brilliant sweetness, the essence of nectar, in his words.”**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ – subala ! itara-padārthānāṁ vṛddhiṁ kiṁ tvayā vismṛtā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “O Subala! Have you forgotten his increase in other items?**

***subalaḥ –*** *smārya keṣām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “Please help me recollect those items.”**

*madhumaṅgalaḥ – makara-kuṇḍala-maṇi-mañjīra-hāra-valaya-keyūra-maṇi- mudrikādibhiḥ parama-saundarya-darpādika-madhura-keli-vilāsānām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “There has been an increase in the manifestation of the sweet, playful movements of his supreme beauty and pride, by means of his makara earrings, jeweled anklets, necklaces, bracelets, crowns and rings.”**

***lalitā –*** *ārya ! anyataraika-vastunaḥ kathaṁ vṛddhiṁ saṅgopitavān asi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Respected Madhumaṅgala! Why do you hide the increase in one other item?”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *katarasya ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “What is that?”**

***lalitā –*** *ballava-kula-sādhvīnām adharāmṛtocchiṣṭasya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “There has been increase in the remnants of the nectar from the lips of the chaste cowherd girls.”**

*rādhā sa-smitaṁ – lalite ! satyaṁ satyaṁ vapur ākhyāti bhojanam ity etat praguṇatara- rasāyana-pānenaiva bāhu-yugale suvṛttendra-nīlārgalā-darpa-dalana-balanasya, vakṣasi mārakata-kavāṭāhaṅkāra-vidhvaṁsi-vistārasya, ūru-dvaye ca marakata-kadalī- stambha-garva-sarvaṅkaṣa-suvṛttitāyāḥ, vadane ca śārada-śaśadhara-parārdha- mādhurya-saṅkoca-kāri-nirbhara-suṣamāyāḥ, caraṇayoḥ nava-rasāla-pallava-praśasti- vitrāsi-mādhuryasya, sarvāṅge madhuratara-sanniveśa-kuśala-lajjā-kāri-subhaga- sauṣṭhavasya, vapuṣi ca nava-nava-mudirendīvaraindranīla-prabhā-hāri-visāri- prakaṭojjvalatā-bharasya, yenāsya sakalāntaram apy abhivyāpya vilasitam iti tan mṛdu-bhāṣitāmṛtaṁ viśākhayāpi sa-smita-sphuṭa-sulapita-kusumena suvāsitaṁ vyadhāyi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā said with a smile, “O Lalitā! It is very true. What you eat determines your body. Just by drinking this excellent tonic, there is increase in the strength of his arms, which defeat the pride of round sapphire door bolts. There is increase in the breadth of his chest, which defeats the pride of emerald door panels. There is increase in the roundness of his thighs, which oppress the pride of emerald banana stalks. There is increase in the great beauty of his face which restricts the sweetness of millions of autumn moons. There is increase in the sweetness of his feet, which frighten the best newly sprouted mango buds. There is increase in the charming arrangement of all his limbs, which puts to shame the skill of the most attractive playground. There is increase in the brilliance of his body, spreading everywhere and stealing the effulgence of fresh clouds, blue lotuses and sapphires. This tonic spreads within his body and shines forth.”**  
**The sweetness of Rādhā’s words became most fragrant by the flower of Viśākhā speaking the same words with a bright smile.**
