*madhumaṅgalaḥ sa-parāmarśaṁ – vayasya ! para-ramaṇī-madhurādhara-lolupaṁ tvām etā dhūrtā miṣṭa-miṣṭa-vacana-mādhurī-bhareṇa parilobhayantyaḥ pracuratara- vṛddhiṁ pratiśrāvya mauktikāny ādāya jaṭilādi-durgam āsthāya mūlam api na dāsyanti*  
*\| vṛddhi-vārtā tu dūre vartatām iti suṣṭhu vijñāyāhaṁ mitreṇa hitam āśāṁsanīyam iti vijñāpayann asmi \| agre tubhyaṁ yad rocate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then Madhumaṅgala advised Kṛṣṇa. “O friend! These tricky women are making you greedy by their sweet words, since you are greedy for the sweet lips of other’s wives. Telling you about greater increase, they will take the pearls and place themselves in the fortress of Jaṭilā and other elders, and not pay even the basic price, what to speak of the interest. Understanding this, I wish to benefit you as a friend. I have thus informed you. You may do as you please.”**

*tato’haṁ daśanai rasanāṁ sandaśya – sakhe ! imā gāndharvādayaḥ kulāṅganāḥ prakāmādāna-pradāne mahā-śucayas tad ajānataiva tvayedam ucyate tac chrūyatāṁ sāmpratam eva svādharāmṛtopaḍhaukanena mām atīva santoṣya gāndharvayā mat- sakāśāt prātar gṛhīta-kamala-rāga-rekhikā-yugalena nija-vakṣoruhaṁ paribhūṣya mad- urasi tad eva caturguṇīkṛtya sāyam eva vitīrṇam \| lalitayā ca paraśvaḥ sāyam amṛta- srāvi-cumbaka-ratnam ekam ādāya kali-krameṇa tri-guṇī-kṛtya pariśodhitam \| viśākhayāpi niśīthe paramāgraheṇa man-madhurādharāmṛtam ādāya sva-sarva-bhūtaṁ tad eva prātar bahu-guṇīkṛtya pradāya bāḍham āpyāyito’smi \| campaka-latādayaś ca bāraṁ bāraṁ sva-svābhīṣṭa-padārtham āgraheṇābhigṛhya kāścid dviguṇīkṛtya kāścit triguṇīkṛtya pradāya mām atīva santoṣitavatyaḥ \| kintu āsu jana-dvayī kevalaṁ pradāne kiñcid anyādṛśa-vyavasāyā vartate \|*

**Перевод:**

**I bit my tongue with my teeth and said, “O friend! These women headed by Rādhā are very pure in giving and receiving desired objects. You have spoken in this way because you do not know this. Please listen. Just now, after Rādhā, satisfied me with a gift of her lip nectar, I decorated her breasts in the early morning with two ruby lines, and in the evening she responded by decorating my chest with lines four times as many. The day before yesterday in the evening, Lalitā took one jewel kiss, oozing with nectar, and repaid it three times the amount after some argument. In the night Viśākhā took the sweet nectar of my lips with great eagerness and repaid it in the morning many times the amount by giving her whole being. I am fully satisfied. Taking their desired objects with great enthusiasm, Campakalatā and others have given back two or three times the amount and satisfied me immensely. Only two persons remain to carry out transactions like the others in repayment.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *katarā sā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Who is one of them?”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *ekā raṅgaṇavallī mad-vakṣasi nija-vakṣaḥ-sthita-nistala-madhura-phala- yugalasya trir āropaṇam urīkṛtya mat-kara-mardana-phala-dvayaṁ gṛhītvā sakṛd eva tad-arpitaṁ avaiśiṣṭaṁ dvir-arpaṇaṁ gati-kriyām ācarya nādyāpi karoti \| kācid anyā raṅgaṇamālā-sahacarī tulasī nāmnī dviguṇīkṛtya dātum uktvā mad-eka-parirambha- stavakaṁ samādāya sāmpratam idānīṁ deyaṁ tadānīṁ deyam iti kālaṁ kṣapayantī mūlam api na dadāti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “One is Raṅgaṇavallī. Three times she places the sweet, round fruits situated on her chest upon my chest but she accepted the two fruits of my hands for massaging her fruits only once. Till this day, after bargaining, she still has not given the remaining payment of twice accepting my hands. The other is her assistant Tulasī. After promising to give two times the amount, she gave me one embrace. Time passes, with promises of giving today, just now. But the basic price has not even been paid.”**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *– ayi raṅgaṇavallī-tulasyau ! yuvayoḥ sakāśād alabhyamānātma-divya- dravyo’pi priyaṁvado’smat-priya-vayasyaḥ sahaja-sāralyato bhavad-vidhāsu priyam eva vakti \| tathāpy etādṛśe’py asmin vañcana-cāturīm ācarantībhyāṁ bhavatībhyāṁ kṛtaghnatvāt loka-dharmato bhayaṁ na kriyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “O Raṅganavallī and Tulasī! Though my friend has not received from you two his precious commodities, he is soft spoken and speaks nicely about you because of his compliant nature. But you two who are clever at deceiving a person like him have no fear in breaking the norms of dharma out of ingratitude.”**

*tad ākarṇya cāru hasantī* ***lalitā*** *prāha – sakhyus tavārya madhumaṅgala ! bhāṣitaṁ tat pīyūṣataḥ priyataraṁ nahi kasya goṣṭhe pratyakṣaraṁ pratipadaṁ tad-alīkatogra- śakrāśanasya nahi ced iha bhūri-gandhaḥ \| tataḥ sahāsa-kolāhalaṁ sādhu lalite ! sādhu varṇitam iti sarvās tās tām āliṅgatavatyaḥ \| rādhā ca tathaiva manasā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Lalitā laughed in a pleasing manner and said, “Respected Madhumaṅgala! The words of your friend are sweeter than nectar and cannot be found in any assembly, except that every syllable and every word is infected with the sweet stench of malicious lies.”**  
**All the sakhīs exclaimed, “O Lalitā! That was an apt description.” They all embraced her. Rādhā (who was still hiding) embraced her in her mind.**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *mohana ! katham apratītiḥ kriyate ? lalitā-prema-pātrīyaṁ raṅgaṇa- mālikā, dvitīyā ca viśākhā-priya-śiṣyā tad etābhyām eva te prabodhya tad avaśyaṁ dāpayiṣyate \| yathā tābhyāṁ saha bhavataḥ śuddha-bhāvena punaḥ punar eṣa vyavahāro nirvahati \| yadi vā dāpayituṁ na śakyate tadā tad-avaśiṣṭa-dravyaṁ tat- snehāt tābhyāṁ svayam eva dāpyate \| ete api yadi te jhaṭiti dātuṁ nāṅgīkurutas tarhi kenāpi bhavān anuyoktuṁ na śakyate \| bhavatā tv anaṅga-mañjarī-sahodarāgrataḥ kriyamāṇa eva phutkārārambhe tābhyām eva sādhvasena svīkṛta-vṛddhi-sahitaṁ tad drutaṁ sutarāṁ vitariṣyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said “O enchanting Kṛṣṇa! Do you not trust them? Raṅganavallī is dear to Lalitā. Tulasī is the dear student of Viśākhā. They will enlighten the two and make them pay so that you can continue transacting with them in a pure manner. If they cannot pay, then Viśākhā and Lalitā should pay the remaining dues, since they have affection for the two. If they also refuse, then you should not associate with them at all. If you do this in front of Rādhā, Anaṅga-mañjarī’s sister, with great commotion, the two will quickly absolve the debt with interest, out of fear.”**

*tataḥ sarvāsu nāndīmukhīṁ māṁ ca sa-bhrū-kuṭi-kauṭilyam īkṣamāṇāsu kiñcid upasṛtya* ***tuṅgavidyā*** *sāntaḥ-smitam āha – bho bhoḥ sakhyaḥ ! apūrvaikā vārtā śrūyatām*

**Перевод:**

**When all the sakhīs glared at Nāndīmukhī and me with frowns, Tuṅgavidyā came a little forward and with an internal smile said, “O friends! Hear some astonishing news.”**

***sarvāḥ –*** *sakhi ! kā sā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone said, “O friend, what is it?”**

***tuṅgavidyā –*** *ekaḥ kānta-darpa-nāmācāryaḥ śruto’sti \|;*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “There is a famous ācārya named Kānta-darpa.”**

***lalitā –*** *paramparayā śruto’sti bhadreṇa na jñāyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “I have heard of him from others, but do not have the good fortune of knowing him.”**

***tuṅgavidyā –*** *tat-priya-śiṣyeṇa śyāmala-miśra-nāmnā tat-kṛta-sūtrāṇāṁ sandhi- catuṣṭayākhyāta-kṛd-vṛttaya iti vyākhyātāś catasro vṛttayo yāḥ kṛtāḥ santi tāḥ kiṁ bhavatībhir dṛṣṭa-caryo bhavanti ?*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “His dear pupil named Śyāmala Miśra has written a commentary called Sandhi-catuṣtayākhyāta-kṛt on his teacher’s sutras. Have you seen the explanation of the four sections of the commentary?”**

***viśākhā --*** *viṣṇu viṣṇu tad-vṛtti-darśanaṁ tāvad-dūre’stu sa eva kadāpi na karṇa- gocarīkṛto’sti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “O Lord! I have never heard of this commentary, what to speak of seeing it.”**

***lalitā*** *sākūtaṁ – tuṅgavidye ! kutrāsau tvayā paricitaḥ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said with meaning, “O Tuṅgavidyā, where did you learn about this person?”**

***tuṅgavidyā*** *– sakhīsthalyā eka-mahā-padmāpsarasā tad-vṛtti-pāṭhārthaṁ taṁ mṛgayituṁ sāya atrāgatam āsīt \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “The Apsarā named Mahāpadmā of Sakhīsthalī came here in the evening looking for him in order to study his commentary.”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *tuṅgavidye ! etāvad-dūra-bhūmau kathaṁ tat-sañcāraḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Tuṅgavidyā, why did she wander so far away to this place?”**

***tuṅgavidyā*** *– nikāma-vanyāvṛddhi-sāmarthyena \| ity ākarṇya sarvāḥ smitaṁ kurvanti sma \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “By the great power of floods of desire.” Everyone smiled on hearing this.**

***lalitā –*** *tatas tataḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Then what happened?”**

***tuṅgavidyā*** *– śyāmala-miśrādvitīyenālīka-rāja-paṇḍitena prathamaṁ narma- pañjikāṁ kraya-vikraya-pañjikāṁ ca vidhāya sāmpratam alīka-pañjikā tathādāna- pradāna-pañjikā ca prapañcitā anukrameṇa nāmnāntareṇa ca prapañcitaitat-pañjikā- catuṣṭayaṁ bhavatībhiḥ śrutam astīti manye \|*

**Перевод:**

**Alīka Paṇḍita, the kingly scholar of deception, who is a friend of Śyāmala Miśra (person mixed with darkness), first wrote the Book of Jokes and the Book of Buying and Selling. Recently he has written the Book of Deceptiont and the Book of Loan and Repayment. They are also known by different names. I think you must have heard of these four books.”**

***lalitā –*** *atha kiṁ so’pi suṣṭhu anubhūyamāno’sti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Yes, they have been experienced also.”**

***tuṅgavidyā*** *– tebhyo’pi parama-samīcīna-tīvra-dhī-prāgalbhyena tat-satīrthena kuhaka-bhaṭṭena tad-vrtti-catuṣṭayasya ṭīkā yugapat-kartum ārabdhāḥ santi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “At the same time, his classmate Kuhaka Bhaṭta, Best of the Cheaters, who is audacious and sharply intelligent, began to write is commentary on these four topics.”**

***campakalatā*** *– tuṅgavidye ! tvaṁ sarva-vidyā-viśāradāsi tad eṣāṁ catūrṇāṁ śāstra- kāriṇāṁ nāma-dheya-niruktim avagantuṁ sarvās tvatto’bhilaṣanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Campakalatā said, “Tuṅgavidyā! You are expert in all types of knowledge. Therefore we desire to understand the meaning of the surnames of these four writers of scripture.”**

***viśākhā –*** *ācārya-bhaṭṭayor arthaḥ spaṣṭa eva tan-miśra-paṇḍitayoḥ sa tāvan nirucyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**The meanings of** ***ācārya*** **and** ***bhaṭta*** **are clear. You should explain the meanings of** ***miśra*** **and** ***paṇḍita.***

***tuṅgavidyā*** *vihasya – doṣo’py asti guṇo’py asti tena miśrena miśreta iti miśraḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā laughed and said, “*****Miśra*** **(mixed) means the person who has faults as well as good qualities.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *kataro vā doṣaḥ kataro vā gunaḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “What are the faults and what are the good qualities?”**

***tuṅgavidyā*** *– vaidagdhyāvaidagdhayor avicāreṇaiva yatra kutrāpi sarvatra pravṛttir iti doṣaḥ \| sāralyādhikyena uttamānuttamāvicāreṇaiva vaiṣamyaṁ vinā sarvatra samatayā pravṛttir iti mahān guṇaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “The tendency to respond to others without discriminating whether they are skillful or unskillful is a fault. The tendency to treat all equally without difference, not considering higher or lower, because of a soft nature, is a good quality.”**

***lalitā*** *smitvā – tad asyaitan-miśratā padavī samucitaiva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā smiled and said, “This title** ***miśra*** **is appropriate to him.”**

***citrā*** *– paṇḍito’pi nirucyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Citrā said, “Please explain the title** ***paṇḍita*****.”**

***tuṅgavidyā*** *– sad-asad-vicārikā buddhiḥ paṇḍā \| tayā yuktaḥ paṇḍitaḥ iti \| ayaṁ tu buddhi-gauraveṇa pūrva-parayoḥ para-vidhir balavān iti vicārya parā asad-vicārikā yā sā paṇḍā tām evottaramatve āśritas tad-yuktatvāt paṇḍita iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “*****Paṇḍa*** **means intelligence to distinguish right from wrong. A person who possesses such intelligence is called** ***paṇḍita*****. This person however has by the strength of his intelligence taken the principle that the later rule is stronger than the previous rule and decided that real intelligence is doing wrong. Taking shelter of that intelligence as his supreme principle and using it, he is called** ***paṇḍita*****.”**

***citrā*** *– sakhi tuṅgavidye ! sandhyādi-catuṣṭayasyāpy arthaḥ prakāśya kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Citrā said, “My friend Tuṅgavidyā! Please explain the meaning of the four terms starting with** ***sandhi*****.”**

***tuṅgavidyā*** *– citre ! asmad-goṣṭhyāṁ tat-prapañcena lalitaiva dakṣā tat saiva kathayatu*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “O Citrā! Lalitā is expert at explaining this in our assembly. Therefore she should explain it.”**

***lalitā –*** *citre ! etat-prasaṅga-leśo’pi mayā kadāpi na śruto’pi tad etat-prakaraṇa- vyākhyātrī tuṅgavidyaiva tad-arthaṁ jānāti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O Citrā! I have not heard about this subject at all. Tuṅgavidyā can explain the topic. She knows the meaning.”**

***citrā*** *– tuṅgavidye ! mac-chirasā śāpitāsi tvayaiva vyākhyāyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Citrā said, “O Tuṅgavidyā! Taking an oath on my head, please explain this.”**

*tato’tilajjayā svayam asaṁkathya rādhikātiprīti-pātrīṁ manāg apy anucyamānāṁ tadīya-sukhada-savidhāṁ bhū-cāraṇa-varya-kiśorīṁ tuṅganarmākhya-vaihāsikīṁ cakṣuḥ-kūṇanena sa-smitam ālokayantyāṁ tuṅgavidyāyāṁ, tuṅganarmā kiñcit sannidhāya sa-smita-man-mukham avalokayantī nijagāda – citre ! sa tāvad artho’smābhir na jñāyate kintu tayāpsarasā punaḥ punar āgatya sa-vinayaṁ svayam āgraha-bhareṇa suprasannīkṛtāt yathārtha-padavīka-miśra-varād adhītya tad-vṛtti- catuṣṭaya-vyākhyānam asmat-sahita-tuṅgavidyāgrataḥ sāṅgaṁ yad akāri tat samāsena mayā kathyamānaṁ śṛṇuta –*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā was too shy to speak anything. She glanced with squinting eye at the jester named Tuṅganarmā, a young girl dear to Rādhā, an expert singer, of similar nature, who could give her happiness by her service. She had not been mentioned at all. Tuṅganarmā approached me and glanced at my smiling face. She said, “O Citrā! I do not know the meaning completely, but the Apsarā came repeatedly and humbly pleased him by her eagerness. She learned by the mercy of the person appropriately called Miśra and explained the four topics with their accessories to Tuṅgavidyā and me. Please hear what I speak in summary form.**

*prathamaṁ śṛṇu dūtī-dvārā vā kṛtābhiyogena yūnor milanaṁ sandhiḥ \| tasya vṛttir vivaraṇam abhisārādir iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**“First hear about** ***sandhi*****. This is the meeting of lovers brought about by messengers or personal efforts. Its expansions (*****vṛtti*****) are making appointments etc.**

*tataḥ kucālambha-pariṣvaṅga-cumbanādhara-pāna-rūpāṇāṁ śṛṅgāra-bhedānāṁ catuṣṭayam eva catuṣṭayam \| tasya tasya vṛttir nakha-kṣatādiḥ, bāhu-bandha-bhaṅgy- ādiḥ, gaṇḍa-sthale savilāsa-mukha-kamalāropaṇādiḥ, savaidagdhya-daṁśādir iti \|*  
*tato’nyonya-narma-lapitam ākhyātam \| tasya vṛttiḥ paraspara-jayākāṅkṣayā nigūḍhārtha-prahelikādi-prayoga iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**“The four items refers to four actions of love: touching the breasts, embracing, kissing and drinking the nectar of the lips. Its expansions (*****vṛtti*****) are scratching with nails, embracing with the arms in particular postures, placing the lips playfully on the cheeks of the lover, and skillful biting.** ***Akhyātā*** **refers to joking conversations between lovers. Its expansion is use of puzzles and riddles with hidden meanings with a desire to defeat the lover.”**

***citrā*** *sa-smitaṁ – tuṅganarman ! aśruta-caraitad apūrvārtha-vyākhyānam asmat- karṇa-gocarīkṛtaṁ, bhavatyā tat kṛd-vṛtter apy arthaḥ suṣṭhu nirucyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Citrā said with a smile, “O Tuṅganarmā! This explanation of meanings which we heard is astonishing and unprecedented. You should also explain the meaning of** ***kṛd-vṛtti*****.”**

***tuṅganarmā*** *-- ānandaṁ karotīti ānanda-kṛt sambhogaḥ \| tasya vṛttiḥ śītkāra-cakṣu- nirmīlanādir iti \| kiṁ ca kānta-darpācāryādy-avatāriṇā kalāpa-priyeṇa siddha- kumāreṇānena tat-tad-vigrahāntareṇaiva yat kalāpa-vyākaraṇam āvirbhāvitaṁ tatrātyantopādeyatvena atiśaya-rahasyatvena ca prakaṭam anigadya bhaṅgyā nāma- dheyāntareṇa ca yat kiñcit nigūhitaṁ tad bhavad-vidha-rasika-snigdha-vidagdha- janair vidagdha-buddhi-sakhībhiḥ samam adhika-vicāreṇa samanubhavanīyam iti \|*

**Перевод:**

***“Ānanda-kṛt*** **means “to produce bliss” and thus refers to conjugal enjoyment. Its expansion is sighing and closing the eyes. Kalāpa-priya, a perfected youth who is Kānta Darpa Ācārya’s avatāra, an alternate form of his, produced the grammar book called Kalāpa. It does not reveal things explicitly because they are of the highest excellence and most secret. What lies hidden by use of indirect expression and other terminologies can be understood by skillful persons like you immersed in rasa, through profound deliberation together with the sakhīs endowed with clever intelligence.”**

***campakalatā*** *kiñcid vihasya – tuṅgavidye ! etad-bhaṭṭa-pādānāṁ dvāv eva bhujau tatrāpy atīva sukumārau tat katham ekadaiva ṭīkā-catuṣṭayaṁ lekhituṁ śaknotu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Campakalatā said, laughing slightly, “O Tuṅgavidyā! Kuhaka Bhaṭta has only two arms and is very young. How could he write four commentaries at the same time?”**

***tuṅgavidyā*** *-- mugdhe ! nijendra-jāla-balenāyaṁ bhuja-catuṣṭayam api prāduṣkartuṁ śaknoti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “O fool! He can manifest four arms by the strength of his magic.”**

***lalitā --*** *satyaṁ satyaṁ vāsantika-rāsollāsa-mahotsave parama-rāsa-sthalī-nikaṭa-varti- praviṣṭaka-nāmāraṇya-khaṇḍāntar-nikuñjāntarāle’pi para-rāmā-ratnam apahartuṁ nikhila-ballavī-vṛnda-vañcanāya ca sva-paricyāyakādbhuta-nija-mādhurī-santatim avaguṇṭhya kuhaka-balād evānena caturbhujatvam āviṣkṛtam āsīt \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “That is very true. Within the bower of a portion of the forest named Praviṣṭaka, near Parama-rasa-sthalī, during the spring festival of the raṣa dance, to fool all the gopīs, and to steal the jewel among others’ wives, he covered his multitude of astonishing sweetness by which he could be recognized, and manifested four arms by the power of his illusion (*****kuhaka*****).”**

***viśākhā –*** *lalite ! satyam etat sarvam asya kuhaka-bala-vijṛmbhitam eva yat priya-sakhyā saha gadya-padyair narmālāpa-goṣṭhyāṁ tāṁ jetuṁ nija-kuhaka-pāṇḍityena samprati vṛddhi-vṛttim ācaratā pada-dvayasya vṛddhiḥ kadācid yad anena kriyate tac cāsmābhir api dṛṣṭam asti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “O Lalitā! All this is true. It is a manifestation of his power of illusion. We have seen that he sometimes increases his two feet (buying and selling), increasing the interest rates by his illusory learning in order to conquer Rādhā, while making jokes with her using words or poetry.”**

***sudevī*** *– viśākhe ! caturṇām eteṣāṁ śāstra-kāriṇāṁ prāyeṇaika eva vyavasāyaḥ kathaṁ dṛśyate ?*

**Перевод:**

**Sudevī said, “O Viśākhā! Why do the four authors of scriptures seem to have one business?”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *sudevi ! etad vivaraṇam alpākṣareṇa tuṅganarmaṇā yad akāri tat kiṁ tvayā nahi śrutam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Did you not hear the short description made by Tuṅgavidyā?”**

***sudevī –*** *yāva-grāma-stha-priya-sakhyāṁ tadānīṁ datta-manaskayā mayā tat samyaktayā nāvadhāritaṁ tat kṛpayā tvayaiva vistareṇa saṁkathya śrāvyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Sudevī said, “Since I was absorbed in thinking of our friend living in Yāva-grama, I was not paying attention. Please say it again in detail.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *sudevi ! śrūyatām \| vastuta ete catvāraḥ kuhaka-bhaṭṭa-nāmā eka eva kumāro bhavati \| sa eva kṛtya-bheda-vinoda-sampādanāyātmanaḥ prakṛṣṭatara- kuhaka-prabhāvāt prakaṭitenaikena vigraheṇaiva kānta-darpācārya iti apareṇa śyāmala-miśra iti padavīm adhyārūḍho vartate \| alīka-rāja-paṇḍitena samaṁ tu sphuṭam asya pṛthag-vigrahatā nāsty eva kintv ayam eva sadā dharmī kumāraḥ \| sphuṭam atra vilāsa-viśeṣollāsa-karaṇāya kiñcit prakāśa-bhedenālīka-rāja-paṇḍiteti nāmāntaram urarīkṛtya kāmam ātmīyānām ātmanaś ca paramānanda-kallolam āsphālayan viharate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “O Sudevī! Actually the four are one youth named Kuhuka Bhaṭta. By his power of illusion he alone appears in one form as Kānta Darpācarya in order to accomplish pleasure by different means. In another form he appears as Śyāmala Miśra. He is also Alīka-rāja-paṇḍita, without even a different form. He is always the same youth emanating various qualities in these forms. In order to produce joy in particular pastimes with slight difference in manifestation, he accepts the name Alīka-rāja-paṇḍita and plays about, splashing huge waves of the highest bliss for himself and those close to him.”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *haṁho ! yac caturbhujatvādika-līlāyitam akhilam asmābhiḥ siddhatā- prabhāvair evāvirbhāvyate \| tan mūrkhābhir ābhir ugrābhiḥ kuhaka-bhaṭṭābhir nija- nikṛṣṭa-kuhaka-prabhāvair eveti santatam udghuṣyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Ah! My showing four arms arises by my mystic powers. It manifests by the inferior illusory power of foolish girls, who are ferocious sorceresses (kuhaka bhaṭtas).”**

*iti mad-vacaḥ samākarṇya sarvāḥ – anena siddha-gosvāminā sva-prabhāveṇa nijālīkatāpi siddhī-sampādya sva-mukhenaiva paraṁ nirdhāritā vyadhāyīti sarvair nirbharam ullāsyamāne hāsa-kutūhale \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, everyone said, “By his own mouth this master of powers has said that his show of falsity arise by his powers.” They all heartily laughed in glee.**

***ahaṁ*** *svagataṁ – bho āścaryam āścaryam amūbhir vāvadūka-ballavībhir vacana- prakāśa-paripāṭībhir bāḍham alīkataiva mayi siddhīkṛteti manasi vibhāvya lajjayā mayā tad-anākarṇita-mudrayaiva tāḥ suṣṭhv avadhāryeva kiñcid vihasya vyāhṛtaṁ – bho mugdhā yauvanāndhā vilāsinyaḥ ! yady atra nahi vaḥ pratītis tarhi sarveṣāṁ samakṣam eva sva-siddhiṁ darśayan prathamam asyāḥ kaṇṭhe vana-mālā bhavāni iti vihasya campakalatām upasarpan – vayasya siddhasya tava para-ramaṇī-sparśaḥ paramānyāyya iti vihasya vadatā madhumaṅgalena nivartito’ham avadaṁ – sakhe! siddhasya siddhayā saṁyogo mithaḥ paramānanda-lābhāyaiva bhavati \| uktaṁ ca śrī- nāradena – paramānanda-lābhāya sva-yūthyām eva saṁśrayed iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said to myself, “Ah! This is amazing! It is really amazing! These clownish cowherd girls have proved that I display falsity by the skill of their words.” Thinking, in shame, pretending that I did not hear them, catching their attention, I said with a laugh, “O playful fools blinded by youth! If you do not believe, in front of everyone I will show my powers. I will become a flower garland on Campakalatā’s neck.” Laughing, I approached her.**  
**Madhumaṅgala stopped me, saying “It is most improper for a perfected soul to touch another man’s wife.”**  
**I said, “O friend! By the power of this perfected soul, our union will produce the highest bliss. Nārada has said that one should take shelter of persons of one’s own group to attain the highest bliss.”**

*iti niśamya hasantīṣu sarvāsu paramānanda-janita-kampādi-sāttvika-vikārān bhayenaivācchādayantī campakalatā prapalāyya kuñjāntarāle praviṣṭā rādhāṁ pṛṣṭhe samāliṅgya nilīnāsīt \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, they all laughed. Campakalatā hid her sāttvika-bhāva of trembling which arose from the highest bliss, by pretending that it was fear. She fled and entered the bower. Embracing Rādhā, she disappeared behind her.**

***tato’haṁ*** *citrāṁ mauktikāni haste cālayann uktavān – citre ! samakṣam āgatya mauktika-mūlyaṁ śṛṇu śṛṅgāra-karma-vicakṣaṇāyās tava mūrti-mañjuṣikāyāṁ tat- sādhanāni bahūni santīti tvat-prāṇa-preṣṭha-sakhyā kathitam asti \| tair vicitra- śṛṅgāreṇa mat-pratyaṅgāni tathā bhūṣaya yathāham api tvad-vakṣaḥ-stha-kāñcana- ghaṭa-dvayam ardha-candra-patrāṅkurādibhiḥ santoṣeṇālaṅkṛtya tām ānandayāmīti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said to Citrā, while moving some pearls in my hand, “O Citrā! Come near and hear the price of the pearls. Rādhā has told me that you are expert in decorating (and in love making) and that in the treasure chest of your body there are many ornaments to be used for this purpose. Please decorate my limbs with those colorful ornaments so that I will decorate to satisfaction the golden pots on your chest with patterns of the crescent moon, leaves and sprouts, and give Rādhā bliss.”**

*tac chravaṇataḥ sa-krodhaṁ* ***citrā*** *– aye aviratam ativikaṭa-bhaṇḍatoccaṇḍa-caṇḍikāviṣṭa ! aye trijagati prasiddha-dhṛṣṭatodbhaṭa sākoṭa-bāṭikā-kuraṅga-kuṭumbinī-kula-naṭa ! tvad-yogyābhis tat-tat-sādhana-karma-karmaṭhābhis tābhir eva sa-santoṣaṁ sucitritena bhavatā tā eva suślāghaṁ suvareṇa santatam anukīrtyantāṁ itas tvaritam apasaratu svāmī \|*

**Перевод:**

**On hearing this, Citrā became angry and said, “Ah! You are possessed by ferocious Durgā, causing you to indulge in monstrous buffoonery. Ah! You are famous in the three worlds as the most audacious person. You are the pet dancer for some doe-like women in a garden of Sakoṭa. You can glorify those women who dally with you to your satisfaction, who are suitable for a person of your nature, who busily engage in actions to fulfil your ends.”**

*ity ākarṇanena jāta-hāsāḥ sarvās tāṁ suṣṭhu tuṣṭuvuḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, all burst out laughing and praised her.**

*tad anu* ***nāndīmukhī –*** *gokula-maṅgala ! sakala-gokula-jana-jīvātu-mukha-candra- prakāśaṁ bhavantaṁ akasmād vimanaskam iva saṁvīkṣya bhavad-ekāyuṣām asmākaṁ hṛdayāni marma-vraṇa-vedanā-vahni-jvālitānīva sphuṭanti santi \| tatas tan-nidānam avaśyaṁ kṛpayā prakāśyatām \| yathā bhagavatī-dvārā tat-pratīkāraṁ jhaṭiti niṣpādya śrīmantaṁ bhavantaṁ bāḍham ānandaivātmānaṁ sandhukṣayāma \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī then said, “O Kṛṣṇa, auspicious for Gokula! You, with your moonlight face, are the life of all the people of Gokula. Since you are our life and soul, seeing you suddenly depressed, our hearts are pained, as if burned by fire. This wounds us with sharp pain. You must reveal the cause of this, so that we can have Paurṇamāsī quickly prepare a remedy and we can revive you, who are possessor of all wealth and the essence of bliss.”**

*iti nāndīmukhī-vacanam ākarṇya savaiklavyam iva* ***mayoktaṁ --*** *nāndīmukhi śrūyatām \| adṛṣṭāśruta-cara-cāru-cāturya-niravadya-mahā-vaidagdhya-vividha- sudurbodha-narma-karma-svādhyāya-prathamācāryāyāḥ samasta-kalyāṇa-guṇa-maṇi- mañjuṣāyāḥ sva-parijana-gaṇa-jīvanī-bhūta-smita-nava-ghana-sāra-suvāsita-bhāṣita- pīyūṣāyāḥ śacy-ādi-saubhāgyavatī-vṛnda-varṇyamāna-saubhāgya-bharāyāḥ santatam indirādi-gaurī-gaṇa-mṛgyamāṇa-saundarya-rasa-sphurita-nakhāñcala-pratīkāyāḥ sura- nara-gaṇa-gandharva-vidyādhara-muni-vara-vandita-bhuvaneśvarī-śata-vandyamāna- padāravindāyāḥ, samasta-vaikuṇṭhato'pi paramottama-parama-vyomato'pi bṛṁhita- mahā-mahimottara-vṛndāṭavī-mahā-yoga-pīṭha-mahā-siṁhāsane saṁbhṛta- mahāratnābhiṣekāyāḥ mahādevyāḥ sudhā-sāgara-mathanotthita-rādhety-akṣara- yugala-ghanībhūta-tat-sārāṁśa-kalasa-dvaya-vinirmita-rādheti-nāma-dheyād apūrvāmṛta-sāra-vikāra-viśeṣeṇāpyāyita-śītkārita-caturdaśa-bhuvanāyāḥ, santata- saurabhya-niravadhi-saundarya-nistula-kaumalya-nirbhara-varāruṇyādi-vinirjita- raktotpala-kula-caraṇa-paricaraṇaika-jīvine sakala-varivasyā-viśāradāya mahyaṁ svayam āhūyāsaṅkocatayā mat-kāmita-pratyaṅga-sevā yat tayā na dīyate \| atas tasyā mayi madhurāṁ prītim anavadhārya tat-pūrṇāṁśāṁ yathārtha-nāmnīṁ tuṅgavidyāṁ bhagavatī-mukhāt niśamya satvarābhīṣṭa-lābhāya enāṁ gurutvenāsādyāsyāḥ sakāśāt mahādevī-mantra-rājaṁ didīkṣiṣur ahaṁ tvāṁ bhagavaty-advitīyāṁ prapanno'smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing her, I spoke feebly. “Please listen, Nāndīmukhī! She is the first teacher for studying various type of unfathomable joking and great, faultless, amiable skills, never before seen or heard of. She is a treasure chest of gems of all good qualities. The nectar of her speech fragrant with the new camphor of her smile gives life to her associates. She is filled with all auspiciousness, praised by groups of auspicious women like Śacī. The tip of her toe nail radiates beautiful rasa continually sought by Pārvatī and Lakṣmī. Her lotus feet are worshipped by hundreds of goddesses who are themselves worshipped by the best of sages, Vidyādharas, Gandharvas, devatās and humans. This great goddess was bathed in jewels on a great throne in the great yoga-pīṭha in Vṛndāvana, whose glory is far greater than all Vaikuṇṭhas and even the highest planet in the spiritual sky. The name Rādhā, an astonishing transformation of the essence of nectar, which took the form of two golden pots, the condensation of the two syllables which arose from the churning of the ocean of nectar, caused the fourteen worlds to sigh in satisfaction. The continual fragrance, exalted beauty, incomparable softness, and pink hue of her lotus feet defeat a host of red lotuses. Though I accept service to those feet as my life and soul, and am expert at honoring her, she does not call me for unrestricted service to her limbs, a service cherished dearly by me. Not detecting any affectionate love for me, and hearing from Paurṇamāsī that appropriately Tuṅgavidyā (she who has great knowledge) is Rādhā’s full expansion, to attain my desired goal I will approach her, respecting her as my guru. I surrender to you, Nāndīmukhī, non-different from Paurṇamāsī, with the desire that Tuṅgavidyā give me the king of mantras for attaining the great goddess.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *vihasya \| sulakṣaṇa prathamaṁ tāvat śāstra-nirṇīta-gurūpasattir vidhīyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said while laughing, “O Kṛṣṇa, endowed with good qualities! First you should worship the guru according to the scriptures.”**

***mayoktam*** *\| bhadraṁ vacaḥ prathamaṁ guru-śiṣyayoḥ parīkṣaiva nyāyyā \| tatas tisras triyāmāḥ kutrāpi nirjana-kuñje kṣaṇe mahilāṁ kṣaṇe pumāṁsaṁ māṁ sampādya sva- vidyāyāḥ prabhāvam asau darśayatu \| tato viśrabhya parama-sambhrameṇa mayāpy asyāś caraṇa-yāvābharaṇa-śroṇi-mardana-vakṣoja-kaṇḍūyana-veṇī-bandhanādi-paricaryās sarvāpekṣayā kriyamāṇāsu suprasanneyaṁ tvat-sevayā parama- prasannāsmīti nirucya māṁ manobhavānanda-kara-nikuñja-maṇḍapa-vedikāṁ prāpayya bho vicakṣaṇa mad-vakṣaḥ-sthala-kārtasvara-maṅgala-ghaṭa-yugalaṁ svakara-yugalena spṛśan mṛgamada-kuṅkumādi-paṅka-lepa-pūrvakaṁ maṇi-puṣpa- mālābhir veṣṭayety ādibhāṣitamantrair ghaṭaṁ sthāpayitvā, sva-vakṣoja-mahā-prasāda-kuṅkumena mama lalāṭādiṣu tilakaṁ kṛtvā mṛgamadena mad-vakṣasi mahā- devī-nāma-mudrām abhilikhya bāhu-dvayaṁ ca tac-caraṇa-cihna-saubhāgya- mudrābhir aṅkīkṛtya sva-kaṇṭhād ekāvalī-mālām uttārya mat-kaṇṭhe nidhāya mama vakṣaḥ-pārśvayoḥ sva-kucau aṁsa-dvaye bāhulate mukhe ca svādharaṁ aparāṅgāny āgamānabhijñena mayā sphuṭam ajñātāny api paramāgamācāryeyaṁ svayaṁ tad-arpaṇa-sthāne'rpayatu iti ṣaḍ-aṅgāni vinyasya ṣaḍ-akṣara-mantra- rājasyāsya svayambhū-ṛṣi-gāyatrī chandaḥ śrī-gāndharvā devatā sa-bindu-prathama- varṇo bījaṁ śaktir upāsyopāsakayor mithaḥ-suyukta-rati-janana-pūrvakābhīṣṭa-kāma- siddhaye viniyoga ity ādi pūrvāṅgaṁ yathā-vidhi sampādya,*

**Перевод:**

**I replied. “You have spoken well. It is a rule that the guru and disciple should first test each other. For three nights in a solitary bower, let her change me into a female and then back into a male and thus show her powers. Thus developing faith in her as a guru, with all expectations, I will enthusiastically decorate her feet with lac, massage her buttocks, relieve the itching on her breasts and tie her braids. Being pleased she will say, ‘I am very satisfied with your service.’**  
**“She will take me to the platform in the bower pavilion, the delight of Cupid. She will say, ‘O splendid disciple! Touch the two auspicious golden pots on my chest with your hands. After anointing them with musk and kuṅkuma, decorate them with jewels and flower garlands.’ She will have me establish the pot and placing tilaka on my forehead using the mahāprasāda kuṅkuma from her breasts. She will write the name of the great goddess on my chest with musk and mark my arms with the auspicious symbols of her feet. She will take her single-stranded necklace and put in around my neck. The supreme knower of the scriptures will offer her two breasts to the sides of my chest, her two arms to my shoulders, her lips to my lips, and other limbs not known to me, since I do not know the scriptures, to the appropriate places. In this way she will perform ṣaḍ-aṅga-nyāsa (touching six parts of the body). She will recite the sage of the six syllable king of mantras, Svayambhū, and as well the meter, gāyatri; she will recite the devatā, Gāndharvā, and then the śakti, which is the bīja** ***raṁ*****. The viniyoga (application) is for attaining desires which produce harmonious union of the worshiper and the object of worship. After completing these preliminaries, she will make me meditate for a long time using the following verse.**

*atha svīya-saras-tīra-kuñja-raṅga-sthale mudā \|*  
*sabhyānālī-gaṇān bhaṅgyā dhinvantīṁ narma-nartanaiḥ \|\|*  
*gaurīṁ raktāmbarāṁ ramyāṁ sunetrāṁ susmitānanām \|*  
*śyāmāṁ śyāmākhilābhīṣṭa-sādhikāṁ rādhikāṁ śraye \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**‘I take shelter of Rādhā, who fulfills all of Kṛṣṇa’s desires, who is the counterpart of Kṛṣṇa, who has a smiling face, beautiful, attractive eyes, red cloth, gold complexion, who joyfully pleases her gathered friends with her joking dance of indirect expression, in the playground within the bower on the bank of Rādhā-kuṇḍa.’**

*iti dhyānaṁ ca suciraṁ kārayitvā kṛpayā mahyaṁ kāmine kāma-bīja-puṭitaṁ mantra- rājam upadiśya māṁ kṛtārthīkarotu \|*

**Перевод:**

**“She will then make me successful by mercifully teaching the king of mantras enclosed with the kāma-bīja, which I desire. She will in this way teach me the king of eulogies as follows:**

*tad anu --*  
*mahā-bhāvojjvalac-cintā-ratnodbhāvita-vigrahām \|*  
*sakhī-praṇaya-sad-gandha-varodvartana-suprabhām \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Your form has been created with cintāmaṇi and shines with mahābhāva. You shine with ointments of the best, pure fragrance of your sakhīs’ affection.**

*kāruṇyāmṛta-vīcibhis tāruṇyāmṛta-dhārayā \|*  
*lāvaṇyāmṛta-vanyābhiḥ snapitāṁ glaptendirām \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Defeating Lakṣmī, you are bathed in waves of sweet compassion, in showers of sweet youth, and in floods of sweet beauty.**

*hrī-paṭṭa-vastra-guptāṅgīṁ saundarya-ghusṛṇāñcitām \|*  
*śyāmalojjvala-kastūrī-vicitrita-kalevarām \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Your limbs are hidden by the silk cloth of shyness and honored with the saffron of beauty. Your body is made astonishing with dark, shining musk.**

*kampāśru-pulaka-stambha-sveda-gadgada-raktatāḥ \|*  
*unmādo jāḍyam ity etai ratnair navabhir uttamaiḥ \|\|4\|\|*  
*kḷptālaṅkṛti-saṁsliṣṭāṁ guṇālī-puṣpa-mālinīm \|*  
*dhīrādhīrātva-sad-vāsa-paṭa-vāsaiḥ pariṣkṛtām \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You are ornamented with excellent gems of shivering, tears, hairs standing on end, being stunned, perspiration, choking voice, changing color, madness and insentience. You wear a flower garland of all good qualities. You are adorned with the perfumed petticoat of steadiness and unsteadiness.**

*pracchanna-māna-dhammillāṁ saubhāgya-tilakojjvalām \|*  
*kṛṣṇa-nāma-yaśaḥ-śrāva-vataṁsollāsi-karṇikām \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**‘You wear the bright tilaka of auspiciousness and braided hair of covered anger. You wear sparkling earrings for hearing Kṛṣṇa’s name and fame.**

*rāga-tāmbūla-raktauṣṭhīṁ prema-kauṭilya-kajjalām \|*  
*narma-bhāṣita-nisyanda-smita-karpūra-vāsitām \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Your lips are stained red with the betel juice of attachment and your eye mascara is your crooked prema. You are fragrant with smiles permeated with clever words.**

*saurabhāntaḥ-pure garva-paryaṅkopari līlayā \|*  
*niviṣṭāṁ prema-vaicittya-vicalat-taralāñcitām \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You sit upon the couch of pride in the inner chambers of fragrance and wear a center jewel of fickle prema-vaicittya.**

*praṇaya-krodha-sac-colī-bandh-guptī-kṛta-stanām \|*  
*sapatnī-vaktra-hṛc-choṣi-yaśaḥ-śrī-kacchapī-varām \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Your breasts are hidden by the bodice of anger caused by affection. Your fame and beauty are like a vīṇā, which shrivels the faces and hearts of competitors.**

*madhyatātma-sakhī-skandha-līlā-nyasta-karāmbujām \|*  
*śyāmāṁ śyāma-smarāmoda-madhulī-pariveśikām \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“You place your lotus hand casually on the shoulder of your sakhī, moderate temperament. You, the beloved of Kṛṣṇa, distribute the sweet honey of Kṛṣṇa’s love.**

*tvāṁ natvā yācate dhṛtvā tṛṇaṁ dantair ayaṁ janaḥ \|*  
*sva-dāsyāmṛta-sekena jīvayāmuṁ suduḥkhitam \|\|11\|\|*  
*na muñcec charaṇāyātam api duṣṭaṁ dayāmayaḥ \|*  
*ato gāndharvike hā hā muñcainaṁ naiva tādṛśam \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Offering respects to you, holding grass with my teeth, I beg that you bring to life this suffering person by sprinkling on him the nectar of serving you. Being merciful, you should not reject the person who has surrendered to you, though he may be sinful. O Rādhā! Do not give up this sinful person.**

*premāmbhoja-marandākhyaṁ stave-rājam imaṁ janaḥ \|*  
*śrī-rādhikā-kṛpā-hetuṁ paṭhaṁs tad-dāsyam āpnuyāt \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The person who recites this king of eulogies called ‘Nectar of the Lotus of Prema’ for attaining Rādhā’s mercy will attain service to her.”**

*imaṁ stava-rājam apy upadiśatu \|\|*  
*tataḥ śrī-guror labdhābhīṣṭa-kāmo'smi iti sagadgadaṁ vadantaṁ sakampaṁ tac- caraṇa-kamalayoḥ patitaṁ māṁ sānandaṁ samutthāpya samāliṅgya svādharocchiṣṭa- pīyūṣa-prasādaṁ saṁbhojya ca sva-mukhodgīrṇa-tāmbūlaṁ man-mukhe vitaratu \| brahmacāriṇo mama tāmbūla-bhakṣaṇam anucitam iti cet tarhi karpūra-vāsita-nija- mukha-vāsena man-mukha-śuddhiṁ vitanotu \| tataḥ kṛtārthena mayaitad-abhīpsitāni anyāny api bahūni ca muktāphalāni dakṣinātvena samarpyamāṇāni kṛpayā svīkṛtya mām anugṛhṇātu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Having received what I desired from the sacred guru, falling at her lotus feet while trembling and speaking in the choked voice, she will raise me up joyfully, embrace me, and feed me the nectar prasādam from her lips. She will then give to my mouth betel chewed by her mouth. Since it may be unsuitable for a brahmacārī like me to eat betel, she may give me mouth spices with camphor from her mouth, for purifying my mouth. Mercifully accepting many pearl fruits, those which she desired and others also, which I will offer to her as dakṣiṇā because of achieving my goal, she will show mercy to me.”**

*iti niśamya sarvāsu sa-smitaṁ* ***tuṅgavidyā****-mukham avalokayantīṣu tayāpy ucchalitāntarānandam āvṛtya bhrū-bhaṅgena saroṣam iva mām īṣad avalokya bhāṣitaṁ*  
*\| nāndīmukhi tvaṁ siddhā tapasviny asi \| tasmād etad-vidhinā tvam evainaṁ dīkṣaya \| tadāsya siddhāto mantra-grahaṇāt svābhīṣṭa-kāma-lābho jhaṭiti sampatsyata iti vyāhṛtya sakrodhaṁ gṛhāya gacchantīṁ kareṇa gṛhītvā vyāghoṭya* ***viśākhā*** *vihasya nāndīmukhīṁ prāha -- nāndīmukhi! asya samprati prāpta-vyalīkasya dīkṣā-dāne mahān eva pratyavāyaḥ syād ity ācāryeyaṁ tvāṁ prati krudhyati \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**When everyone, smiling on hearing this, looked at Tuṅgavidyā’s face, Tuṅgavidyā covered her rising bliss within and glanced at me as if angry, while frowning. She said, “O Nāndīmukhī! You are a perfect renunciate. You should give him initiation according to these rules. He will quickly attain his desires from receiving the mantra from such a perfected being.”**  
**As she was leaving in anger, Viśākhā took her hand, brought her back, and laughingly said, “O Nāndīmukhī! The ācārya is angry with you because it is a great sin to give initiation to that hypocrite.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *-- mithyā-vādini viśākhe! sakala-sadharma-rājīva-bandhor gokula-rājasya sat-tanaye katham alīka-parivādam āropayasi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “You are a liar Viśākhā! Why do you spread the rumor that the pure son of Nanda, the friend of the lotus of dharma, is a hypocrite?”**

***viśākhā --*** *tubhyaṁ śape satyam evaitad doṣam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “I swear to you that this is the truth. He is at fault.”**

***nāndīmukhhī*** *-- kas tāvad doṣaḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “What is his fault?”**

***viśākhā --*** *ucchiṣṭa-bhojanam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “He has eaten left overs.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *-- kasya ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Whose left overs?”**

***viśākhā --*** *dāsyāḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “A maid servant’s.”**

*nāndīmukhī vihasya -- kā tāvat sā dāsī ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Who is this maid servant?”**

***viśākhā --*** *śākhoṭa-vana-vāsinī kācin māyayā gopīva bhūtvā kaṁsa-bhṛtya-govardhana- malla-gṛhiṇīm āsādya tām uvāca candrāvali ! tvaṁ bhuvaneśvaryāś caṇḍikāyāḥ priya- paricārikāsi \| aham api tad-gaṇa-madhye ekāsmi \| tataḥ sneha-bhareṇa tva-sakhītvam icchāmi \| tac chrutvā samīcīnam etad ity āliṅgya sā candrāvalī yāṁ sakhyena parijagrāha saiva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “This inhabitant of Śākhoṭa-vana, disguising herself as a gopī, came to the house of Govardhana Malla, the servant of Kaṁsa and said to Candrāvalī, ‘You are the servant of Caṇḍikā, goddess of the universe. I am one of her followers. Out of great affection, I desire to be friends with you.’ Hearing this Candrāvalī said, “That is agreeable,” and embraced the woman, having accepted her as a friend.**

***nāndīmukhī --*** *saiva kā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Who is she?”**

***viśākhā --*** *apūrvā padmā sā sarvatra prasiddhā bhavatyāpi jñāyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “She is the remarkable Padmā, famous everywhere. You know her.”**

***nāndīmukhī --*** *kiṁ tad ucchiṣṭam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “What are the remnants?”**

***viśākhā --*** *tan mecakādhara-kupī-sthitaṁ parama-pāvanaṁ madhu \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “The most purifying honey kept in the well of her dark lips.”**

*etan niśamya kiñcit smayamānāṁ rādhikām avalokayantas tat-sabhāsadaḥ sarve janā jahasuḥ \| tato* ***nāndīmukhī --*** *viśākhe ! kenāpy etad dṛṣṭam asti ?*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, glancing at Rādhā who was smiling, the assembly laughed. Then Nāndīmukhī said, “Viśākhā! Did anyone see this?”**

***viśākhā --*** *atha kim \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Yes.”**

***nāndīmukhī --*** *kena ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Who saw?”**

*viśākhā - vividha-giridhātu-paricchadān ānetuṁ priya-sakhyā gāndharvayā preṣitābhyāṁ mallī-bhṛṅgībhyāṁ mānasa-gaṅgā-nikaṭoṣara-taṭe prakaṭam evaitat parama-pāvanaṁ karma paraśva eva sākṣād avalokyāgatya sarvāsāṁ purato vivicya kathitam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Mallī and Bhṛgī, who were sent by our dear friend Gāndharvā to bring cloth dyed with various minerals personally saw this most pure activity the day before yesterday at a barren spot near the bank of Mānasa-gaṅgā. They told this to everyone.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *sacintam iva -- sakala-gokula-jīvanībhūtasyāsya kathaṁ tad-doṣa- dhvaṁso bhavati ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī looked thoughtful and then said, “How can he who is the life of Gokula destroy this sin?”**

***viśākhā --*** *prāyaścittācaraṇenaiva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “He can do this by some acts of atonement only.”**

***nāndīmukhī --*** *tato bhagavatītas tad-doṣa-vihita-niṣkṛtiṁ sampādya puruṣottamam enaṁ śuddhaṁ vidhāya dīkṣayantu bhavaytaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “After he has performed an atonement for the sin according to Paurṇamāsī’s direction and been purified you should initiate this exalted man.”**

*tac chrutvā* ***campakalatā*** *prāha -- mugdhe ! ujjvala-maṇi-saṁhitāyām eva vivṛto'sti tan-niṣkṛti-vidhir bhavatyāḥ prāyeṇa gocaro na bhavatīti tayaiva kathā-prasaṅge kathitam asti \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Campakalatā said, “Fool! The method of atonement that you describe has been given in Ujjvala-maṇi-saṁhitā. But Paurṇamāsī says that this work is not available.”**

***nāndīmukhī --*** *atra tat-saṁhitāṁ kas tāvaj jānāti ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Who knows this saṁhitā here?”**

***campakalatā --*** *priya-sakhī gāndharvaiva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Campakalatā said, “Our dear friend Gāndharvā knows it.”**

***nāndīmukhī --*** *adhunā sā kilātra sabhā-madhye na vidyate \| tat katham idaṁ jhaṭiti nirvahatu ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “But she is not here in the assembly. How can we quickly purify him?”**

***viśākhā --*** *tad-advitīyeyaṁ tato'dhīta-sandarbha-tat-saṁhitā-lalitā nirantaraṁ tad abhyasyantī suṣṭhu taj jānantī tan nirūpayiṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Lalitā, who is non-different from her, has repeatedly studied the saṁhitā arranged in order. She knows it thoroughly and will determine the atonement.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *sa-kāku -- sakhi lalite ! vicārya yathocita-niṣkṛtir ādiśyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “My friend Lalitā! Think carefully and teach us the suitable atonement.”**

***lalitā*** *kiñcid vihasya -- priya-sakhi ! kṛta-vyalīko jano yadi sabhā-madhye svayam āgatya niḥśeṣaṁ svayam aghaṁ niṣkāpaṭyena nivedyānutapati, tadaivāsya prāyaścittaṁ kāryate iti purāna-kṛdbhiḥ nirṇitam asti \| tathā hi --*  
*saṁkathya svam aghaṁ goṣṭhyāṁ paścāt tapati yaḥ sphuṭam \|*  
*tasyaiva niṣkṛtiḥ sāṅgā munibhiḥ kāryate'khilā \|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā, slightly laughing, said, “Dear friend! If a liar personally comes into the assembly and completely reveals his sin with sincerity, atonement is accomplished. This has been prescribed in the Purāṇas. It is said:**  
**The person who reveals his sin in the assembly and clearly repents has his complete atonement performed by the sages.”**

*ity ākarṇya nāndīmukhyāṁ man-mukhaṁ sākūtam ālokayantyāṁ mayi ca subala- madhumaṅgalābhyāṁ saha lalitā-savidham āsādya kiñcid vivakṣamāṇe sati,* ***viśākhā*** *prāha -- he dhīra ! kāmāturāṇāṁ na ghṛṇā na lajjeti svabhāvataḥ kāminā tvayā yat kṛtaṁ tat kṛtam eva \| adhunā tv etat tasyāḥ puro vivicya tat sarvaṁ kathaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this Nāndīmukhī glanced at my face with meaning and approached Lalitā with Subala and Madhumaṅgala. As she was about to speak, Viśākhā said, “O sober Kṛṣṇa! Lusty people do not have aversion or shame. By nature you are a lusty person. What is done is done. Now, however, consider and reveal everything in front of Lalitā.”**

***tato'haṁ*** *smitam apavārya sānutāpam iva -- lalite ! caturthe'hani vighaṭita- gavānveṣaṇe gaurī-tīrtham evāptasya mama gaurī-mandirān niḥsṛtya kācid gaurī sahacarī carcikā haṭhena mad-urasi savya-kucenāhatya mādhavī-catuḥśālikāntarālaṁ mām ānīya kampamānasya mama mukhe tāmbūla-carvitaṁ pradāya yat kiñcit kṛtavatī tan-mohito'haṁ kim api nāvediṣam \| paraśvo'pi suvarṇa-sūtreṇa vividha-kusumair grathita-mālām ādāya rādhā-kuṇḍa-taṭa-nikuñja-nāgarīṁ gāndharvām anusmarato mama mālya-haraṇa-kuṇḍa-taṭa-nikaṭa-mandārodyāna-parisare sā carcikā punar apy āvirbhūya balenaiva mama vāma-gaṇḍaṁ paricumbya mukhe svādhara-pīyūṣam arpitavatī tad etad agha-yugala-nirāśāya tan-mukha-kamalocchiṣṭa-madhu-pāna- rūpaṁ prāyaścittam ādiśatu bhavatī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Concealing a smile, as if repenting, I said, “O Lalitā! Four days ago while searching for straying cows, coming to Gaurī-tīrtha, some assistant of Durgā named Carcikā, coming out of a Durgā temple, forcefully struck her left breast against my chest, brought me within a mādhavī bower, and gave her chewed betel to my mouth as I trembled. Since I was bewildered, I do not know what else she did. The day before yesterday holding a garland made of various flowers, strung on a golden thread, I was remembering Gāndharvā who inhabits the bower on the bank of Rādhā-kuṇḍa. Near Mandara garden near the bank of Mālya-hāraṇa-kuṇḍa, the same Carcikā again appeared and forcibly kissed my left cheek and offered her lip nectar to my mouth. Please declare the atonement in the form of drinking the honey remnants from her mouth to annul these two sins.”**

***madhumaṅgalo*** *vihasyāha -- vayasya ! bhadrataram idaṁ niṣkṛtaṁ yat tad aghasyaiva vṛddhiṁ vitanoti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala, laughing said, “O friend! This atonement is wonderful since it increases the sin.”**

***tato’ham --*** *dhiṅ mūrkha ! kim api na jānāsi \| viṣasya viṣamauṣadham iti \| yathā -- vahni-santāpato naśyed vahni-santāpajo braṇaḥ \| iti \| evaṁ --darayet kaṇṭakaṁ viddhaṁ kaṇṭakenaiva paṇḍitaḥ \| ity ādivad ucchiṣṭa-bhojana-doṣam ucchiṣṭa-bhojanam eva darayati \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O fool! You do not know anything. The medicine for poison is poison. It is said that the pain arising from the heat of fire is destroyed by the heat of fire. And it is also said that the wise person removes a thorn piercing his body by another thorn. Thus the sin of eating remnants is removed by eating remnants”.**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ --*** *evaṁvidha-pāpa-vyādhi-cikitsāyāṁ tvam eva sad-vaidyo'si tat kiṁ lalitām anuvartase ?*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “You are an expert physician in curing the sickness of such sin. Why do you go to Lalitā?”**

***tato’ham --*** *sakhe \|*  
*jñāta-sāro'pi khalv ekaḥ sandigdhaḥ syāt svakarmaṇi \|*  
*iti cikitsakasyāpi cikitsānyaiḥ kriyata eva \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O friend! Even if a person is very knowledgeable, he will have a doubt concerning his own affairs. Thus to cure himself a doctor uses other doctors.”**

*iti niśamya* ***lalitā*** *smayamānā prāha -- he deva ! devyā saha devasyaiva saṅgaḥ sampatsyate tat katham asmākaṁ mānuṣīṇāṁ sā gocarībhavatu ?*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this Lalitā said while smiling, “O Lord! A deva should only associate with a devī. How can we see this devī?”**

*tato* ***mayoktaṁ --*** *lalite ! sā kutrāpi mānuṣi bhavatīnām adūra eva virājamānā vartate \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Lalitā! She exists not far away from you somewhere in a human form.”**

*tataḥ sarvāsu sakautukaṁ sa-śaṅkaṁ karṇākarṇi keyam iti vicārayantīṣu* ***rādhā***  
*svagataṁ -- etasya dhūrtasya narma-nyāsa etad-goṣṭhyām eva bhaviṣyatīti lakṣyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**All the sakhīs with worry and curiosity began whispering, “Who is she?” Rādhā said to herself, “It seems that this rascal will play a joke on the assembled sakhīs.”**

***viśākhā --*** *deva ! kathyatām kutra sā yathaināṁ saṁstutya sabhā-madhye samānīya bhavat-kāryam acirād asmābhir niṣpādyate ?*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “O Lord! Please tell us where she is, so that we can praise her, bring her among us, and we can quickly help you accomplish your atonement.”**

***tato’haṁ --*** *viśākhe ! bhavad-goṣṭhyām eva taḍid iva virājayantī sā \| kiṁ dīrgha- netrayāpi tvayā na dṛśyate ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Viśākhā! She exists among you like lightning. Though you have long eyes, do you not see her?”**

*iti mad-gambhīrālāpam ākarṇya lalitāyām anyāsu sarvāsu ca ātmany ātmani tāṁ narma-bhaṅgīm āśaṅkya parasparam ālokayantīṣu* ***viśākhayoktaṁ*** *-- lampaṭa ! kiṁ campakalatā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing my profound words, everyone including Lalitā began looking at each other, worrying that the joke referred to each one of them. Viśākhā then said, “O lecher! Is Campakalatā the devī?”**

***tato’haṁ --*** *na hi na hi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “No, not at all.”**

***viśākhā --*** *kiṁ jyeṣṭhā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Is it Jyesṭḥā?”**

***ahaṁ --*** *na hi na hi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “No, not her.”**

***subalaḥ --*** *raṅgadevī-sudevyayor ekatarā bhaviṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “It must be either Sudevī or Raṅgadevī.”**

***ahaṁ*** *-- anayor ekatarāpi na \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “It is not either of them.”**

***viśākhā --*** *āṁ niṣkṛti-kāriṇī lalitaiva bhaviṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Ah! It must be Lalitā, who performs atonements.”**

***tato’haṁ --*** *viśākhe ! iyam api na \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Viśākhā! It is not her.”**

***viśākhā --*** *iyam api na eṣāpi na asāv api na \| tat kiṁ ito'ntarhitā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “It is not her, nor her, nor her. She must have disappeared.”**

***tato’haṁ --*** *dhūrte ! tvam eva tāṁ jānāsi \| smṛtvā paśya \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O deceitful one! You know who she is. Think and see.”**

***lalitā --*** *hā hā sakhi viśākhe ! tāṁ prakāśya vikalasyāsya kārya-siddhiṁ drutam āpādaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Ah! My friend Viśākhā! Reveal the devī and help the crippled rascal quickly accomplish his atonement.”**

***viśākhā*** *smṛtim abhinīya -- lalite ! yā mayā jñāyate sā devī-manuṣyayor ekatarāpi na syāt \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā, pretending to remember, said, “O Lalitā! The person that I know is neither a devī nor a human.”**

***nāndīmukhī --*** *goṣṭha-madhye devīṁ mānuṣīṁ vā vinā kā punar itarā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “In our assembly who is there other than devīs and humans?”**

***viśākhā --*** *sā khalu śaṅkhinī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “She is a śaṅkhinī.”**

***lalitā --*** *kā tāvat sā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “And who is she?”**

***viśākhā*** *smitvāha -- sā durmukhī padmā yayāsya vyalīkaṁ kṛtam asti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā smiled and said, “It is the ugly-faced Padmā. She has made him sinful in the first place.”**

***tuṅgavidyā --*** *sakhi viśākhe ! sā śaṅkhinīti saṁkathya kathaṁ kaluṣam utpādayasi ? eṣā khalu bahu-tithaṁ kātyāyanī-devyā bahutara-sevā-prasādataḥ sākṣāt kātyāyanī vṛtāsti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “O my friend Viśākhā! How can you slander her with the name śaṅkhinī? By devotedly serving Kātyāyanī for a long time, by her mercy, she has directly become empowered as Kātyāyanī.”**

*tac chrutvā madhumaṅgalādiṣu hasatsu* ***mayoktaṁ*** *-- viśākhe ! tal-lajjayā yadi tvam anyataḥ kṣipasi tan mayaiva kathyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Madhumaṅgala and others laughed. I said, “O Viśākhā! If, because you are shy, you wish to insult others, I can name the person.”**

***viśākhā --*** *kathaya kathaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Please say.”**

*tato* ***mayoktaṁ --*** *sā carcikā-devī tvam eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “You are that Durgā devī (carcikā).”**

*tataḥ sā sarvāḥ smayamānā vilokya sakampādharam āha -- aye cañcala vañcaka! gopa- niṣṭhūra ! kaṁsāsura-sevaka-duṣṭa-kāliṅga-malla-gṛhiṇī-hāridra-rāga-carcita-sarvāṅga ! vidagdhya-sat-khaṇḍa ! cāturya-sac-candra-virahita ! tad-dhṛta-sneha-lubdha-tat-keli- koli-kānana-cara \| ayukta-padmā-śaṅkhinī-parivṛḍha \| avaidagdhya-raṅga-milita- pañcabāṇa-rasa-vyāpāri-mahādhūrta-vaṇig-vara ! etad dhārṣṭya-jambula-guḍa-haṭṭaṁ tatraiva śākhoṭa-vane gatvā prasāraya \| ito drutam apasara \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing everyone smiling, Viśākhā said with a trembling lip, “O fickle cheater! Cruel cowherd! You smeared turmeric on the limbs of the wife of the evil snake Govardhana Malla, servant of the demon Kaṁsa. You are an assemblage of craftiness, devoid of the moon of friendliness! You wander in the badari forests where she plays, greedy for her affection! You are the supplement to the unfit Śaṅkhinī Padmā! O best of the scheming merchants dealing in the five arrows of rasa mixed with clumsy play! Go to Śākhoṭa forest and hawk your jambu fruits of audacity. Leave this place immediately.”**

*iti śrutvā* ***rādhā*** *anuccaiḥ sa-ślāgham āha -- priya-sakhi viśākhe ! vijayinī bhava yan man-mānasa-gata-sambodhanoktyā māṁ sukhayanty asi \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Rādhā spoke inaudibly with praise. “O dear friend Viśākhā! May you be victorious since you please me with your words which awaken what is in my mind!”**

***raṅgadevī --*** *sakhi viśākhe ! tvaṁ kathaṁ kupasīty uktvā sakhi lalite ! śākhoṭavanīṁ mādhavī-catuḥśālikātvena mānasa-gaṅgā-taṭoṣara-pradeśaṁ mālya-haraṇa-saras-tīra- mandārodyānatvena, padmā-śaṅkhinī-mukhocchiṣṭaṁ ca priya-sakhī- viśākhādharāmṛtatvena nirūpya svamukhenaiva sānutāpaṁ sva-duritam aśeṣam anena vidagdha-dhūrtena sva-kadarya-kārya-prakaṭana-janiṣyamāṇa-lajjayā arthottha- vyaṅgenaiva tvayi niveditam \| tad upadiśāsya prāyaścittam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Raṅgadevī said, “My friend Viśākhā! Why are you angry? My friend Lalitā! He accepts the Śakhoṭa forest to be the mādhavī bower and he accepts the barren land on the bank of Mānasa-gaṅgā to be the Mandara garden on the bank of Mālya-haraṇa Lake. He takes the remnants from Padmā śaṅkhinī’s mouth to be the nectar from the lips of our dear friend Viśākhā. Thus this skillful cheat constantly offers himself to you in a repentant mood, which is difficult for him, using indirect expression, because of his shyness born from performing low actions. Therefore you prescribe his atonement.”**

*iti śrutvā* ***lalitā*** *vihasya -- ārya madhumaṅgala ! sakhe subala ! sannidhāya śrūyatām prāyaścittam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this Lalitā said with a laugh, “O respected Madhumaṅgala! O friend Subala! Come and hear the atonement.”**

***citrā --*** *sakhi lalite!*  
*sampattau vidyamānāyāṁ prāyaścittaṁ caturguṇam \|*  
*tato'pi rāja-putrāṇāṁ niṣkṛtiḥ ṣaḍ-guṇā matā \|\|*  
*iti smṛti-vākyaṁ smṛtvaiva prāyaścittam ādiśyatām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Citrā said, “My friend Lalitā! You should prescribe the atonement on remembering the smṛti statement ‘For persons having wealth, the atonement is four times what is normal. For sons of a king, the atonement is six times the regular amount.’”**

***lalitā --*** *mugdhe ! tato'pi rāja-putrāṇāṁ niṣkṛtiḥ syād dviṣaḍ-guṇā iti pāṭhaḥ saṁhitā- saṁmato mayā jñāyate \| ṣaḍguṇeti kathaṁ bhaṇyate ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “You are a fool! The atonement for the son of a king is twelve times. I know that this is approved by the saṁhitās. Why do you say only six times?”**

***citrā --*** *etac-chāstra-vijñayā tvayā yaj jñāyate tad eva satyam \| kintu govardhanoddharaṇa-dāvāgni-vimocana-śaṅkhacūḍādi-mardanādibhiḥ katidhānena yuvarājena vayaṁ na rakṣitāḥ sma ? tasmād idānīntana-tat-kṛta-bahutara- vaiguṇyam avigaṇayya kṛpayā ṣaḍ-guṇenaiva nirūpyatām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Citra said, “Since you know the scriptures well, whatever you know is correct. But have we not been saved by the prince many times—he lifted Govardhana, he freed us from the forest fire, and he killed Śaṅkhacūḍa. We should not consider his recent transgressions and instead fix his atonement at six times.”**

***lalitā --*** *bhadraṁ tva-kathitam evedam anuṣṭhīyate \| ādau pāpa-mocane snātvā tathā mānasa-gaṅgāyāṁ tri-dināni snātaḥ sann eka-viṁśati-dināni mallī-bhṛṅgyādhara- pañcāmṛta-pānena prathamaṁ mukha-doṣam utsārayatu \| paścād dvi-ṣaḍ-guṇā niṣkṛtiḥ karaṇīyā \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “You have spoken well. We will do that. He should bathe to remove his sin. After bathing for three days in Mānasa-gaṅgā, for twenty-one days he should drink the five types of nectar from the lips of Mallī and Bhṛṅgī to remove the sin in his mouth. Later the twelve times atonement should be enacted.”**

*iti śravaṇena kapaṭa-kopa-vikaṭo* ***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *prāha -- lalitike ! sad-dharma-setu- vrajendrayor etan-mātra-putrasya tathāsmad-vidha-vayasya-vargāṇāṁ sakala-gokula- vāsinām apy eka-jīvātor asya ceṭikā-pulindī-juṣṭa-bhakṣaṇena jāti-dhvaṁsaḥ kartum ārabdho bhavatībhis tad aham atidrutaṁ gatvā vṛttam etat sa-viśeṣam āśrāvya etat- pitarāv atrānīya etat saṅkaṭād amuṁ mocayan sauhṛdaṁ vitanomi \| iti nigadya phutkṛtya drutaṁ gacchantaṁ madhumaṅgalaṁ subalaḥ kare gṛhītvā balād iva nivartayāmāsa \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, with a great show of anger Madhumaṅgala said, “O Lalitā! This great son of Nanda and Yaśodā who uphold true dharma is the life of all us friends and all the people of Gokula. You are attempting to destroy his lineage by making him eat the remnants of servant girl Pulindas. I will immediately go and tell his parents about this, bring them here, release him from difficulty and show him friendship.” As he yelled this and hastily departed, Subala took his hand and by force brought him back.**

***lalitā --*** *anārya baṭo ! asmat-priya-sakhī-praṇayi-sakhyor anayor māhātmya-vijñāne tvam anabhijño'si \| tad bhavan nāndīmukhī-mukhād etac chrūyatām \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O uncultured brāhmaṇa boy! You do not know the glories of these two great friends of our dear sakhī. Hear about them from Nāndīmukhī.”**

***nāndīmukhī --*** *bhrātar madhumaṅgala !*  
*govardhana-girau ramye rādhā-kuṇḍaṁ priyaṁ hareḥ \|*  
*yathā rādhā priyā viṣṇos tasyāḥ kuṇḍaṁ priyaṁ tathā \|\|*  
*tathā --*  
*vaikuṇṭhāj janito varā madhupurī tatrāpi rāsotsavāt*  
*vṛndāraṇyam udāra-pāṇi-ramaṇāt tatrāpi govardhanaḥ \|*  
*rādhā-kuṇḍam ihāpi gokula-pateḥ premāmṛtāplavanāt*  
*kuryād asya virājato giri-taṭe sevāṁ vivekī na kaḥ \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “My brother Madhumaṅgala! Rādhā-kuṇḍa at Govardhana is dear to the Lord. As Rādhā is dear to the Lord so is her kuṇḍa. Mathurā is superior to Vaikuṇṭha. Vṛndāvana is superior to Mathurā because of the rāsa dance. Within Vṛndāvana, Govardhana is superior because Kṛṣṇa lifted it with his hand. There, Rādhā-kuṇḍa is superior because of its flood of prema. What intelligent person would not serve this shining place near Govardhana?**

*ity ādy-anusāreṇa śrī-bhagavatī-guru-śrī-devarṣi-prabhṛti-mahā-mahā-munīndra- gaṇair varṇita-mahā-mahimnaḥ śrī-govardhanopari-virājamānasya rādhā-kuṇḍasya dakṣiṇa-taṭe giri-darī-vāsinyāḥ pūrṇāḥ pulindya urugāyety ādi śrī-śukadeva-varṇita- mahā-saubhāgya-bharayoḥ pulinda-rāja-sutayor anayor adhara-galita-parama-pāvana- pañcāmṛtam etad-durita-nāśakaṁ kiṁ na syāt ? anyac ca śrūyatām \| vraja-navīna-yuva-dvandva-ratnaṁ prati viśākhādi-dvārā kvacit svayaṁ vā lajjām abhinīya mṛdu bhāṣita-vivicyamāna-narma-kalā-kalāpa-janita-paramānanda-viśeṣa-lābhāya tathā svābhilaṣita-paricaraṇa-viśeṣa-lābhāya ca raṅgamālā-prabhṛtaya etāḥ parama-praṇayi- sakhyo'pi paricārikā iva vyavaharanti \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“According to such statements, glorious Rādhā-kuṇḍa situated at Govardhana is described by Nārada, the guru of Paurṇamāsī, and other great sages. On its southern bank there are many cave dwellers, Pulindas, Urugayas etc. Why shouldn’t the five types of pure nectar flowing from the lips of these two auspicious daughters of the king of the Pulindas not destroy this sin? Hear something else. In order to attain special bliss arising from gentle joking in other’s gentle conversations and so that others can attain their desired service, sometimes by arrangement of Viśākhā, or by feiging shyness towards the young couple of Vraja, Raṅgamālā and others, though they are most exalted, act as servants, and help others (like Mallī and Brṅgī) meet Kṛṣṇa.”**

*etad-avasara eva netra-kuṇanena goṣṭhī-madhyāt tulasīm āhūya bandhurātibandhura- sugandhī-gandha-phalī-sadṛkṣa-dakṣiṇa-kara-kaniṣṭhāṅguli-śikharākṛṣṭa-sva-sīmanta- sindūra-rasa-prakaṭita-tat-saurabha-prasara-rūpa-surūpa-lipi-pracayenāti-suvāsitīkṛta- kanaka-ketakī-kusuma-dala-bhūta-varṇa-dūta ekaḥ \| śrī-gāndharvayā tat-kareṇa lalitādiṣu sañcāritaḥ \| tatas taṁ lalitā śirasi nidhāya sakhībhiḥ saha rahaḥ paripaṭhya nāndīmukhī-kare sa-smitam arpitavatī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā called Tulasī from the gathering by a glance. In a graceful script, scented by the vermillion from her hair part, using the tip of her little finger of her round right hand resembling the fragrant bael fruit, a scented letter having the hue of the golden ketakī flower manifested itself. Tulasī delivered the message to Lalitā and others. Lalitā, placing the message on her head, read it privately with the sakhīs and then gave it to Nāndīmukhī with a smile.**

***nāndīmukhī*** *smitvānandam abhinīya vakṣasi nidhāya vācayati --*  
*svasti śrīnāndīmukhī-śrī-lalitā-śrī-viśākhā-prabhṛti-prāṇa-preṣṭha-sakhī-vargeṣu pariṣvaṅga-parārdha-raṅga-pūrvikā kasyāścid vijñaptir iyam --*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī, feigning joy, held it to her chest and then read it: Good fortune to Nāndīmukhī, Lalitā, Viśākhā and all the dearest sakhīs. After offering millions of embraces, this is the message.**

*śrīdāma-subala-bhadrasenādi-pramukha-priya-vayasya-gaṇaiḥ saha govardhana- parisare sambhṛta-gocāraṇa-paramāmodasya vraja-rāja-nija-jīvita-parārdha-śatādhika- parama-priyatama-tanayasya vrajendra-mahiṣī-sva-prāṇa-parārdha-paripakva- hiraṇmayeṣṭakāghaṭā-ghaṭita-mahā-mandirāntarāla-vinirmita-prāṇa-parārdha-vividha-*  
*ratna-khacita-mahā-paryaṅka-kpta-vātsalya-nānā-vidha-kusuma-suvāsita-*  
*sukumāra-sutūlikopari-samadhigata-śayana-keli-paramānandasya*  
*mat-prāṇa-parārdhārbuda-parārdha-śata-nirmañchyamāna-vāma-caraṇa-kaniṣṭhāṅguli-nakhāñcala-pratīkasya nava-mṛgamada-parimalita-navanīta-nīlotpala-dala-kula-racita-sundara-sukumāra-vigrahasya dhīra-lalitasyāsya*  
*etāvat kaṭhora-prāyaścitta-śravaṇena mama hṛdayaṁ navanītam iva vidravavad āste \|*  
*tataḥ --*  
*kṛtānutāpa-lakṣāṇāṁ sukumāra-śarīriṇām \|*  
*snigdhānāṁ niṣkṛtiḥ samyak tantreṇaiva vidhīyate \|\|*  
*ity ujjvala-saṁhitā-vacanam ananusmṛtyaiva lalitayā yat pañcāmṛta-pāna-rūpaṁ prāyaścittaṁ kevala-mukha-śodhanāya ca tantreṇa vidhāyāsmin nirdoṣe vihite mama mahaty eva nirvṛtir jāyate ity alam ativistareṇa \|\|*  
*ihaiva prāṇa-pratima-praṇayi-sakhyau mallī-bhṛṅgiyau prati sa-praṇaya-pariṣvaṅga- sañcāraḥ kāryaś ca \|*  
*rāja-putro'yaṁ parama-pavitro mahā-vilāsī ca \| tan nija-caraṇa-kamala-ghātena kaṅkelli-latikāṁ puspitīkṛtya tan-makaranda-prasyanda-gaṇḍūṣaiś caturviṁśatyā sva- mukhaṁ viśeṣeṇa prakṣālya smita-karpūra-vāsita-madhura-pañcāmṛtaṁ vidhāya premṇā śanaiḥ śanais tathā pāyayitavyaṁ yathāsya sukumārasya duritaṁ drutam apayāti parama-sukham api niṣpadyate iti \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Taking great delight in herding the assembled cows near Govardhana with his dear friends headed by Śrīdama, Subala and Bhandrasena, the son of Nanda, is dearer to Nanda than millions of his lives. Within a pavilion constructed of golden bricks baked by millions of Yaśodā’s life airs, lying on a soft cushion scented with various flowers composed of her motherly affection, on a huge bed inlaid with various gems which are millions of her life airs, he attains the highest bliss in his pastimes of sleep. The tip of the little toe of his left foot is worshipped by my life airs billions of times. He has a beautiful form as soft as butter, made of blue lotus petals scented by fresh musk. Hearing that such a person must perform harsh atonements, my heart melts like butter.**  
**‘The atonement for persons of soft, charming body, who have signs of repentance, is directly prescribed in tantra.’**  
**“Not remembering the words of Ujjvala-saṁhitā, Lalitā has prescribed the atonement of drinking five nectars, only for purifying the mouth. I will become most happy if he becomes completely free of fault by use of tantra. What more can be said? Please offer my affectionate embraces to Mallī and Bhṛṅgī, sakhīs dear to my life. This prince is most pure and also most playful. Making the aśoka tree blossom by the kick of his lotus feet, he should be made to wash his face particularly twenty-four times with drops of sap flowing from it. He should then be made to drink sweet pañcāmrṭa of the gopīs’ smiles scented with camphor. In this way his sin will be quickly removed. This will produce the greatest happiness.”**

*iti niśamya sāntarānandaṁ rādhā-nikuñjam avalokayati mayi smitvā lalitā prāha -- yadyapi kṛtānutāpa-lakṣāṇām ity ādi sāmānya-vacanāntaraṁ*  
*sampattau vidyamānāyāṁ prāyaścittaṁ caturguṇam \|*  
*tatrāpi rāja -putrāṇāṁ niṣkṛtiḥ syād dviṣaḍ-guṇā \|\|*  
*iti viśeṣa-vacanena sāmānya-viśeṣayor viśeṣa-vidhir balavān iti nyāyenāsya dviṣaḍ- guṇam eva prāyaścittaṁ yuktam eva pṛthak pṛthak nirūpitam asti tathāpy ājñā gurūṇāṁ hy avicāraṇīyeti tad-ājñāṁ śirasi nidhāya tantreṇaiva kārayitavyam iti \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, I glanced towards Rādhā’s bower with inner bliss. Lalitā, smiling, spoke. “The general rule for a person who is repentant is prescribed in the tantras. Then there is a special rule mentioned after that. ‘Four times the amount is prescribed for wealthy persons and twelve times the atonement for princes.’ Between the ordinary and special rules, the special rules are stronger. Therefore his atonement should be twelves the regular one. That is prescribed. One need not consider the order of elders in this regard. Taking that order on your head, perform atonement according to tantra.”**

*tataḥ śiṣye vidyā garīyasīti mayokte lalitā sākūtam āha -- nāndīmukhi ! saundarya-*  
*rasa-bhareṇāpyāyita-nikhila-gokula-jana-nayanāravindāṁ para-rāsa-sthalī-vihāriṇīṁ parama-rasa-taraṅgiṇīṁ raṅgiṇi-sahacarīṁ hariṇīm etāṁ parihāya bāḍhamaya-*  
*vidagdha-kṛṣṇasāra-yuvā sakhī-sthaly-upaśalye tad-vāstavya-śaivyā-sahacarī- mṛgatṛṣṇikāṁ muhur muhur dhāvati \| tathā gāndharvā-sarovara-nikuñjāṅgane saurabha-prasara-parivāsita-sakala-diśāṁ campakalatā-sakhīṁ kāñcana-yūthikām apahāya govardhana-malla-gṛhakoṇa-sthāṁ nirgandha-puṣpavatīṁ kuṣmāṇḍa-latāṁ tan nikaṭa-stha-nīrasoṣara-sthala-padmāṁ ca tat-sahacarīṁ cañcalo'yam avidagdha- bhramaraḥ punaḥ punar uḍḍīyoḍḍiyānusarati tad etad ālambanāvidagdhya-rūpa- vairūpyeṇa vṛndāvana-maheśvarī-sahacarī madhurā ratir aprasannā satī etac chyāmala- rasaṁ na puṣṇāti \| tasmāt tathainam anabhijñaṁ yukta-mādhurībhiḥ prabodhaya \| yathā tām avidagdhāṁ rasa-gandha-śūnyāṁ sarvadaiva vihāyaināṁ vṛndāvana-mahā- devīṁ sarvato-bhāvenānusarati \| tarhi tat-priya-sakhī madhurā ratiḥ svayam eva paramojjvalā bhavantī etad abhīṣṭa-kāmān sampādayiṣyaty eva \|*  
*ity akhila-vilāsa-rasa-mādhurīm āpīya satyabhāmā vyājahāra -- prāṇanātha ! etad apūrva-rasa-sāgare nimajjantyā mama manāg api tṛpteḥ paryāptir na vidyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “The learning of the disciple is greater than that of the guru.”**  
**Lalitā then said with meaning, “Giving up the doe who has Raṅginī as her assistant, who plays in the waves of most intense rasa, who plays at the rāsa arena, who satisfies the lotus eyes of all people Gokula with the full taste of her beauty, the lusty, shrewd young kṛṣṇa-sāra deer, continually pursues a mirage assisted by Śaibya, resident of a place near Sakhī-sthalī. The fickle, inexperienced bee, giving up the friend of Campakalatā, a golden yuthikā flower, who spreads her fragrance in all directions in the yard of the bower at Rādhā’s lake, flies after the scentless pumpkin vine in the corner of the house of Govardhana Malla, and flies after the lotus Padmā, her assistant, located nearby on barren ground. Because of the deformity of this object of love in the form of foolishness, the assistant to the great queen of Vṛndāvana called sweet rati has become displeased and does not nourish mādhurya-rasa. Enlighten this fool by suitable types of sweetness so that he will give up the clumsy, scentless, juiceless pumpkin vine and pursue the queen of Vṛndāvana with complete passion. Then her friend mādhurya-rati will become most brilliant and fulfill his desires.”**  
**Hearing the sweet rasa of these pastimes, Satyabhāmā said, “O lord of my life! You have drowned me in an ocean of unprecedented rasa. I can never be fully satisfied.”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *-- priye ! etad gokula-vilāsānanda-niṣkuṭe viharamāṇasya mama tvat-praśna eva mat-priya-vayasya-samaya-rāja-vasantollāsa iti tām āliliṅgati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa said, “O beloved! I play in the forest of bliss composed of the pastimes of Gokula. With your question, the spring, king of seasons, who is my dear friend, makes its appearance.” He embraced her.**

***satyabhāmā*** *sānandaṁ -- tatas tataḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Satyabhāma said in joy, “Then what happened?”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *-- tataḥ subalaḥ sasmitam āha -- priyavayasya ! priya-sakhīyam indulekhā durdurūḍhāt sva-bhartur bhāsvarākhyād udvignā mām iṅgitena prerayati \| tad asyāḥ mauktika-mūlyaṁ nirṇīya tad dattvā jhaṭiti gṛha-kṛtyāya gantum ājñāpyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa continued to speak: Subala said with a smile, “Dear friend! Your dear friend Indulekhā, disturbed by the daily abusive words of her husband send me some hint. Fixing the price of her pearls, give them to her and send her home to do her household duties.”**

***tato’haṁ --*** *sakhe satyaṁ satyaṁ mayy atīvānurakteyaṁ mām api tyaktuṁ na śaknoti \| tato'pi suṣṭhu bibhetīty aham api jānāmi \| tad etan mauktika-mūlyaṁ śṛṇu -- yathārtha-nāmnīyaṁ mad-viraham asahamānā kāya-dvayaṁ kṛtvā śyāmalatara-mad- vakṣo-nabhasi śāṇita-nakhāgreṇa racitām ekāṁ sva-mūrtiṁ saṁrakṣatu \| aham apy asahyaitad-virahas tāvad asyāḥ vakṣoja-parvatopari sva-vidyā-balenārdha-candra- yugalībhavann udayaṁ karomi \| tadaiṣā labdha-mahā-ratnam iva sarvato māṁ celāñcalenāvṛṇotu \| rahasi ca kṣaṇe kṣaṇe sa-romāñcam avalokayantī paramānandam āsādayatu \| athavā mṛga-lāñchana-rahitāyā etasyā hṛn-madhye mañjula-kṛṣṇa- sāro'haṁ bhavāmīti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O friend! That is true. She is most attached to me and cannot give me up, though she is afraid of her husband. I know this. Listen to the price for the pearls. According to her name (crescent moon) Indulekhā, not able to tolerate separation from me, should produce two bodies and keep one of those forms, produced by her sharpened nails, on the dark sky of my chest. Unable to tolerate separation from her, I, becoming two half moons by the power of my mystic powers, will rise on the mountains of her breasts. Then let her cover me, the precious jewel that she has attained, with the edge of her cloth. Let her glance there secretly, moment after moment, with hairs standing on end, and taste the highest bliss. Or I can become the charming black deer on her heart, since she, as the moon, presently lacks the mark of the deer.**

*tataḥ sakampādharaṁ kuṭilaṁ mām avalokayantīm indulekhām ālokya smayamānā* ***tuṅgavidyā*** *prāha -- nāgara ! raṅgadevīyaṁ yathārtha-nāmnī tad asyā mauktika-paṇaḥ praṇiyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Indulekhā glanced at me with a frown and trembling lips. Tuṅgavidyā, smiling, said, “Lecher! Raṅgadevī (goddess of the dancing arena) is also appropriately named. Please tell the price of her pearls.”**

***tato’ham --*** *sakhi raṅgadevi ! rāsa-madhye tava lāsya-vilāsollāsaḥ santatam ālokito'sti \| tad adhunā nikuñja-mandirāṅgane rahasi tad-viśeṣam anubhavitum abhilaṣāmi \| tad ehi sva-vakṣoja-kanaka-kumbhau mad-urasi tathā nartaya yathāham ānanditaḥ sakala- ballavī-samudaya-sarvasva-bhūta-mat-svādhara-pīyūṣa-mahā-prasāda-dānena tvām ānandayāmīti \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“My friend Raṅgadevī! I constantly gazed at your enthusiastic dancing in the rāsa arena. I now desire that particular delight in the privacy of the bower. Therefore, come! Make the two pots of your golden breasts dance on my chest so that out of bliss I can give you bliss by giving the mahāprasāda nectar of my lips, which is the all of all the gopīs.”**

*tac chrutvā apaihi bhaṇḍa-śekhara ! apaihīty uktvā kuṭilaṁ mām avalokya viśākhā- pṛṣṭhāntaritāyāṁ raṅgadevyāṁ* ***sudevī*** *sa-smitam āha -- rasika-śekhara ! ballavī-kula-*  
*bhukta-mukha-sujuṣṭa-mahā-prasādāsvādaneneyaṁ tvaritam eva siddhim āsādayantī sphuṭam asmān api siddhārthāḥ kṛtavaty eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Raṅgadevī, glaring at me with a frown, said, “Go away, king of jesters! Go away!” She disappeared behind Viśākhā.**  
**Sudevī said with a smile, “O king of libertines! She will quickly taste perfection by tasting the gopīs’ mahāprasāda from your mouth which has already been enjoyed by all the gopīs. Clearly that will make us perfect also.”**

***subalaḥ --*** *vayasya ! sudevī sva-mauktika-mūlyaṁ śrotu-kāmā śrīmad-bhavan- mukham īkṣamāṇeyam utkaṇṭhate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “O friend! Sudevī desires to hear the price of her pearls. She is very eager, gazing at your beautiful face.”**

***tato’ham --*** *sakhe subala ! sudevīyam akṣa-keli-dakṣeti prasiddhā tac chapatham eva dadad asmi kenāpi kasyāpi pakṣo na grāhyaḥ \| āvām eva sva-buddhi-balena kheliṣyāvaḥ \| tatra yady aham anayā suṣṭhu jīyeyaṁ, tarhi vāmena vakṣojena mad- vakṣṣasi mām āpīḍya mama sarvasva-bhūtam adharaṁ dviḥ pibatu \| yadi vā mayedaṁ jīyate, tadā sva-dakṣiṇa-kaṭhina-vakṣojaṁ mad-dakṣiṇa-kareṇa nikāmaṁ pīḍayitvā dviḥ svādharāmṛtam āpāyayatv iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “My friend Subala! She is famous for her expertise in playing dice. I have made a vow that no one should take either side. We two alone will play using the strength of our intelligence. If I am defeated by her, she can press my chest with her left breast and drink twice my lips, the final goal of all. Or if she is defeated by me, I will press her hard right breast at liberty with my right hand and drink twice the nectar of her lips.”**

*tataḥ sā bhrū-bhaṅgena mām avalokayantī sāsūyaṁ viśākhām āha -- ayi viśākhe ! sarva-kālam eva narma-svarṇa-vikrayy asau mahājanaḥ \| sāmpratam eva muktā-maṇi- vyāpāram ārabhya tato vṛddhim alabhamānaḥ punaḥ sva-vṛttim evārabdhavān asti \| tataḥ parārdha-dvi-guṇa-pañcabāṇa-lalitāṁ kāñcana-mudrā-tatim itaḥ prakāma- mūlyena pragṛhya tayaiva muktāphalāny asmād ānayantu bhavatyaḥ \| mayā tu gṛhāya gamyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Staring at me with a frown, Sudevī spoke to Viśākhā with hatred. “O Viśākhā! This great man always sells gold and jokes. Starting with business in pearls and not getting profit from that, he now starts his own business (jokes). For an excessive price more than a pile of gold coins, endowed with two trillion of Cupid’s five arrows, all of you should have her take the pearls from him. But I am going home.”**

*iti calitum udyatāñcale gṛhītvānaṅga-mañjaryā nivartitā sa-krodham iva sākūtaṁ punar āha -- keli-lampaṭa ! nikāmam akṣa-keli-nipuṇeyam anaṅga-mañjarī tvayā saha dīvyantī te garva-parvataṁ kharvayiṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Anaṅga-mañjarī grabbed her cloth as she was about to go and prevented her. Again she spoke in anger emphatically. “O playful lecher! Anaṅga-mañjarī, who is sufficiently skillful at dice, can play with you and shatter the mountain of your pride.”**

*tatas tad-vacana-mādhuryam avadhārya* ***mayoktaṁ*** *-- asyās tv abalāyās tat-keliṣu kā śaktiḥ ? kintu mad-vakṣo-harid-grāva-haritālīya-rekhikāyā rādhikāyāḥ prāṇa- sahodareyaṁ mayy atīva snihyati \| mama ca mānasa-madhukaraḥ param asyām anavaratam anuvrajyati \| ataḥ samucita-paṇam antarāpi sutārāṇi suvṛttāni bahūni mauktikāny apy asyai vitariṣyāmi \| paraṁ ca nirjana-nikuñja-vedikāyāṁ smara- pañjarākṣarāṇy asyāḥ pañcāśad aṅga-pratyaṅgeṣu sva-hastena vinyasya svāṅgaṁ svāṅgenālinṅgya mantreṇaināṁ vyāpayya tathā siddha-mantram ekam upadiśāmi yathā tad-grahaṇa-mātrād anaṅgaṁ sāṅgaṁ viracayya santuṣṭena tena prasādīkṛta-sva- sarvasva-svarūpa-vilāsa-ratnāni mantra-gurau mayi dakṣiṇātvena sa-vinayam upaḍhaukayati \| tataḥ suprasannena mayā tādṛg-āśīḥ-śatam ādhāsyate yena viṣama- śara-vilāsācāryeti padavīm āsādya dvi-guṇaṁ mām eva bhaktyā paricarati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Taking note of her sweet words, I said, “What is the power in playing with this child? Anaṅga-mañjarī is the dear sister of Rādhā, who is a golden line on my emerald chest, and she has great affection for me. The bee of my mind also follows after her continuously. For a suitable price I will give her additionally many round, strung pearls. On an altar in a solitary bower, placing the fifty syllables encased in kāma-bīja on each of her limbs with my hand, embracing her limbs with mine, spreading her with mantras, I will teacher one perfect mantra, so that just by receiving it, she will develop love with all its parts. Out of satisfaction with the results, she should present me, her guru, with a dakṣiṇā of the gems of merciful pastimes which indicate her innate surrender. Pleased, I will bestow on her hundreds of blessings and the title “Ācārya of pastimes with unequalled arrows.” She will then serve me with twice as much devotion.”**

*iti mad-bhāṣita-kusuma-stavakaṁ śravaṇāvtaṁsīkṛtya sakampādharaṁ mām īṣad avalokayantī smita-subhaga-vadanābhir ābhiḥ snehenotphullatayā tad-agrajayā ca sa- smitaṁ sa-snehaṁ ca nirīkṣyamāṇā tulasī-raṅgaṇamālikayor upapṛṣṭham antarhitāsīt \|\|*

**Перевод:**
