**Hearing the flowers of my words, and making them her ear ornament, with trembling lips Anaṅga-mañjarī glanced at me momentarily. All the sakhīs looked at her smiling face and Rādhā looked at her with a beaming smile. She then disappeared behind Tulasī and Raṅganamālī.**

***satyabhāmā*** *smitvā -- prāṇanātha ! tatas tataḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Satyabhāmā said with a smile, “O lord of my life! Then what happened.”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ --*** *tadānīm eva mallī-bhṛṅgībhyām ānīya dattaṁ lekha-dvayam \| sarvābhiḥ samam eva vācayitvā paramotphullā lalitā tayor ekaṁ lekhaṁ subala-haste samarpitavatī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa continued to speak: Just then Mallī and Bhṛṅgī brought two letters and presented them. This caused everyone to begin speaking. Lalitā, full of joy, gave one of the letters to Subala.**

***tato’ham --*** *lalite ! kasyāyaṁ lekhaḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Lalitā! Who wrote this letter?”**

***lalitā*** *smitvā -- lekha eva kathayiṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā smiling said, “The letter will say.”**

*tataḥ subale lekham udghāṭya laghu laghu vācayati sati* ***nāndīmukhī --*** *subala ! sphuṭam eva vācaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**When Subala opened the letter and began softly reading it, Nāndīmukhī said, “O Subala! Speak clearly.”**

***subalaḥ*** *smitvā -- sakhe ! apūrva-patrīyam avadhāryatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala smiling said, “O friend! Listen to this remarkable letter.”**

***ahaṁ --*** *sakhe ! vācaya mamāpi mahatī śuśrūṣā vartate \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O friend! Please speak. I am also very eager to hear it.”**

***subalaḥ --*** *svasti samasta-saṁmukha-sarvopamā-yogya-bandha-prabandha-sad-guṇa- prakara-parivṛteṣu śrī-subalābhidhāna-priya-narma-sakha-mahānubhāveṣu yāvaṭābhidha-grāmato rādhāyāḥ praṇaya-bhara-pīyūṣa-paripūritākṣara-prakara- sumiṣṭa-piṣṭaka-pariveṣāṇa-hiraṇmaya-bhājana-rūpô'yaṁ svasti-mukham --*  
*bhavat-prāṇa-bandhu-pāda-padmānāṁ bhavādṛśāṁ ca maṅgala-kulam anavarata- virājamānam api vraja-maṇḍale śaśvan nitarāṁ virājatām \|\| kāṛyam idam anyad api avadhehi priya-jana-sakāśāt kara-grahaṇam anucitam iti bahutaram api kara-dravyam apahāyaiva etāvad-dinaṁ kim api noktam āsīt \| samprati mauktika-vyayena kṣubhyad- guru-jana-dīyamāna-mahānuyoga-nicayena kṣaṇam api nirvṛti-lavam alabhamānayā yat kiñcin mayā vijñāpyamānam asti \| tad-aguṇam avigaṇayya mad-vṛndāvana- janapade yamunopakaṇṭha-kedārikāyāṁ bhavat-prāṇa-bandhunā samprati kṣetrājīvatām āśrayatā kṛta-mauktika-kṛṣer lalitayā saha lekha-pratilekham ācārya samucita-kara-dravym iha tvaritam eva prahitya dīyatāṁ, yathā dravyeṇaiva mathurāto mauktikāny ānāyya gurubhyaḥ pradāya kadana-sāgarād asmād uttarāmaḥ \| athavā bahu-mūlya-mauktikotpatti-bhūmeḥ karasyāpy atibāhulyād etad-dravya- pradāne yadi bhavādṛśām aśaktiḥ syāt tarhi pañcabhir militvā samucita-mūlyaṁ nirṇīya etad-dravya-parivartanena mauktikani lalitā-haste deyānīti kiṁ bahunā paramābhijña-vareṣv iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “Greetings! This letter of Rādhā from Yāvaṭa village is a golden casket covering the sweet cake full of syllables of nectar and love, directed to the most respected priya-narma-sakha, honorable Subala, endowed continually with all good qualities which are completely suitable for all metaphors uttered by everyone present. May the continual presence of all auspiciousness concerning the lotus feet of your intimate friend and persons like you continue forever! Please attend to another duty also. Exhorting taxes from dear friends is not correct. Though he has taken away abundant taxes in the form of goods, till now we have said nothing about it. I have not obtained a moment’s peace because of the agitated elders’ intense scolding from the loss of the pearls. I have a request to make. Without us considered his bad conduct, your dear friend, who now makes a living from farming by cultivating pearls in a field near the Yamunā in the district of Vṛndāvana ruled by me, should make a written agreement with Lalitā, bring the suitable tax money here immediately, and give it, so that by that money we can take pearls from Mathurā and give them to our elders. In this way we will deliver ourselves from the ocean of suffering. Or if you are unable to give the amount, because of the excessive taxes on expensive land used for cultivating pearls, after meeting in a group of five to decide the amount, pearls can be given in lieu of money to Lalitā. What more to say to the most intelligent gentleman…..”**

*etan niśamya paramānanda-pūrṇenāpi* ***mayā*** *sākūtam uktaṁ -- ayi malli ! ayi bhṛṅgi ! bhavad-īśvarī sukhaṁ vartate ?*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, full of the highest bliss, I said with meaning, “O Mallī! O Bhṛṅgī! Is your highness well?”**

***mallī-bhṛṅgau --*** *śrīman-nātha-caraṇa-śubhānudhyānena \|*

**Перевод:**

**They said, “By auspicious meditation on the feet of the Lord.”**

*tatas tan-nikuñja-diśaṁ tiryag-avalokayann* ***ahaṁ --*** *kutra vartate sā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Glancing towards the bower, I said, “Where is she?”**

*te yāvaṭābhidha-grāme \|*

**Перевод:**

**They said, “She is in Yāvaṭa village.”**

*ahaṁ -- kiṁ kurvatī tiṣṭhati ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “What is she doing there?”**

*te lalitā-devī-prabhṛtīnāṁ vartma-diśam avalokayantī vṛndāvana-rājya-sārvabhauma- kāmam utkaṇṭhiteva virājamānā vartate \|*

**Перевод:**

**They said, “Anxious for love in the kingdom of Vṛndāvana, she is looking at the path by which Lalitā and others will come.”**

***tato’haṁ --*** *sakhe subala ! amuyā lalitayaiva kasyacid dvārā lekhayitvā lekho'yaṁ vitīrṇo'sti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O my friend Subala! Lalitā has had someone write this letter and deliver it.”**

***subalaḥ --*** *nahi nahi \| rādhāyā eva sva-hasta-likhita-varṇānāṁ vinyāso'yam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “No, no! It is in Rādhā’s own handwriting.”**

***tato’ham --*** *sakhe ! tat tāvad idaṁ darśayeti tad-dhastād ākṛṣya lekham ālokya sa- camatkāram ātmagataṁ -- aho akṣarāṇāṁ paṅkti-vistṛtir iyaṁ man-netrayoḥ pīyūṣa- vartikeva pratibhātīty ānanda-janita-pulakān vismaya-sañcāri-bhāvenaiva kṛtān iti bhaṅgyā vikhyāpya prakāśaṁ vihasya āścaryam āścaryam ākāśa-kusumam iva dhūrtābhir etābhiḥ kim apy anyad akasmād eva samutthāpitam \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O friend! Show it to me!” Taking it from his hand, I looked at the letter and became astonished. I said to myself, “The lines of letters shine like strokes of nectar for my eyes. They make my hairs stand on end as a vyabhicārī-bhāva.” Then sensing a trick, audibly I laughed. “Suddenly the devious gopīs have raised this most amazing topic like a flower from the sky.”**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ --*** *vayasya ! vikaṭa-kuṭināṭi-kapaṭa-nāṭikā-tāṇḍavodbhaṭa-naṭācārya- mahā-naṭīm imāṁ tad advitīyāṁ durlalitāṁ lalitāṁ vāg-vilāsābhāsa-mātreṇaiva parājitya avatiṣṭhate dhriyate yāvad eko'pi ripus tāvat kutaḥ sukham ity ādi saṁsmṛtya sarvāsām asmad-vṛndāvana-rājya-kāṅkṣiṇīnāṁ dhūrtānāṁ tatim imām ito vṛndāvanāt jhaṭiti vidrāvaya \| tato niṣkaṇṭaka-rājye madhurāṇi rasavanti phalāny upabhujya sukhenaivāhaṁ nṛtyann iva vasāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “O friend! Ill-mannered Lalitā is a great dancer, the ācārya for bold performances and dancing dramas with horrid, crooked deception, and is non-different from Rādhā. Defeat her by a false play on words. As long as one enemy remains, how can you be happy? Remembering this, immediately drive this group of devious girls who desire the kingdom of our Vṛndāvana from Vṛndāvana. Enjoying juicy, sweet fruits in the thornless kingdom, I can live and dance happily.”**

*tato* ***mayoktaṁ --*** *lalite ! iyam asmad-vṛndāṭavī \| bhavat-sakhyā rājyaṁ kuto jātam \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Lalitā! This is our Vṛndāvana. How did it become the property of your friend?”**

***lalitā --*** *parama-kapaṭin ! tad-rājya-mahābhiṣeka-mahotsavaṁ samakṣam ākalayyāpi kim evaṁ gopāyasi ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O supreme deceiver! By displaying her coronation as queen of the land, can you hide it?”**

***tato’ham --*** *lalite ! samakṣa-darśanaṁ tāvad-dūre 'stu tac-chravaṇam api jātam astīti nāhaṁ smarāmi ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Lalitā! Let us not speak of directly seeing it. I do not even remember hearing of it.”**

***viśākhā --****lalite ! asya tāvad dūṣaṇaṁ nāsti \| ballava-sādhvī-śata-dharmollaṅghana- janita-tamaḥ-pracayenāntarhitam asty asya cetas tat-saṁsargī ca tādṛśa iti etad- vayasyayor anayor api \| tat katham ete smarantu nāma ? tasmāt tat kathana-pīyūṣa- prasyandena karṇa-vivareṣu praveśitena bāḍham amīṣāṁ cetāṁsi kāruṇyenāvirbhāvaya yathaiṣāṁ smṛtiḥ sampadyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “O Lalitā! He is not completely at fault. His awareness has disappeared because of the intense darkness arising from transgressing the dharma of hundreds of cowherd girls. His associates are the same. Thus his two friends are without memory also. How can they remember again? Compassionately revive their consciousness by having them hear the nectar of that description. Then they will remember.”**

*tatas tadānīm evāgatāṁ sarvābhir evānanditāṁ vṛndām āliṅgya* ***lalitā*** *sa-smitam āha -- vṛnde ! bhadrāvasare tvaṁ prāptāsi \| tat tvayaiva nirvarṇyatāṁ mahotsava-raṅgaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**At that moment Vṛndā arrived to give joy to everyone. Lalitā embraced her and with a smile said, “O Vṛndā! You have come at the right time. You should describe the arena of the great festival.”**

***vṛndā*** *sa-vinayānandaṁ -- sakhi lalite ! tvan-mukha-padmād viniṣyandamāna-tan- mahotsava-rasa-makarandam aham apy āpātum āgatāsmi tat tvayaiva pariveṣyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā spoke with joy and humility. “O my friend Lalitā! I have come to drink the sweet juice of that great festival flowering from your lotus mouth. You should describe it.”**

***lalitā*** *sānandam -- bhadraṁ sarvair avadhāya śrūyatām \| ṛtu-pati-vasanta-samaye paurṇamāsyāṁ viśākhā-nakṣatre bhagavato hiraṇyagarbhasya gagana-vāṇyā vṛndāvana-vāsino bhagavataḥ śrī-gopīśvarasya svapna-kṛta-nideśena ca mānasa- suradhunī-kālindyādi-sarid-varobhiḥ sāvitrī-saṁjñaikānaṁśā-prabhṛti-devībhiś ca samaṁ muralī-mahatī-jharjharī-muraja-dundubhi-prabhṛti-vādya-śabda- kolāhalenākṛṣṭeṣu kinnarī-gandharvī-kuleṣu sānandaṁ gāyatsu \| apsaro-vidyādharī- vargeṣu nṛtyatsu śacyādi-surāṅganā-nicayeṣu jaya-jaya-śabda-pūrvakaṁ pārijātādi- kusuma-vṛṣṭiṁ kurvatsu viśākhādi-sahacarī-samudayeṣu parimilita-paṭa-vāsa-surāga-*  
*bandhana-nava-ghanasāra-kṣoda-mañjula-mṛgamada-sulalita-kuṅkuma-paṭīra-drava- vividha-surabhi-kusuma-kulāni sulalita-gāna-puraḥsaram itas tato vikiratsu bhūsura- ramaṇī-vṛndeṣu śubhāśīḥ-śatāni vitaratsu ca sakhī-vṛnda-samarpyamāṇa-śobhana- kuṅkumāguru-karpūra-candana-vividha-surabhi-kusumādi-suvāsita-jala-paripūrita- kāñcanāṣṭottara-śata-ghaṭaiḥ suvarṇa-vedikā-nihita-padmarāga-maṇi-pīṭhopari sanniviṣṭāṁ priya-sakhīṁ gāndarvāṁ bhagavatī paurṇamāsī sānandam abhiṣicya vividha-sulakṣaṇa-mahāratna-sañcayodaka-sahasra-dhārābhir jaya-jaya-śabda- pūrvakaṁ ratnābhiṣekaṁ cakāra \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā spoke with joy. “Everyone listen with attention. In the spring, on the full moon, during Viśākhā constellation, on the order of Lord Brahmā heard in the sky and by the dream of Gopīśvara, the lord who resides in Vṛndāvana, the Kinnarīs and Gandharvīs sang in joy, attracted by the uproar of flutes, tambourines, and various drums, along with Mānasa-gaṅgā and Yamunā as well as Sāvitrī, Saṁjñā Ekānaṁśa and other devīs. Apsarās and Vidyādharīs danced and Śacī and other women showered pārijāta flowers while shouting “Jaya! Jaya!” Viśākhā and other sakhīs sprinkled here and there various fragrant flowers, sandalwood paste, delightful kuṅkuma, pleasing musk, and fresh camphor powder bound with mixtures of colorful cloth, with attractive singing preceding them. Heavenly women uttered hundreds of blessings. Joyful Paurṇamāsī bathed our dear friend Rādhā, who was seated on a ruby throne on a golden dais, using a hundred and eight golden pots filled with scented water, various fragrant flowers, sandalwood, camphor, guru and brilliant kuṅkuma, offered by the sakhīs. She performed the bath using gems by holding up a sahasradhara filled with water and precious various attractive jewels, accompanied by shouts of “Jaya Jaya.”**

***vṛndā*** *sa-harṣam -- tatas tataḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “Then what happened?”**

*lalitā tato mayaiva sucīna-vasanena śanaiḥ śanair aṅgāny āmṛjya surakta-dukūlaṁ paridhāpya ketakī-kusuma-suvāsita-jalada-nīlottarīya-vasanenāvaguṇṭhya sukham anyatara kanaka-pīṭhe niveśya nānāvidha-kusuma-nava-guñjā-puñja-sumadhura- muktādi-maṇi-nikara nindita-śikhi-piñcha-kalāpa-kānti-sukeśa-pāśaṁ sudīrgha- prāntocchalat-kusuma-gucchaṁ veṇītvenāvirbhāvya sva-sva-sevā-karma-karmaṭha- parijana-gaṇa-dīyamāna-vividhālepa-bhūṣaṇa-mālyādibhir yathā-sthānaṁ yathā- śobhaṁ nikāmam alaṅkṛtety ardhokte tat-smaraṇānanda-janita-kampa-pulaka-svara- bhaṅga-parimilita-divya-moham āsādayantī sā lalitā viśākhayā sasambhramaṁ pṛṣṭhe samālambya karṇe rādhe kṛṣṇa rādhe kṛṣṇeti kīrtanāmṛtena sandhukṣitā kṣaṇaṁ maunena dhairyam ālambya punaḥ kathayitum ārebhe \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “I then wiped her gently with a fine cloth, dressed her in bright red cloth and covered her top portion with a blue cloth perfumed with ketakī flowers. I had her sit on a golden throne in another place and braided her beautiful hair, more exquisite than a bunch of peacock feathers, intertwining it with various flowers, new guñja berries and attractive pearls and other gems, with a flower cluster at the tip. She was decorated with various garlands, ornaments and cosmetics supplied by her associates busily engaged in their services.”**  
**Without completing the description, Lalitā experienced divya-moha mixed with trembling, hairs standing on end and breaking of the voice because of her bliss on remembering the event. Viśākhā supported her from behind with attention and revived her by chanting sweetly in her ear “Rādhe, Kṛṣṇa, Rādhe, Kṛṣṇa!” Lalitā, after a moment’s silence, composed herself and continued to speak.**

*tadānīm eva mūrcchitāṁ lalitām ālakṣya -- hā mat-prāṇa-pradīpa-rājinīrājita-carite priya-sakhi lalite ! hata-bhāgyāhaṁ tvayā nirāśā kṛtāsmi \| hā bhagavan ! bhakta-vatsala! bhāskara-deva ! rakṣa rakṣa \| hā santatam āpulinda-sakala-gokula-janāvanārtha- kalita-nirvikalpa-mahā-saṅkalpa-gokula-sudhānidhe jhaṭiti nija-sudhāmaya- karābhimarṣaṇena mad-vidha-jīvita-kokila-kula-jīvātu-lalitā-nāmādbhuta-pīyūṣa- rasāla-vallīṁ jīvitām ācārya kilaitat-paṇenaiva saṁkrīya dāsī krityatām iyaṁ tapasvinī rādhiketi vilapya sāśru-dhāraṁ vegena tām āliṅgitum āgacchantī rādhikā rasamaya- sāttvika-mahā-stambha-santati-sahacaryā suṣṭhu pariṣvajya rakṣitāsīt \|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing Lalitā faint, Rādhā said, “Ah! O my dear friend Lalitā, the lamp of my life in the night, with unconquerable character! What misfortune! You have left me without hope. O Lord, affectionate to your devotees! O lord of light! Save me, save me! O ocean of nectar in Gokula, who have made the great unwavering vow to protect all the people of Gokula, even the Pulindas! Bring to life the mango creeper of nectar named Lalitā who gives life to the group of cuckoos who give life to me by touching her with your hand of nectar. Arranging the payment, make me your servant.” Weeping, Rādhā quickly went to embrace Lalitā. But she was then tightly embraced by her assistant--the sāttvika- bhāva of intense, continuous paralysis filled with rasa.**

*ity avakalanāt trāsena sāntaḥkampaṁ raṅgaṇavallī-tulasībhyāṁ tat-savidham upalabdhaṁ tato raṅgaṇavallyā vāma-bhujayā tat-pṛṣṭham avaṣṭabhya dakṣiṇa-kareṇa mṛdu mṛdu mṛjyamānā hā nātha rakṣa rakṣa iti sāśru-pravāhaṁ sa-gadgada-bhāṣitena tulasyā tu nava-mṛdula-tamāla-pallava-kula-vyajanenātijavena bījyamānā bāhyam upālabhya susthām iva lalitām ālokayantī sānandā babhūva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing this, in fright, Raṅganavallī and Tulasī approached Rādhā with trembling hearts. Raṅganavallī supported her with her left arm and with her right hand softly wiped her body. Tulasī with choked voice and tears exclaimed “Save her, save her!” and fanned her with soft, fresh tamāla buds. She came to consciousness and in joy saw that Lalitā had recovered.**

***lalitā --*** *huṁ ? tāṁ tadā tadānandocchalita-sāttvika-bhāvālaṅkāra-bhūṣita-sabhya- gaṇaiḥ saha bhagavatī vicitra-ratna-siṁhāsanopari samupaveśya ekānaṁśā-sodara- kāmākhyā-śyāmala-devatā-hṛdya-sthasakād āhṛta-datta-mṛgamadena vṛndāvana-rājya- mahā-rājñītvena śaṅkha-ghaṇṭā-dundubhi-kolāhala-śabda-pūrvakaṁ jaya-jaya- śabdena tilakaṁ cakāra \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Ah! With all the assembly, decorated with ornaments of sāttvika-bhāvas arising from bliss, Paurṇamāsī seated Rādhā on a throne of colorful gems. She applied tilaka, making her the queen of the kingdom of Vṛndāvana, using musk taken from the perfumed mark on the bosom of the dark god called Love, brother of Ekānaṁśā, while conches, bells, and drums resounded.”**

*tata etac-chravaṇena sarvāsām ānanda-hāsa-kolāhale jāte tac-chravaṇānanda- samutthita-sāttvikādy-anubhāvān yatnenāvṛtya kiñcid vihasya* ***mayoktaṁ --*** *lalite ! etat kathaṁ mayā na jñāyate etena kiṁ vo rājyam āyātam ? pratyuta etad uṭṭaṅkanāt sva- mukhenaiva bhavatībhir yuṣmat-sahitam idaṁ rājyaṁ mamaiveti nirṇītam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this everyone loudly laughed in bliss. Covering my sāttvika-bhāvas which arose from bliss on hearing this, I laughed a little and said, “O Lalitā! Why do I not know this? By this, does kingdom belong to you? By this description,by your own words, you have concluded that the kingdom is mine as well as yours.”**

***nāndīmukhī --*** *katham iva ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “How is that?**

***tato’ham --*** *yato vṛndāvana-purandarasya mamaiva rājñītvena mad-iṅgitenaiva bhagavaty-abhiṣikteyam \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Because she was made queen by Paurṇamāsī by the suggestion of me, king of Vṛndāvana.”**

***viśākhā*** *vihasya -- asaṅgata-bhāṣin ! purandarasya mahiṣi devy eva bhavati \| sā khalu śacīti prasiddhā svarge vasati \| iyaṁ tu mama sakhī bhūmi-vihāriṇī subhagābhimanyor jāyā mānuṣī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā laughed and said, “You speak nonsense. The queen of Indra (purandara) is Śacī. She is famous and lives in Svarga. My friend lives on earth. She is human and is the wife of fortunate Abhimanyu.”**

***mayoktaṁ --*** *tarkācārya-śiromaṇiṁ-manye viśākhe ! tvaṁ suṣṭhu jaḍāsi yad bāraṁ bāram adhītam api pratyakṣa-khaṇḍaṁ tvayā vismṛtam eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Viśākhā, you think you are the best of arguers. But you are dull headed. Have you forgotten the volumes of scripture dealing with sense perception which you studied in detail?”**

***viśākhā --*** *kiṁ tad vismṛtam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “What did I forget?”**

***mayoktaṁ --*** *śrūyatām \| yadi bhavat-sahacarī mat-preyasī na syāt tarhi mad-vakṣaḥ- sthāsakāhṛta-mṛgamadena kathaṁ bhagavatī tāṁ tilakinīṁ cakāra ? kathaṁ vā mat- kaṇṭha-mālā-hāra-vaijayantībhyāṁ tat-kaṇṭham alañcakāra ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Please listen. If your companion is not my beloved, then why did Paurṇamāsī anoint her with tilaka of musk taken from the mark on my chest? Why did she decorate Rādhā’s throat with my necklace and vaijayantī garland?”**

***lalitā --*** *bhoḥ śaśa-śṛṅga-dhanurdharālīka-purandara ! pravara-subhaga-lekhāvali- kalita-padāravindāyāḥ gandharva-vidyādhara-gaṇa-gīyamāna-mahā-vaibhavāyāḥ ātmabhuvāpi saṁstūyamāna-caritāyāḥ vividha-kāma-sampatti-dāyinyāḥ nandy- ādīśvara-gṛhiṇyāḥ kāmāripura-vāsinyāḥ vindhya-vāstavyaikānaṁśāgrajāyāḥ kāmākhyā- nāma-śyāmala-devatāyāḥ mahāprasāda-mṛgamada-mālābhir eva sā kila bhagavatyā vibhūṣitā \| tava kas tatra sambandhaḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O fake king holding a bow made from a rabbit’s horn! She was decorated by Paurṇamāsī with mahāprasāda garlands and musk of the dark goddess called Kāmākhyā, the elder sister of Ekānaṁśā who lives in the Vindhya Mountains. She lives in Kāmāripura. She is the wife of Śiva, and gives various benedictions including wealth. She is praised even by Brahmā, and has great glories praised by Gandharvas and Vidyādharas. Her lotus feet are marked with the most auspicious signs. What is your relation to her?”**

***tuṅgavidyā --*** *sakhi lalite ! sādhu sambodhitaṁ yad ayam alīka-purandara eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “My friend Lalitā! You have nicely said that he is the king of liars or a false Indra.”**

***viśākhā --*** *katham iva ?*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “How is that?”**

***tuṅgavidyā --*** *śrūyatāṁ asmin māthura-pradeśe yācaka-dvija-kalāvadādibhir eva pañcaviṁśati-kaparrdikā-mātra-labdhaye deva ! mahārājety-alīka-sambodhanaiḥ kṣudrataraika-grāmādhyakṣo'pi yathā praphullito bhavati tathaiva śakrāśanāśana- durgata-bhaṇḍa-bhaṭṭādi-vargaiḥ palaika-parimita-navanīta-mātram āptuṁ vṛndāvana- purandareti kṛtāsambhavālīka-sambodhana-pūrvaka-stavābhāsenaiva nisargāgambhīra eṣa kṛṣībalo bāḍham ātmanaḥ purnadaratā-mananena nija-jālmato'marāvatī- purandaratvam eva prakaṭīcakāra \|*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “Please listen. In the district of Mathurā, begging brāhmaṇas or musicians, in order to obtain twenty-five coins, can become puffed up with titles like Deva and Mahārāja and assume positions as low village leaders. Similarly, a farmer’s son of unclear birth, attaining false praise through false, impossible titles like King of Vṛndāvana, given by buffoons and hired praisers who cannot reach Indra’s throne, in order to obtain one pot of butter, manifests himself as king of heaven by his contemptuous acts, thinking himself a king.”**

*tatas* ***tuṅgavidyā*** *narma-smitam apavārya -- bhoḥ pāvana-sarovara-jambāla-jāta- jāmbunada-jāta-sutāra-muktāphalādi-vividha-ratna-vrāta-viracita-mahā-siṁhāsanopari suṣṭhu niviṣṭasya saurabha-bharonmādīkṛtāruṇa-bhramara-kulocchalita-jhaṅkāra- rava-nikareṇānugamyamāna-nirmala-gagana-sumanaḥ-prapañcāvirbhāvita-vara- mukuṭa-bandhātibandhūrottamāṅgasya sānandaṁ śirasi subalena dhṛta-pariṇata- kamaṭha-kaṭhora-pṛṣṭha-supakṣa-kṛta-prakaṭa-surabhi-sudha-kaṇābhivarṣi-*  
*pravarāṭapatrasya masṛṇa-tara-karatalodbhūta-tanu-rūha-prakara-racita-cāmara- dvayenojjvala-caturābhyām ubhaya-pārśvayor ānandenābhivījyamānasya, atisupratiṣṭhita-bandhyā-garbha-jāta-mahā-mahā-sat-puruṣa-vargaiḥ padma- gandhābhidhādivalīvardha-sañcayānāṁ sumadhura-payaḥ-pravāheṇa vṛndāṭavī- mahendratve’bhiṣiktasya, pariṇata-saśottuṅga-śṛṅga-vinirmita-mañjula- kārmukālaṅkṛta-vāma-kara-muṣṭikasyāsya, tad avadhi yaśaḥ-pratāpa-prakāśa-laharī brahmāṇḍa-bhare yādṛśī prasarantī vartate \| tām anubhavantībhir bhavatībhir api sākṣān mahendratvaṁ yan na manyate, tad bhavatīnām anayo’yaṁ mahān eveti mayi prabhāsate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hiding a smile, Tuṅgavidyā said, “He sat comfortably on a huge throne made of various gems, bright pearls and gold arising from the jambu fruits at Pāvana-sarovara. He wore an excellent crown on his attractive head covered with profuse sky flowers pursued by swarms of buzzing red bees, maddened by the heavy scent. Subala joyfully held an excellent umbrella made of hard tortoise shell over his head which sprinkled drops of fragrant nectar. On his sides, Ujjvala and Catura blissfully fanned him with two cāmaras made of body hairs which grew from soft hands. He was bathed in streams of sweet milk from bulls with names like Padma-gandha, to make him king of Vṛndāvana by groups of great, pure men born from high-class barren women. In his left hand he held a bow made from rabbit’s horn. Waves of his glory spread throughout the universe. To me, it is a great offence that you do not respect him as Indra directly, though you know this.”**

*iti niśamya sa-smitaṁ nayana-kūṇanena sa-smita-lajjita-man-mukham ālokya parasparaṁ cālokayantīṣu tāsu* ***citrā*** *vihasya – bhoḥ katham asau parihasyate bhavatībhyām ? satyam evāyaṁ devendraḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**When everyone looked at my bashful, smiling face with smiles and squinting eyes, and then looked at each other, Citrā said with a laugh, “Oh! Why do you two laugh at him? Truly he is the king of the devatās.”**

***tuṅgavidyā*** *– citre ! iti cet sa katham atrāgataḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Tuṅgavidyā said, “O Citrā! If that is so, why did he come here?”**

***citrā*** *– śrūyatām \| para-ramaṇīto’yam iti sarvataḥ samadhigamya krodhinyā devā-padāghātena nirbhartsitas tāṁ parama-sukhada-tan-nija-bhavanaṁ ca nirvedataḥ parityajya vanam idam āgatya parama-mañjula-navīna-gopatvam ivāsādya puraścaraṇa-vidhānenaiva vṛndāvaneśvaryāḥ karṣako bhūtvā sukhena samayaṁ gamayann asti \| tad enaṁ hāsya-rasa-viṣayālambanam avidhāya vāṭika-rūpa-prāghuṇe’ smin sneha eva nikāmaṁ vidhīyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Citrā said, “Please listen. Confirming that he was enjoying with other women, his angry wife rebuked him with a kick. Out of remorse he gave up his house filled with the highest happiness and came to this forest. Taking the form of an attractive cowherd boy, by his atonements, he became the farmer of the queen of Vṛndāvana. He happily passes his time here. Not making him the object of laughter, we should show this vaiśya guest affection.”**

*tac chrutvā sarvāsu smera-mukhīṣu* ***nāndīmukhī*** *vihasya – sakhi citre ! vraja eva nitya-vihāriṇi vrajendra-nandane yat kiñcit tvayedaṁ vyāhṛtam \| tasya śabdārthottha- gūḍhābhiprāyeṇa bhavitavyam iti lakṣyate \| tatas tasya vivecana-pūrvaka- kathanenāsmān baḍham ānandaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone smiled on hearing this. Nāndīmukhī said with a laugh, “O my friend Citrā! You have explained about the son of Nanda who always plays in Vraja. There seems to be some deeper meaning to the words. Give us pleasure by explaining this.”**

*tataḥ smitvā muni-vratam ālambitavatyāṁ citrāyāṁ vṛndā sānandam āha – nāndīmukhi ! asyāḥ parama-vidagdhāyā gūḍhābhiprāyaḥ sphuṭaṁ mayaiva nirvarṇyamānaḥ suṣṭhu samākarṇyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Smiling, Citrā remained silent. Vṛndā then spoke joyfully. “Nāndīmukhī! Listen carefully as I explain the deep meaning of her most clever words.”**

***nāndīmukhī****n – katham asya devendratvam ? tat prakaṭaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “How did he become king of the devatās?”**

***vṛndā –*** *dīvyanti krīḍantīti devāḥ – vicitra-vividha-manohara-keli-vilāsa-śālinaḥ \| tathā dīvyanti viśeṣeṇa dyotante iti devāḥ \| paramojjvala-tejas-taraṅga-hṛdyādbhuta- saundaryāmṛta-pravāha-śālinas teṣāṁ teṣām apīndro mahā-parivṛḍha iti devendras tebhyo’pi paramotkarṣeṇa suṣṭhu virājamāna ity arthaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “The word** ***devā*** **means ‘those who play and amuse themselves.’ They have an inclination to various attractive and astonishing amusements. Also** ***devā*** **means ‘those who shine excellently.’ They possess sweet streams of astonishing beauty and charm with waves of the most brilliant light. The king of such persons, who has these qualities to greater degree, is called “king of the devas.” He exceeds the others in excellence.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *sa-smitaṁ – samīcīno’yam artho vivṛtaḥ kintu para-ramaṇī-rata ity asya ko’rthaḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said with a smile, “That is the correct explanation of the meaning. But what is the meaning of “para-ramaṇi-rata (engaged in enjoying other women).”**

***vṛndā*** *– parā anyā tathā parā vipakṣā, tathā parā paramotkṛṣṭā, parā cāsau ramaṇī ceti para-ramaṇī śrī-rādhikā tasyāṁ rataḥ paramāśaktas tām eva paramānurāgeṇa ramayann ity arthaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “*****Para*** **means ‘other’ or ‘opponent.” It also means ‘most excellent.’** ***Para-ramaṇī*** **means ‘the most excellent woman, Rādhā.’**  ***Para-ramaṇī-rata*** **means ‘the person extremely attached to the most excellent woman, he who gives enjoyment to her with the most attachment.’”**

***campakalatā*** *smitvā – vṛnde! devy api nirūpyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Campakalatā said smiling, “O Vṛndā! Explain the word** ***devī*****.”**

***vṛndā*** *– na devo devam arcayed iti caṇḍikā-paricaryāparatvāt asya devasya bhāryeti vā \| kiṁ vā amaṅgale maṅgala-śabdavad iyaṁ devī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “Another text concerning worship of Caṇḍikā says “A person who is not a** ***deva*** **cannot worship a** ***deva*****.” Or** ***devī*** **refers to the wife of the** ***deva*** **or it refers to a person who is not like a deva, just as the word auspicious can be used to refer to an inauspicious situation.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– seyaṁ kā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Who is this person?”**

***viśākhā*** *– etādṛśī candāvaly eva bhaviṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “This should refer to Candrāvalī.”**

*vṛndā smitvā maunam ālambate \| sarvāḥ smitaṁ kurvanti \|* ***nāndīmukhī*** *padāghāteneti dhārṣṭyātiśayena tasyā anuttamatā sphuṭaiva kintu parama-sukhada-tan-nija- bhavanaṁ vā katarat ?*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā smiled by remained silent. Everyone smiled. Nāndīmukhī said, “By the audacious act of kicking him she (devī) clearly showed her low character. But why mention** ***parama-sukhada-tan-nija-bhavanam*** **(that his house was filled with the highest happiness)?”**

***vṛndā*** *– niviḍatva-puṣpavattva-bhṛṅga-guñjitvādi-rāhitvāt param asukhadam \| kiṁ vā parama-sukhaṁ dārayatīti parama-sukhadaṁ yat tasyā devyā nija-bhavanaṁ sakhīsthalī-nikaṭa-vartinaṁ tad apahāya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “It was completely without happiness (*****param asukhadam*****) since it was devoid of humming bees and thick growth of flowers. Or it destroyed (*****dārayati)*** **the highest happiness. He gives up the house of this woman who lives near Sakhī-sthalī.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– aho ! śabdānāṁ gūḍhārtha-vijñāyās tava vyākhyā-kauśalaṁ tad asmādṛśībhir duravagāho navīna-gopatvādi-padānāṁ gūḍho’rthaḥ kṛpayā prakāśyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Ah! You really know the secret meaning of words. Your explanation has been skillful. Please reveal the deep meaning of the words like** ***navīna-gopatvā*****, which are hard for us to understand.”**

***vṛndā*** *– veṇu-viṣāṇa-laguḍa-niryoga-pāśa-giri-dhātu-citra-nava-śikhaṇḍa- guñjāhārāraṇya-śṛṅgāra-dhāritvaṁ mañjula-navīna-gopatvaṁ tatrāpi navīna-śabdena tasya prathamato nitya-nūtanatā ca dhvanitā \| puraścaraṇa-saṁvidhāneneti puraḥ saṁmukhe bhṛṅga-raṇita-kusuma-kalita-bakula-rāja-tale caraṇaṁ proddāma- madonmatta-gajendravad-vividha-vilāsa-samullāsitam itas tato bhramaṇaṁ tat-puraḥ- saraṁ yat saṁvidhānaṁ līlā-kamala-cumbana-kaṅkelli-nava-pallava-daṁśena nistula-nistala-dāḍimī-phala-kara-vinyāsa-kāñcan-yūthikā-samāliṅgana-pūrvaka- smita-nava-karpūra-saṁmilita-cañcala-nayana-kamalāñcalāvalokana-paramonmādaka- madhura-mādhvīka-pāyanam \| tena malli-bakula-campaka-mādhavī-kanaka-yūthikādi-kusuma-cayana-vilāsa-mādhurī-bharam anubhavantyāḥ śrī-vṛndāvana-rājadhānī-vilāsinyāḥ karṣaka ākarṣako bhūtvā tena tām unmādya nija-nikaṭam ākṛṣyety arthaḥ \|sukhena ślāghya-madhura-madhura-rasāsvādana-janita-paramānanda-sandohena samayaṁ sahaja-sarvadojjṛmbhamāṇa-vasanta-kālaṁ nirantaraṁ nirupama-vilāsa- mādhurībhiḥ saubhāgya-lakṣmī-bharaṁ gamayan prāpayann asti tayā sahānavaratam anirvacanīya-madhura-khelā-vilāsa-tat-paraḥ san sadā virājata eveti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “He is the attractive cowherd (*****gopatva*****) decorated with fresh guñja berries and forest ornaments, peacock feathers, mountain minerals, flute, horn, stick and ropes for tying the cows. The word** ***navīna*** **means that he is ever fresh.** ***Puraścaraṇa-saṁvidhānam*** **means he wanders about (*****caraṇa*****), performing various pastimes like an impassioned king of elephants at the base (*****puraḥ*****) of a flowering bakula tree buzzing with bees.** ***Vidhānam*** **(arrangement) means drinking sweet, highly intoxicating wine in the form of glancing from the corner his fickle lotus eyes mixed with the fresh camphor of his smile, while embracing golden yūthika trees and placing his hands on bulky, bumpy pomegranate fruit, while biting new aśoka sprouts and kissing play lotuses. He becomes the attractor** ***(karṣakah*****) of the woman who plays in the royal city of Vṛndāvana and experiences sweet bliss in plucking jasmine, bakula, campaka, mādhavī, and golden yūthika flowers. He makes her insane and attracts her to his side.”**  
***Sukhena samayam gamayan*** **means he bestows (*****gamayan*****) the ultimate, auspicious Lakṣmī with incomparable sweetness of pastimes, with the highest bliss (*****sukhena*****) arising from tasting the praiseworthy sweetest rāsa, in the natural, eternal spring season, at all times (*****samayam*****). He is continually absorbed in inconceivable, sweet pastimes with her.”**

***tato’haṁ*** *sāntar-ānandaṁ-viśākhā-ghātini vṛnde mama vṛndāvanodyāna-pālikāpi tvaṁ katham etāsu militāsi ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Vṛndā, who gives inner bliss to Viśākhā! You are my guardian for the garden of Vṛndāvana. Why do you mingle with these women?”**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *– priya-vayasya ! iyam udyāna-pālī sa-lavaṇa-takra-ślathita-bhakta- bhakṣaṇāya tad-udyāna-pālanaṁ parityajya sāmpratam āsāṁ gṛha-pālī vṛttāsti \| tat kathaṁ na bukkiṣyate ?*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “Dear friend! She takes care of the garden but has given up taking care of the garden to eat rice mixed with salty yogurt. She now works as the gopīs’ guardian in their houses. Will she not speak?”**

***vṛndā*** *– aye bhūsurābhāsa ! kaṭubaṭo ! nija-sahacara-prathama-mudira-vacana- jaladhārā-varṣeṇa praphulla-varṣābhūs tvaṁ santata-śravaṇa-kaṭu-śabdaṁ kurvan sarvān udvejayann asi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “O tarnished brāhmaṇa! Envious boy! The land was joyous when the shower from the clouds made your friend speak like a frog. You strike fear in everyone when they hear your sharp words.”**

***mallī-bhṛṅgyau*** *– devi lalite ! svāminyā yad anya-patreṇa likhitam asti \| tat kiṁ vismṛtaṁ bhavatyā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Mallī and Bhṛṅgī said, “O Lalitā! Have you forgotten that there is another letter written by our mistress?”**

***lalitā*** *– kiṁ tat smāryatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Let it be remembered.”**

***mallī-bhṛṅgyau*** *– samucita-kara-dāne yaḥ kuyuktim uttolya virodham ācarati so’tra baddhvā śīghraṁ praheya iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Mallī and Bhṛṅgī said, “Whoever opposed paying the prescribed tax by arguing illogically should be tied up and quickly sent here.”**

***lalitā*** *– āṁ \| tat-kara-dāna-virodhī madhumaṅgala eva \| tad enaṁ latā-pāśena dṛḍhaṁ nibadhya komalāyāḥ priya-sakhyāḥ savidham ānītvaiva jaṭilābhimanyu-pārśve yuvābhyām eva satvaraṁ samarpyatām \| yathā sa yāva-grāma-siṁho’bhimanyur eva tāḍana-pūrvakaṁ sva-karaṁ gṛhṇāti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Yes. Madhumaṅgala is opposed to giving the tax. Tie him up firmly with a rope of creepers and bring him to our mild, dear friend and she can then quickly give him to Jaṭilā and Abhimanyu, so that the lion of Yāva village can obtain the tax by beating him.”**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *antaḥ-sa-bhayam iva – vayasya ! kiñcin nigūḍhaṁ kāryaṁ mama gṛhe vidyate \| tat sampādyāgacchann asmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said as if afraid, “O friend! I have some private affair in my house. I am going to attend to that.”**

***tato’haṁ*** *– dhig brāhmaṇa ! katham abalā-vāg-āḍambareṇa mad-agrato’pi bibheṣi ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “What a brāhmaṇa! Why do you fear the noisy words of children?”**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *– mahā-śūra ! ghaṭṭī-pālasya tava govardhane dāna-vartanyāṁ bahuśaḥ śauryaṁ anubhūtam asti \| yat tasmin dine etābhir eva vṛndāvana-kara- nimittaṁ gāndharvā-nideśena nijottarīya-paṭāñcalena baddhā nīyamānam api māṁ vīkṣamāṇa eva vilakṣas tvam āsīḥ \| aham eva nija-bhūsuratvaṁ vivṛtya bhāgyena kathañcid urvārito’smi\|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “O great hero! I have experienced your great prowess when you were the collector at the toll gate at Govardhana. On that day, I was bound up with my own upper cloth on the instructions of Rādhā in order to obtain taxes. Seeing me carried off, you stood there surprised. Using my prowess as a brāhmaṇa, by good fortune I somehow escaped.”**

*ity uktvā bhītim anukṛtya palāyantam iva taṁ kare gṛhītvā parāvartya* ***mayoktaṁ*** *– lalite ! tādṛśyāḥ komalābalāyā api katham ahaṁ karaṁ dāsyāmi ? pratyuta balāt kāmam ādāsya eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, he pretended to have fear. As he fled, I took his hand and brought him back. I said, “O Lalitā! Why should I pay tax to such a soft child? Instead, by force, please pay up my taxes in full.”**

*iti niśamya netra-bhāgena mām īṣad avalokayantī rādhā susmitā āsīt \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Rādhā slightly glanced at me with the corner of her eye and smiled.**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– citre ! vāṭikā-prāghuṇa ity asya kas tāvad abhiprāyaḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “O Citrā! What was your intention in speaking about the guest of these gardens?”**

***lalitā –*** *nāndīmukhi ! taj jānaty api kathaṁ pṛcchasi ? yat sarva-kālīna-nija-vāsa-grāma-mahāvanam apahāya vatsara-ṣaṭ-saptaka-mātram atrāgato’sti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O Nāndīmukhī! You know. Why do you ask? Having left Mahāvana where he always lives, he came here only six or seven years ago.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– priya-janma-bhūmes tyāgena kiṁ tāvat kāraṇam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Why did he leave his dear birth place?”**

***rādhā an****uccaiḥ – tat-sthānasyāsvācchandyāt janatādhikyena tad-grāme nagara iva jāte abalā-vadha-bhāṇḍa-sphoṭana-nava-nīta-haraṇādi-vividha-visadṛśa-vyavasāyābhyāsena janita-bahu-vidha-vikarmābhilāṣasyāsiddhatvāt nirjane’smin mahati ghane vṛndāvane kulābalā-kulānāṁ geha-deha adhara-daśana-vasanāni svācchandyenāpahartum*  
*adhika-lālasaiva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā said softly “At that place he could not act freely, since that village is almost a city because of the great population. He could not fulfill his desires for performing various wicked acts arising from repeated practice of uncouth actions, like obstructing young girls, breaking pots and stealing butter. In the great, dense, lonely forest of Vṛndāvana he have developed even stronger desire to plunder freely the young girls’ houses and bodies and make teeth marks on their lips.”**

*ity evaṁ lalitayā sphuṭaṁ vyāhṛte sa-smitaṁ sākūtam āha – lalite ! sāmpratam asya tādṛśa-vyavasāyo na dṛśyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**When Lalitā repeated this aloud, Nāndīmukhī with a smile said with meaning, “O Lalitā! Now we do not see such behavior.”**

***rādhā*** *punar anuccaiḥ – satyam eva kathyate \| yad ayaṁ samprati sva-dharma-tyāgena visadṛśa-saṁskāreṇa ca janitānyādṛśa-buddhi-kṛta-cauryādi-duṣkṛtaṁ lalitācāryayā pūrvam api vihita-niṣkṛtena nirvāsya, nirveda-kṛta-vivekena tāsām eva kṛṣi-vṛttam eva nija-dharmam ācarya prakāmaṁ śasyāny utpādya tābhyaḥ pradāya tat svāṁśam api svayam ādāya tāḥ svātmānam apy ānandayan mahāśucir iva virājate \| ity eva sphuṭaṁ viśākhāpi sa-smitaṁ bhāṣitavatī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā said softly, “That is true. Having given up thievery and other sinful acts arising from criminal intelligence, arising from giving up his dharma and taking opposite training, because of atonements prescribed by Lalitācarya, he performs his dharma of agriculture, which is the occupation of the gopīs, with an understanding of repentance. He produces abundant grains, gives them to the gopīs and takes a portion for himself. This way he gives bliss to the gopīs and himself. He seems most virtuous.” Viśākhā repeated this aloud with a smile.**

*tatas tan niśamya sarveṣu hāsa-kolāhala-mahotsavam āviṣkurvatsu sa-kapaṭāsūyaṁ* ***mayoktam*** *– sakhe subala ! dhūrtābhir imābhir narma-bhaṅgī-miṣeṇa mama vṛndāvana-rājyādhikāritaiva dūrīkriyamāṇāstīti samadhigataṁ bhavatā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, everyone broke out in loud, joyous laughter.**  
**I said with false hatred, “O my friend Subala! You can understand that these deceptive gopīs are taking away my authority over the kingdom of Vṛndāvana by hiding behind joking words.”**

***subalaḥ*** *– na kevalam adhikāritaiva dūrīkṛtā kintu karṣako’pi kṛto’si \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “Not only have they taken away your authority but they have made you a farmer.”**

***vṛndā –*** *subala ! tvaṁ bahu-śruto vicakṣaṇas tathânayor api parama-snigdhaḥ \| tat kathaṁ nāndīmukhyā sahānayor parama-snigdhayor api rājya-hetor vivadamānayor ucita-nyāyāvalokanena virodhaṁ na davayasi ?*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “O Subala! You are well read and wise and very dear to the couple. Though they are most affectionate to each other, they are quarreling over rights to Vṛndāvana. Why don’t you, along with Nāndīmukhī, settle the dispute by using the required logic?”**

*tato bhadraṁ bhadram iti mayā lalitayā cokte subalaḥ prāha – vayasya ! prathamaṁ tāvat katham āsām etad rājyaṁ tal lalitaiva kathayatu \| paścād bhavān api kathaṁ vā bhavad-rājyaṁ taj jñāpayatu \|*

**Перевод:**

**I and Lalitā agreed. Subala then said, “O friend! First Lalitā should tell why they have rights to the kingdom. Next you can inform us why you have rights to the kingdom.”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *subala ! yāsāṁ vraja-deva-kanyānām ajñānenāpi saṅgam ācarya jāta- vyalīko’haṁ prāyaścittena śuddho’bhavaṁ sa kathaṁ tābhiḥ samam api punar vākovākaṁ vistarayāmi ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Subala! I associated out of ignorance with these divine girls of Vraja and developed bad qualities. By atonement I became purified. How can I again talk to them?”**

***vṛndā*** *– mahāśuddha ! bhavad-vāg-gandha-mātreṇāpi samudita-kaluṣa-sañcayā etā api śrī-vṛndāvana-cakravartinyā nūnam aśeṣa-vidhinā śyāmala-viṣama-śara-sarovara- mahā-tīrthe punaḥ punaḥ snapanena śuddīkṛtā eva sva-nikaṭam āpitāḥ santi tac cīrṇa- niṣkṛtayor ubhayoḥ saṁvāde doṣo nāsti \| tal lalite! bhavaty eva kathayatu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said “O most pure one! Though these girls have developed contamination just by a whiff of your words, they have become purified by repeatedly bathing in the great tīrtha called Śyāmala-viṣama-śara-sarovara, according to the many rules laid down by the empress of Vṛndāvana. They have come to you in this pure condition. There is no fault if two parties who have performed abundant atonements speak to each other. O Lalitā, now you should speak.”**

***lalitā –*** *prathamaṁ tāvad asmin vṛndāvana-rājye’syālīka-vādino dhūrtasyādhipatyaṁ tāvad dūre’stu sambandha-leśo’pi nāsti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “First, what to speak of ruling Vṛndāvana, the liar has no relationship at all with Vṛndāvana.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– katham iva ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “How is that?”**

***lalitā –*** *sva-paitṛka-rājye bṛhad-vana eva kula-kramāgata-santata-vāsitvāt \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “His ancestors have always lived in Mahāvana, his paternal kingdom.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– kim atra pramāṇam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “What is the proof of this.”**

***lalitā –*** *bhāgavatādi-purāṇa-vaktṛ-vyāsādi-munīndra-vacanāny eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “The words of the best sages like Vyāsa, who spoke Bhāgavatam.”**

***subalaḥ*** *– lalite ! madhyasthena mayā yathārtham eva vaktavyam \| tvan-mukham ālokya mithyā vaktuṁ na śakyate \| tad asya bṛhad-vana-rājyatvenaiva bhavatyā vṛndāvana-rājyādhipatyaṁ kim āyāti ? kintu tatra cet mahā-pramāṇam asti, tad vada \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “O Lalitā! I should speak truthfully as the moderator. Seeing your face, I cannot tell a lie. Do you have rights to Vṛndāvana simply because his kingdom is Mahāvana? If so, there must be some strong proof. Please speak.”**

***lalitā –*** *pratyakṣaṁ parokṣam api bahutara-purāṇa-vacana-kulam asti \| tatrāpi pratyakṣa-parokṣayoḥ pratyakṣam eva balīya ity anusāreṇa pratyakṣam eva grāhyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “There are many statements in the Purāṇas, both direct and indirect. Since direct statements or direct perceptions are the stronger of the two, we should accept the direct perceptions only.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– kiṁ tat pratyakṣaṁ pramāṇam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “What is the direct proof?”**

***lalitā –*** *mayā kiṁ tad vaktavyam ? yuṣmākam anubhūtam eva tat \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “What should I say? You are aware of it also.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– na smaryate \| tat prakāśaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “I do not remember. Please tell us.”**

***lalitā –*** *brahma-gṛhiṇī-sāvitrī-prabhṛti-devy-ādibhir dundubhi-vāditrādi-kolāhala- pūrvakaṁ kṛto’tra mahā-siṁhāsane yo ratnābhiṣekaḥ sa ca trilokī-vāsiṣu kasya vā paramānanda-pradāyako na saṁvṛtto’sti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Did not the bath using jewels when Rādhā sat on the throne while the wives of Brahmā, Sāvitrī and others celebrated loudly with drums and music give the highest bliss to the inhabitants of the three worlds?”**

*tataḥ* ***subalaḥ*** *– lalite ! etat tu tāvad anyathātvena nirūpya priya-vayasyo yuṣmān apy ātmasāt kartuṁ virājamāno vartate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “O Lalitā! If you describe something untrue, my dear friend is present to use you to his advantage.”**

***lalitā –*** *subala ! etac ced bhavad-dvipada-gopa-priya-vayasyaḥ satyam eva vakti tarhi vṛndāvana-rājye ‘bhiṣiktāyā mahā-siṁhāsana-sthāyā ity ādi tathaitasyāś caraṇānujīvino mamety ādi vyāhṛtya svāmy asau sāmpratam api kathaṁ tac caraṇa-paricaraṇa- mātraika-lābhāya tan-mantra-rājam ādātum āgraheṇādhyavasyann astīty ādi sarvaṁ kiṁ bhavādṛgbhir vismṛtam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O Subala! If your dear friend, the two legged cowherd, speaks the truth, then he has said that the feet of she who was bathed on the throne in the kingdom of Vṛndāvana are his very life. Why does he now make effort to receive her king of mantras in order to attain service to her feet? Has he forgotten all this?”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *smitvā – lalite ! asya vilāsino rāja-putrasya madana-modaka-bhojino mahā-kāmukasya yat pralapitaṁ tat kim atra pramāṇaṁ syāt ? anyac ced gāḍhaṁ pramāṇaṁ syād darśaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī smiled and said, “O Lalitā! Can what this lusty, playboy prince who feasts on Cupid’s sweets utters be taken as proof? If there is other strong proof, please show that.”**

***vṛndā –*** *yad yat pramāṇam iyaṁ darśayati, tat tad evāsyonmādino rāja-putrasya pakṣam āśritya yuvābhyāṁ khaṇḍyate \| ato’nyat sarvatra prasiddham api pramāṇaṁ lalitātikopena noṭṭaṅkayati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “Taking the side of the insane prince, you two (Subala and Nāndīmukhī) destroy whatever proof she gives.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *vṛnde ! tat tvayaiva tāvat kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “O Vṛndā! You should say something.”**

***vṛndā –*** *premnā nijāsādhāraṇa-sārūpya-dāna-pūrvakam iyaṁ vṛndāṭavī priya-sakhyā sva-priya-sakhītve nityaṁ nirūpitāstīti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “This Vṛndāvana is eternally described as the dear friend of Rādhā. She gave the place extraordinary spiritual form non-different from her by her prema.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *kīdṛśaṁ svārūpyaṁ tat kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Please describe this similar form which Vṛndāvana manifests.”**

***vṛndā –*** *śrūyatām –*  
*tanor asyā jyotiḥ-parimala-carac-campaka-latā-*  
*valī vidyud-vallī-jayi-kanaka-yūthī-tatir api \|*  
*svarūpaṁ sārūpyaṁ param agamayad vallava-pure*  
*mukhasyāpi prodyad-ruciratara-rājīva-nivahaḥ \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “Please listen. In the city of cowherds, the groves of golden yūthī flowers which defeat clusters of lightning and the groves of campaka creepers diffusing fragrance and brilliance attained a form similar to the supreme form of Rādhā’s body. The clusters of attractive, raised lotuses are the very form of her face.**

*dṛśor īṣad ghūrṇan-nava-kuvalayānāṁ samudayas*  
*tathā līlās tiryak-gamana-hariṇaḥ \|*  
*lasad-bimbī-vṛndaṁ vara-vikaca-bandhuka-nikaro’py*  
*asausīdhu-syandi-sphurad-adhara-yugmasya sukṛtī \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“All the softly waving, fresh lotuses and the play of the prancing does are the form of her eyes. The clusters of excellent, shining bandhuka flowers and the clusters of bright bimba fruits are the virtue of her shining lips flowing with nectar.**

*bhujāyā vallaryaś calana-lalitāś cañcu-nicayāḥ*  
*śukānāṁ nāsāyās tila-kusuma-sakhyā dyuti-bhṛtaḥ \|*  
*smitasya pronmīlat-kumuda-nikaro danta-surucer*  
*alaṁ gandhair andhīkṛta-madhukarāḥ kunda-kalikāḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Her arms are the creepers with graceful movements. Her radiant nose, friend of the sesame flower, is the beak of the parrot. Her smile is the host of fully blossoming lotuses. Her shining teeth are the buds of kunda flowers which blind the bees by their fragrance.**

*bhruvor bhaṅgī-bhrāmyad-bhramara-vara-paṅktiḥ parimitā*  
*lalāṭasya sphūrjat-subhaga-baka-puṣpāti-suṣamā \|*  
*alaṁ veṇer udyan-mada-śikhi-śikhaṇḍāvalir asau*  
*śruter muñjāyuktir madana-dhanuṣo jyā-dyuti-muṣaḥ \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Her brows are the restricted, fine lines of bees wandering in graceful curves. Her forehead is the most beautiful baka flower radiating auspiciousness. Her braid is a group of raised tail feathers of excited peacocks. Her ears, stealing the effulgence of the curved bow string of Cupid, are the totality of muñja grass.**

*alaṁ bilvaṁ tālaṁ karakam udayac cāru-kucayoḥ*  
*sphurad-vakṣaḥ-sthalyāḥ kanaka-kṛta-siṁhāsana-gaṇāḥ \|*  
*nitambasyaitasya sphurta-nava-ghaṇṭā-rava-bhṛtaḥ*  
*sphuṭaṁ vaṁśa-dhvānāñcita-guru-giri-sphāra-vikaṭāḥ \|\|5\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Her rising breasts are the bilva, tāla and pomegranate fruits. The surface of her gleaming chest are the many golden thrones in the forest. Her hips with jingling bells are the great expanses of Govardhana decorated with the sound of the flute.**

*varoru-dvandvasya smara-kali-samarthasya sarasa-*  
*prabhārambhā-stambhāḥ sthala-kamala-saṅghāś caraṇayoḥ \|*  
*sadonmāda-prodyad-gaja-gamana-śikṣā-guru-gater*  
*marālī-pālīnāṁ lalita-calitodyac-caturatā \|\|6\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Her excellent thighs, capable of competing with Cupid, are elegantly shining banana trunks. Her feet are groups of land lotuses. Her gait which is the sīkṣā- guru for the gait of elephants continually intoxicated is the charm of the graceful movement of flocks of swans.**

*alaṁ bhrājaj-jambū-phalam alaka-paṅkteḥ śata-mitaṁ*  
*madhau mañju-dhvānāḥ pika-yuva-kṛtāś cāru-bhaṇiteḥ \|*  
*prasanna-śrī-vaktra-sphurita-nayana-prānta-naṭana-*  
*kramasya smerodyat-sarasija-naṭat-khañjana-gaṇaḥ \|\|7\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Her lines of lac are hundreds of shining jambu fruits. Her charming speech is the sweet sound of young cuckoos in spring. The dancing of the edge of her eye appearing on her pleased face is the khañjana birds dancing over blossoming lotuses.**

*sphurat-premṇā netrāñjana-galita-pānīya-vitater*  
*yamī ceto-gañgā-jalam amala-cittasya satatam \|*  
*ghana-sveda-syandasya ca vividha-kāsāra-nivaho*  
*mahārāgasyoccai ruci-rucira-guñjā-phala-kulam \|\|8\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Her flood of tears mixed with collyrium arising from intense prema is the Yamunā. Her pure heart is eternally the water of Mānasa-gaṅgā. The flow of her profuse perspiration is the multitude of various ponds. The intense manifestation of her great rāga is a family of tasty, attractive guñja berries.**

*sapatnī-nāsānāṁ satatam atitāpaṁ janayatāṁ*  
*suhṛc-chreṇī-nāsā-pramadam anuvelaṁ racayatām \|*  
*vapuḥ-saurabhyāṇāṁ parimilita-sarva-vraja-bhuvāṁ*  
*nikāmaṁ kāśmīra-vraja-kamala-garbhā vara-rucaḥ \|\|9\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The fragrance of her body, which makes the noses of co-wives feel remorse, and sometimes drives the noses of her friends mad, is the excellent delight of pollen within the lotuses which appear all over Vraja.**

*sumeroḥ kāntīnāṁ mada-damaka-dṛpyal-lava-kaṇa-*  
*chaṭāyā vidyuc-chrī-racita-bhajanāyās tanu-rucaḥ \|*  
*sphurad-bhūmi-bhāgāḥ kanaka-nicitāḥ sadma-nivahā*  
*guhāntaḥ-kuñjāntar-girikula-śilāś ca kvacid api \|\|10\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“The beauty of her body, which is made of the beauty of lightning, a small fraction of whose pride subdues the pride of all the rays from Sumeru, is the gleaming area of land, the golden groups of houses, as well as the mountains with caves and bowers.**

*hradāḥ śrīman-nābhes tad-upari-lasal-loma-lahare*  
*r bhujaṅgālī-kālī bata vakaripor vepathū-bhṛtaḥ \|*  
*vadānyatvādīnāṁ surataru-gaṇānāṁ ratna-khacitāḥ*  
*paraṁ romāñcānāṁ priyaka-guru-kiñjalka-nikaraḥ \|\|11\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Her beautiful navel is the lakes and the line of hair rising from her navel is Kāliya, friend of the snakes, belonging to Kṛṣṇa, with his trembling body. Her generosity and other qualities are the jeweled objects emanating from the desire trees. Her hairs standing on end are the many stamens of the kadamba.**

*pare ye muktāḥ syuḥ sthira-cara-padārthā vraja-bhuvaḥ*  
*sadā stavyā bhavyais trijagati nikāmaṁ jana-kulaiḥ \|*  
*sad-aṅga-pratyaṅga-prakara-suṣamāyāḥ param umā-*  
*rati-śrī-varṇyāyāḥ pariṣadi yathā yuktam api te \|\|12\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“Rādhā’s beauty in every limb, praised by Pārvatī, Rati and Lakṣmī, is all the moving and nonmoving objects of Vraja, which will later become liberated, and which are always praised profusely by all the righteous people in the three worlds.**

*rameśa-svānandocchalita-parama-vyoma-purato’py*  
*ajasraṁ visphūrjad-vipula-sukhadaika-druma-latā \|*  
*samasta-brahmāṇḍe śaśivad-udayat-prauḍha-yaśasas*  
*tataḥ śrī-rādhāyāḥ prakaṭam aṭavīyaṁ priya-sakhī \|\|13\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**“This forest, whose one creeper from a tree gives immense, expanding happiness surpassing the spiritual sky immersed in Viṣṇu’s bliss, is the dear friend of Rādhā whose great fame is continually increasing in the universe.”**

***tato’haṁ*** *sāṭopam avadam –*  
*vrajendra-svārājye vijayi-yuvarājatva-vidhinā*  
*samāsiktaḥ sneha-stimitam atimitrādi-valitaḥ \|*  
*nijām etāṁ rakṣāmy aṭavim iha gocāra-miṣataḥ*  
*kathaṁ svārūpyeṇaiva tu bhavatu vaḥ sāmpratam iyam \|\|14\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**I said with some pride, “I have been installed according to rules for appointing me as the conquering prince in the kingdom of Nanda. I protect my forest here on the pretext of herding cows. How does this land become yours through some talk of identity of Rādhā with the land?”**

*tac chrutvā* ***vṛndā –***  
*sakhitvaṁ yo yasya vrajati nija-sārūpyam athavā*  
*na cet tasya syān no bhavati param anyasya hi tadā \|*  
*kathaṁ no gṛhṇāsi druta-gatir idaṁ hanta kathayan*  
*svayaṁ gatvā lakṣmīṁ tadīya samarūpaṁ gaṇam api \|\|15\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Vṛndā said, “The person who develops friendship to another becomes identical in form to that person or the object of the friendship becomes identical the person offering friendship. If the forest did not develop identity with us, then the forest would not be ours, and would belong to someone else. Why do you not quickly go to Lakṣmī and become identical to her?”**

*tan niśamya sāṭṭa-hāsaṁ madhumaṅgalaḥ – bho asatya-vādini vṛnde ! kurcikā-lobhena nija-devītvam utsṛjya vandinī bhūtvā, mithyā-stava-mātreṇaivāsmad-vṛndāṭavīṁ nija- sakhyāṁ sañcārayituṁ kathaṁ śakṣyasi ?*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Madhumaṅgala laughed loudly and said, “O Vṛndā, you are lying! Giving up your status as a goddess out of greed for some milk, you have become a praiser. By false glorification, how can you deliver our Vṛndāvana to your friend?”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *lalite ! vijña-prācīna-munīśvara-vacanaṁ vinā ko’pi nirasto na bhaviṣyati tasmāt purāṇa-vacanam eva darśaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “O Lalitā! Except for statements from ancient wise sages, no one will be defeated. Show some statements from the Purāṇas.”**

***lalitā –*** *asya pakṣam āśritya tādṛgbhir evaṁ cet kathyate tadā tatra gatvā bhagavatyāḥ sakāśād bahūni purāṇa-vacanāni śrūyantām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “If you take his side and speak like this, then go to Paurṇamāsī and hear plenty of statements from the Purāṇas.”**

***subalaḥ –*** *bhavatya eva paṭhantu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “You should cite the statements.”**

***lalitā –*** *vallava-jātīnāṁ tatrāpi strīṇām asmākaṁ purāṇa-vacana-pāṭhe’dhikāra eva nāsti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Since we are women and cowherds, we have no qualification for reciting the Purāṇas.”**

***subalaḥ –*** *vṛndeyaṁ devī \| tad iyam eva śrāvayatu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Subala said, “Vṛndā is a goddess. Let her recite them to us.”**

***vṛndā*** *smṛtim abhinīya – atra subahūni vacanāni santi \| tāni kati pāṭhyāni kintu anyeṣu deśeṣu anyāḥ pṛthak pṛthag devīr adhikariṇīr uktvā rādhā vṛndāvane vane iti sarvopamardakaṁ purāṇa-vacanaṁ bhagavatyāḥ sakāśāt kena vā na śrutam asti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā, as if recalling something said, “There are many statements in the scriptures. A few can be recited. In other places, other goddesses preside. Having stated this, the scripture says that Rādhā presides in Vṛndāvana. This statement from a Purāṇa defeats all other statements. Who has not heard this from Paurṇamāsī?”**

*tatas tāsāṁ jayābhimānena praphullatām ālokya* ***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *sāṭopam āha – nāndīmukhi ! sarva-purāṇa-śirasi mahopaniṣadi gopāla-tāpanyāṁ vṛndāvanasya kṛṣṇa-vanatvena prathita-khyātyā vṛndāvana-rāja-dhānī-purandaratvena priya- vayasyaṁ brahma-bhavādayo’pi nirantaraṁ gāyantaḥ santīti ke vā na jānanti ? tataḥ śrutyā smṛtir bādhyate ity asmākam evedaṁ rājyaṁ susiddham \| tat sakhe subala ! vidāvayām urvitaḥ para-rājya-kāṅkṣiṇīḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Seeing the gopīs happy with a pretense of victory, Madhumaṅgala said proudly, “O Nāndīmukhī! In the Gopāla-tāpanī Upaniṣad, above all Purāṇas, it is said that Brahmā and others continually glorify our friend as the king of the city of Vṛndāvana, which is known as Kṛṣna-vana. Who does not know this? Smṛti is annulled by śruti. Thus it is proved that this is our kingdom. O Subala! Drive these women who covet others’ kingdoms from the earth.”**

*tataḥ sakhe tvam eva me priyaṅkaraḥ priya-vayasya iti sānandaṁ madhumaṅgalam āliṅgati mayi kiñcid vailakṣyam iva rādhādhiṣṭhita-nikuñjam ālokya lalitādi-mukham ālokayantyāṁ nāndīmukhyāṁ* ***rādhā*** *smitvā anuccaiḥ – aho kṛṣṇeti śabda-śravaṇa- mātrata evānena mahā-dūra-darśinā tad-artham abuddhvaiva para-rājye’smin sva- tātam arpayatā kiṁśuka-kusuma-sādṛśyena durvidha-bodhasya rolambasya śuka- tuṇḍa-pāna-smaraṇaṁ suṣṭhu samānītāḥ smaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**I then said, “O my friend! You are affectionate to me, a dear friend.” I hugged him in bliss.**  
**Nāndīmukhī, as if confused, looked towards the bower where Rādhā was staying and then looked at Lalitā’s face. Rādhā, smiling, said to Nāndīmukhī softly, “By hearing the word** ***Kṛṣṇa*****, not knowing the meaning, this great prophet has offered another’s kingdom to himself. This untrustworthy person, or crooked intelligence, like a kiṁśuka (is this a parrot?) flower, reminds us of a parrot drinking (or kissing) with its beak.”**

*tatas tasminn eva varṇa-vinyāse solluṇṭhaṁ lalitayāpi prakāśite sati kṛṣṇa-vana- śabdasyārthāntaraṁ nūnam eṣā ghaṭayiṣyatīti tad-abhiprāyaṁ manasi manāk vicārayati mayi* ***nāndīmukhī*** *sacamatkāraṁ māṁ prāha – jayākāṅkṣin ! ekaṁ vijñāpayitum icchāmi yadi tubhyaṁ rocate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā conveyed her words with irony. I reflected on her intention. “She will interpret the word Kṛṣṇa-vana in another way.”**  
**Nāndīmukhī then surprised me and spoke to me. “You who desire victory! I desire to announce something if it pleases you.”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *kāmaṁ kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Please speak.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *śyāma-vana-samāsam ācarya tvayā saha tat-samāsenaiva nyāyam ācarya tvāṁ parājitya svarājyam ādātuṁ laliteyam abhilaṣati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Lalitā desires to receive the kingdom by defeating you with logic by analyzing the compound** ***kṛṣṇa-vana.*****”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *vividha-kusuma-candana-varṇikādibhiḥ śṛṅgāra-racanācāryeyam \| tad eva jānāti yad anayā tad-vana-puṣpair muhuḥ śṛṅgārito’smi \| tad vyākaraṇasya keyam ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “She is the ācārya for making decorations using various flowers, sandalwood and writing. She knows that, since I am always decorated with forest flowers by her. How can she be the ācārya of grammar?”**

***vṛndā*** *smitvā – vyākaraṇa-vijñaṁ-manya ! tvatto’pi mat-priya-sakhī lalitā tad- vyākaraṇasya prathitācāryā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā, smiling, said, “O you who think your know grammar! More than you, our dear friend Lalitā is known as the ācārya of grammar.”**

***lalitā –*** *pāmari vṛnde ! apehi apehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Wicked Vṛndā! Go away!”**

***vṛndā –*** *tvat-pakṣam āśritya vivadamānāṁ māṁ kim ity ākṣipasi ?*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “Taking shelter of you, I am quarrelling with him. Why do you scold me?”**

***lalitā –*** *bhoḥ samāsācārya-śārdūla ! kathaṁ svepsitāpara-samāsena vibhīṣikāṁ pradarśya mat-priya-sakhyāḥ khelāspada-kṛṣṇa-vanam idam ādātuṁ vṛthaivādhyavasyasi \| yato’tra nityaṁ vaiśvānaravaj jājjvalyamāna-bahuvrīhi- karmadhārayādy-avadhāraṇataḥ svayam eva dūrād evāpasariṣyasi \| \[samāsābhijña kṛṣṇa-vana-śabdasya narma-cchalena tat-puruṣa-samāsaṁ viracayya para-rājyaṁ grahītuṁ katham abhilaṣasi \| yad atra karmadhāraya-bahuvrīhi-samāsayor evāvakāśaḥ \|\]*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Oh! Tiger ācārya of compound words! Why do you uselessly attempt to grab this kṛṣṇa-vanam which is the playground of my dear friend, by showing something frightening through your meaning of the compound? You will retreat far away automatically when you understand that the phrase is obviously a bahuvrīha-karmadhāraya compound (adjective compound). O knower of compounds! Why do you desire to take another’s kingdom by foolish trickery through interpreting the word kṛṣṇa-vanam as a tat-puruṣa compound (Kṛṣṇa’s forest)? The phrase reveals a bahuvṛīhi-karmadhāraya compound.”**

*tato* ***mayoktaṁ*** *– caturaṁmanye ! prakaṭe’tra tat-puruṣa-samāse kathaṁ karmadhārayādi-yojanā sambhavati \| bhavatu bhavatu durjana iti nyāyena tad eva tāvat kathaya śrotavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “You think you are intelligent! Since clearly it is a tat-puruṣa compound, how can it be a bahuvrīhi? Well, let the fool be! Please explain it. We are listening.”**

***rādhā*** *anuccaiḥ – vanasyāsya ghanatā-prācaryeṇa kṛṣṇaṁ śyāmaṁ ca tad-vanaṁ ceti karmadhārayaḥ sphuta eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā said softly, “It is clearly a karmadhāraya meaning ‘dark forest’--a forest which is dark, because the forest spreads darkness.”**

*ity evaṁ lalitayāpi sphuṭaṁ bhāṣite* ***campakalatā*** *sa-ślāgham āha – lalite ! sādhu sādhu satyaṁ satyaṁ yataḥ karmāṇi ariṣṭa-keśyādi-vadha-kāliya-damana- govardhanoddharaṇa-nitya-rāsādi-līlā dhārayati niṣpādayati prakāśayati vā ity asya vanasya karmadhārayatvaṁ sphuṭam eva prasiddham \|*

**Перевод:**

**This was conveyed by Lalitā aloud. Campakalatā praised this by saying, “O Lalitā! That was well spoken. It is also true because** ***karma-dhāraya*** **means ‘that which reveals or produces (*****dhārayati*****) pastimes like killing Ariṣṭa, and Keśī, subduing Kāliya, lifting Govardhana and the eternal rāsa dance, through action (*****karma*****).” The karmadhāraya nature of the forest (to reveal pastimes) is clear.”**

*punar* ***lalitā*** *rādhānucca-bhāṣitam evānuvadati – kṛṣṇāni kvacid kvacid atiśyāmāni vanāni yatra tat kṛṣṇa-vanam iti vṛndāvanasya viśeṣaṇatvena bahuvrīhir api \|*

**Перевод:**

**Again Lalitā repeated Rādhā’s soft words. “Where there are forests which are dark, that is Kṛṣṇa-vana. It is a** ***bahuvrīhi*** **describing Vṛndāvana.”**

***vṛndā --*** *satyaṁ satyaṁ kālindī-tīra-rāsa-sthalyām andhakāri-baṭa-vanaṁ, tathā govardhanopaśalya-para-rāsa-sthalyām andhakāri-niviḍa-vanaṁ sarvānanda-karaṁ suprasiddham eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “That is true. At the place of the rāsa dance on the bank of the Yamunā there is a forest of banyan trees which is very dark. There is a dense, dark forest at the other place of the rāsa dance near Govardhana. It gives all bliss and is very famous.”**

***indulekhā –*** *lalite ! satyaṁ satyaṁ bahavo brīhayo dhānyādi-śasyāni kiṁ vā kedārikā- jātatvān muktā eva brīhayaḥ \| bahavo brīhayo yasmin nityasya vanasya bahuvrīhatvaṁ sphuṭam eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Indulekhā said, “O Lalitā! That is true.** ***Bahuvrīhi*** **means ‘many grains.’ Or Vṛndāvana is the place where there are pearls as grains since they have sprouted from the fields there. It is clearly a** ***bahuvrīhi*** **describing the eternal forest in which there are many grains.”**

*tataḥ sa-garva-garbhitaṁ hasantīṣu tāsu* ***mayoktaṁ*** *– nānā-kuṭa-kalpanā-nagarī- cakravartini lalite ! mukham atra tat-puruṣa-samāsaṁ kalpita-karmadhāraya- bahuvrīhibhyāṁ kathaṁ davayituṁ śakyase \|*

**Перевод:**

**While they laughed with pride, I said, “O Lalitā, empress of women who constructs various lies! How can you escape the main** ***tat-puruṣa*** **meaning by imagined meanings of** ***karmadhāraya*** **and** ***bahuvrīhi*****?”**

***lalitā –*** *mahā-paṇḍita ! tat-puruṣas tatpuruṣa iti bāraṁ bāraṁ jalpasi \| tatpuruṣas tāvad aneka-vidho bhavati \| tatra katamo’yaṁ sa iti suṣṭhu nirṇīya kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O great scholar! You loudly proclaim** ***tat-puruṣa*****. There are many varieties of** ***tat-puruṣas*****. Please clearly explain which one it is.”**

***mayoktaṁ*** *– jaḍa-buddhike ! kṛṣṇasya vanaṁ kṛṣṇa-vanam iti ṣaṣṭhī-tatpuruṣas trijagati suprasiddha eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O foolish intelligent woman!** ***Kṛṣṇa-vanam*** **means the forest of Kṛṣṇa. That** ***ṣasthī-tat-puruṣa*** **(possessive case) is famous in the three worlds.”**

***rādhā –*** *kṛṣṇasya vanam iti cet tarhi sakhīsthalī-baṭa-śreṇir eva puruṣa-śārdūlasya tava vanam \| yataḥ ṣaṣṭhī-tatpuruṣo’pi nityaṁ tatraiva vartate \| atra ṣaṣṭhī-samāsasya sambhāvanāpi na vidyate ity evaṁ vihasya lalitayāpy ukte* ***nāndīmukhī –*** *lalite ! eṣa te vāg-vinyāso garbhita-sandarbha iti lakṣyate tat prakāśya kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā said, “If it means Kṛṣṇa’s forest, it means the banyan grove at Sakhīshalī (Candrāvalī’s place) belonging to you, the tiger among men, for ṣaṣthi-tat-puruṣa remains there at all times. There is no possibility that it is a possessive compound.” Lalitā conveyed this to the others with a laugh.**  
**Nāndīmukhī said, “O Lalitā! It seems that your words have deep meaning. Please explain the meaning.”**

*tato* ***lalitā*** *netrānta-nīlotpala-mālayā mām akurvatī smita-garbhitam āha – nāndīmukhi ! ṣaṣṭhī kācid ekā \| tasyāḥ puruṣaḥ patir eva jano vā ṣaṣṭhī-tatpuruṣaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā, hiding a smile, giving me a garland of blue lotuses by the glance of her eye, said, “O Nāndīmukhī! Ṣaṣṭhī is a certain woman. Her husband (*****puruṣaḥ*****) is called ṣaṣṭhi-tat-puruṣaḥ.”**

***viśākhā*** *sa-smitaṁ – lalite ! tat-puruṣo jñāta eva \| sā tāvat kā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said with a smile, “O Lalitā! We know that puruṣa. Who is the woman?”**

***lalitā –*** *candrāvalī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “It is Candrāvalī.”**

***viśākhā –*** *candrāvalī kathaṁ ṣaṣṭhī ?*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Why is Candrāvalī called Ṣaṣṭhī?”**

***lalitā –*** *devī-gaṇa-madhye prathamaḥ kaṁsa-gopo govardhana-mallo mahā-bhairavaḥ\|*  
*dvitīyā tan-mātā bhāruṇḍā caṇḍī, tṛtīyā candrāvalī-mātā mahī-karālā carcikā \|*  
*caturthī śaivyā kālī \| pañcamī padmā śaṅkhinī prasiddhā \| ṣaṣṭhī sakhīsthalī-baṭa-*  
*vāsinī candrāvalī ṣaṣṭhī \| yato baṭa-vana-vāsitvāt tasyāḥ ṣaṣṭhītvaṁ yuktam eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Among the persons to be worshipped, the first person is the cowherd of Kaṁsa, Govardhana Malla, who is Mahā-bhaivara. The second, his mother Bhāruṇḍā, is Caṇḍi. Third, Candrāvalī’s mother is Carcikā with a huge, gaping mouth. Fourth, Śaibya is Kālī. Fifth, Padmā is the famous Śaṅkhinī. Sixth, Candrāvalī, resident of Sakhīsthalī banyan tree, is Ṣaṣṭhī. She is suitable to be called Śaṣṭhī because Ṣaṣṭhī resides in a banyan forest.”**

*tataḥ sarveṣāṁ hāsa-kolāhala-vṛtte* ***ahaṁ*** *svagataṁ – aho buddher variṣṭhatā vraja- bālānāṁ, yad aham api vacanāṭopa-vilāsair nirvacanīkṛto’smi \| prakāśaṁ sa-nirvedam ivāhaṁ – nāndīmukhi ! asmad-datta-bhoga-rāga-masṛṇa-vasanādibhiḥ saṁvardhitābhir asmad-datta-muktā-maṇi-pravāla-kamala-rāga-marakata-vajrādi- khacita-vividha-bhūṣaṇa-bhūṣitābhiḥ sāmpratam abhinava-yauvana-mahādhana- garveṇojjhita-guru-laghu-gaṇanotkarābhir asmat-kṛṣaka-gujjara-gujjarībhir apy etābhir helollāsita-cañcala-nayana-prānta-nartana-pūrvakaṁ sāhaṅkṛti-vacanāḍambara-vinyāsa-bhareṇa samasta-vraja-sāmrājya-sārvabhaumasya paramoddaṇḍa-kumāro’ham api niravadhi-viḍambyamāno’pi kevalaṁ bhagavatī- caraṇa-parijana-mukhya-nāndīmukhī-mukham ālokya tathaika-grāma-vāsenāpakīrti- bhayena ca etat-kṛtaṁ pratīpa-vyavasitam api etāvantaṁ kālaṁ duḥkhadatvenāpi na gaṇitavān asmi \| sāmprataṁ mama doṣo na deyaḥ \| adhunaiva nija-nija-vakṣasi masṛṇa-mecaka-paṭa- pariveṣṭitam ativicitrita-kārtasvara-sampūṭa-yuga-puṭitam aruṇa-dvitīyā-śaśadhara- mudrā-mudritaṁ sva-sva-paribṛdhair api kadāpy anālokita-caraṁ durlabha-nava- tāruṇya-dhanaṁ mahā-tīvra-nakha-bhaṭais tathaiva nirupama-daśana-cchada- padmarāga-mahā-ratnam api daśanoddhara-sāmantair api luṇṭhayitvā etā vacana- dhana-daridrā vidadhāno’smi \|*

**Перевод:**

**When they all burst out in loud laughter, I thought to myself, “These girls of Vraja have the highest intelligence. Even I am made speechless by their proud display of words.”**  
**Then aloud with some regret I said, “O Nāndīmukhī! Though I am the most exalted prince, controlling all the kingdom of Vraja, because they prosper with soft cloth, delight, and enjoyment given by me, decorated with ornaments inlaid with diamonds, emeralds, rubies, coral and pearls, these wives of us farmers have disregarded and abandoned their elders, out of pride in their great wealth and fresh youth. I have been endlessly insulted by the excessive racket of their proud words as they glance wantonly with the edges of their eyes. Looking at the face of Nāndīmukhī, the chief follower of Paurṇamāsī, and fearing defamation since we live in one village, I do not mind the harassment done by these hostile forces up till now, though it has made me suffer.**  
**Now, do not find fault with me. Being destitute of a wealth of words, I now commence to plunder the wealth of rare, fresh youth never seen before by their superiors, marked with the sign of the second pink moon, enclosed in two fancy gold caskets, surrounded by the soft blue cloth on your chests, using the warriors of my very sharp nails and as well I will loot the great ruby covering your incomparable teeth, using the unrestrained generals in the form of my teeth.”**

*iti sāṭopaṁ sahasopasṛtya tāḥ sandidhīrṣau mayi kuṭila-bhrū-nartana-valita-smita- śabalita-kaṭākṣeṇa mad-avalokana-pūrvakam itas tato manāg apasarpantīṣu sarvāsu, sa-roṣam iva* ***lalitā*** *– aye śyāmala rasa-pāna-nirata ! apehi apehi \| etāṁ te mattatārabhaṭīṁ vrajeśvaryai varṇayituṁ vayaṁ calitāḥ smaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**When I boldly advanced to grab them, they glanced at me with smiles and dancing, variegated, crooked brows and moved away. Lalitā said in anger, “Ah! You who are absorbed in tasting mādhurya-rasa! Go away! Go away! We will go and describe your insane boldness to Yaśodā.”**

***satyabhāmā*** *sa-camatkāraṁ hasantī – vinodin ! ekaṁ praṣṭum icchāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Satyabhāmā said, in astonishment, while laughing, “O playful Kṛṣṇa! I desire to ask one question.”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *– priye ! vijñāpaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa said, “My dear, please tell me.”**

***satyabhāmā*** *– rādhā-svagata-lapitāvalir eva lalitādibhir api katham anvavādi ?*

**Перевод:**

**Satyabhāmā said, “How could Lalitā and others repeat what Rādhā said to herself in the bower?”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *– priye ! rādhāyāḥ kāya-vyūha-rūpā eva lalitādayas tat kathaṁ nādhigamiṣyanti ?*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa said, “O beloved! Since Lalitā and others are the expanded bodies of Rādhā, why could they not understand?”**

***satyabhāmā*** *– subhaga ! rādhāyā narmottara-varṇa-vinyāsaḥ kathaṁ vā bhavan- mānasa-sañcārī babhūva ?*

**Перевод:**

**Satyabhāmā said, “O auspicious lord! How did the joking of Rādhā (in the bower) spread to your mind?”**

***Madhumaṅgala*** *sa-gadgadaṁ – priyasakhi satye! parimala-mañjarī-mañjula- mṛgamadāv iva paraspara-saṁpṛktau gāndharvā-giridhāriṇau tat kathaṁ na sañcaratv iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, with choked voice, “O dear friend Satyabhāmā! Rādhā and Kṛṣṇa are an intimate combination, like sweetness to musk or fragrance to a bud. Why would her joking not reach him?”**

*iti tad-vacana-niśamana-madhura-payaḥ-pāna-parivardhita-gāndharvā-virahotkaṭa- kaṭu-garalodbhaṭa-vikaṭa-jvālā-jāla-janitābhiḥ \| punaḥ punaś cālyamāna-hṛdaya- marma-nimagnārdha-bhagna-pratapta-lauha-tiryag-avaśalyojjṛmbhita-yātanā- bharato’pi mahā-tīvra-pīḍāvalībhir abhito’ntara-nirbhara-kātaro yadā babhūva tadaiva tad-avadhānataḥ sañcarad-apūrva-jāḍya-mohonmādādi-priya-sahacara-samudaya-sahita-sūddīptatama-stambha-kampādy-aṣṭa-sāttvika-priya-vayasyeṣu bāḍham ahaṁ pūrvakaṁ ahaṁ pūrvaka-khelana-vidhinā narmocchalita-paraspara-vijayāya pragāḍha- prāgalbhyam upalabhya yugapat tvaritam upary upari tam āliṅgituṁ sarvataḥ sarabhasam ārabhamāneṣu*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa became completely agitated with sharp pains arising from the intensely acrid poison of separation from Rādhā, increased by hearing the sweetness of Madhumaṅgala’s words. He was filled internally with increasing pain from a hot iron arrow broken in half, which penetrated deep into the center of his palpitating heart. At that time, being attentive to him, his friends named intense paralysis, shivering and other of the eight sāttvika-bhāvas-- along with dear assistants in the form of unprecedented immobility and bewilderment (fainting)--spreading through his body, began to embrace him tightly and simultaneously assumed intense boldness by saying jokingly, “I am first, I am first” in order to compete and defeat each other, in a playful manner.**

*– re re vidagdha-śiromaṇiṁ-manyāḥ ! khelana-samayam uttamaṁ labdhāḥ stha ity upālabhya śrīmat-premānirvacanīya-pariṇāma-viśeṣa-svarūpeṇa niravadya-hṛdya-sauhṛdyācintya-mahima-mahauṣadha-rasāti-vaśīkṛta-sapriya-parijana-gaṇa-kṛṣṇaika-sarvasva-rūpeṇa samastātarkya-mahā-mahā-prabhāva-bahuvidha-līlādi-śakty-āpanna-cintā-ratnādi-ratna-viñjolīka-maṅgalākareṇa nirupama-vividha-vaidagdhya-cāturya-samayādi-vijñatva-sugandha-kusumotkara-nirbharārāmeṇa śrī-śrī-rasa-nāma-prāṇa-priyatama-narma-sakhena sa- bhrū-bhaṅgaṁ nayana-ghūrṇana-līlayā vihita-pratiṣedhāt svayam api ca paścād avadhāya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then the prāṇa-priya-narma-sakha, endowed with a full garden of plentiful fragrant flowers of incomparable cleverness, skill and knowledge of time, who was an auspicious form of a collection of jewels such as a cintāmaṇi, endowed with many inconceivable, powerful śaktis like līlā-śakti--the form of complete surrender to Kṛṣṇa which controlled all his dear associates and friends, like a great medicine of inconceivable greatness and a faultless intimate friend, who was the special, inconceivable transformation of prema--then scolded those friends with a frown, with eyes rolling to forbid them. “O you who think you are the crest jewels of cleverness! You have found the opportunity to cavort to the extreme.” They then withdrew.**

*bho bhoḥ ! anāyyam anāyyam iti rasajñāṁ daśanaiḥ saṁdaśya laghu laghu sa-saṅkocaṁ sva-pṛṣṭena dūram apasaratsu teṣu – tato’sphuṭa-bahir-vikāra-nikaraḥ sambhṛta-hṛt-kampātiśayaḥ sa* ***śrī-śrīmān yādavendraḥ*** *svagatam idaṁ vilalāpa – hā mat-prāṇa-kapota-vāsa-baḍabhi-prema-sphuran-mādhurī-dhārāpāra-sarid-vare ! guṇa- kalā-narma-prahelī-khane ! hā man-netra-cakora-poṣaka-vidhu-jyotsnā-tate rādhike ! hā hā mad-duritena kena nidhivat prāptā karāt tvaṁ cyutā ?*

**Перевод:**

**“O! We have acted wrongly.” They bit their tongues and shrinking, quietly withdrew far behind him. The external symptoms subsided, but his heart was still trembling violently. Kṛṣṇa, lord of the Yādavas uttered to himself, “O excellent, unsurpassable river, with streams of sweetness which reveal prema. O pinnacle where the dove of my life air lives! O storehouse of jokes, riddles, arts and qualities! Ah! Mother of the moonlight which nourishes the cakora bird of my eyes! O Rādhikā! Having attained you like a jewel after much difficulty, have you fallen from my hand?”**

*tataḥ* ***satyabhāmā*** *– yādavendra ! śrīman-mukhāravindād gokula-vilāsa-mādhurī- makarandaṁ dhayantyā api mama pipāsā param ullālasīti \| tat-kṛpayā tad eva punaḥ pāyaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Satyabhāmā said, “O king of the Yādavas! By sipping the sweet honey of the pastimes of Gokula from your lotus mouth, my thirst has increased to the extreme. Please let me drink again.”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *– priye śrūyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa said, “Please listen, my beloved.”**

*tato* ***madhumaṅgalenoktaṁ*** *– sakhi lalite ! priya-sakhasyāham alaṅghya-vākya- sacivo’smi \| tad apūrvaṁ kam apy utkocaṁ mahyaṁ dehi \| mayā mauktika-mūlyena vaḥ sācivyaṁ vidhāsyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala then said, “My friend Lalitā! As minister of my dear friend, I cannot disobey his orders. Give me some extraordinary bribe. I will assist you in getting the proper price for the pearls.”**

***viśākhā*** *– ārya ! kim apy atra nāsti \| sāyaṁ śakrāśanārthaṁ tubhyaṁ varāṭikā- catuṣṭayam avaśyaṁ deyam \| yadi pratītir na kriyate tvayā nāndīmukhī pratibhūr gṛhyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Respected sire! There is nothing here. In the evening we can give you four cowries for some intoxicants.”**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *sa-krodhaṁ – avadya-bhāṣiṇi ābhīrike ! tiṣṭha tiṣṭha \| eṣo’haṁ sadya eva tavaitat prāyaścittaṁ kārayann asi iti nigadya māṁ pratyuvāca – priya-vayasya ! bhavan-mṛdula-vacana-druta-ghṛta-dhārayā saṁvardhita-garva-vaiśvānarābhir amūbhiḥ suṣṭhu bhavantam upālabhya mām api baddhā netuṁ vyavasīyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said angrily, “O foul-mouthed cowherd woman! Stop! Stop! I will make you atone for that.” He then spoke to me. “Dear friend! Their pride just increases like fire by the streams of liquid ghee of your gentle words. They scold you and will try to tie me up and take me away.”**

***tato’haṁ*** *– sakhe ! satyaṁ satyaṁ yathā vinā rājadhānī-jayena tad-deśāḥ suṣṭhu vaśā na bhavanti \| tathaitad yūtheśā parābhavam antareṇa tadīyāḥ katham amūr amūkā na bhavantu \| iti nigadya rādhā-sanāthaṁ kuñjaṁ paśyan sa-nirvedaṁ punar avadaṁ – kiṁ kartavyaṁ sā kilaitad bhayenaiva mat-sānnidhyaṁ nāsāditavatī iti niśamya drutam udgrīvikāvalokanāt saṅkucantī rādhā kuñja-madhye niviṣṭaḥ babhūva \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Dear friend! That is true. Without conquest of the kingdom, the place is not congenial to live in. The head of their group must be defeated. Then her followers will be silent.”**  
**Saying this, I glanced at the bower where Rādhā was hiding. I said with regret, “What should I do? Out of fear she will not approach me.” Rādhā, after looking up, quickly crouched and hid within the bower.**

***satyabhāmā*** *– tatas tataḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Satyabhāmā said, “Then what happened.”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *– tato’haṁ – nāndīmukhi ! lalitādīnāṁ tāruṇya-dhanād api tasyās tāruṇya- dhanaṁ pracurataram amūlyam apīti jñāyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa said, “O Nāndīmukhī! Please understand that the wealth of Rādhā’s beauty is of more value than the treasure of youth of Lalitā and others.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– katham iva ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Why is that?”**

***mayoktaṁ*** *– yata āsāṁ tāvat sampūṭa-dvaya-parimitaṁ tat tasyās tu mahā-mataṅgaja- kumbhato’pi nistalatvenonnatatvena pariṇāhena ca pravīṇatare hṛn-madhyasthe gokula-prasiddha-taskara-bhītyeva mṛgamada-paṅkādi-lepena varṇāntaram āpite hiraṇmaya-kumbha-yugale paripūrṇaṁ tat \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Because they have only two treasure chests.
