She has two golden pots, situated in the center of her heart, greater in breadth, height and firmness than the bumps on the forehead of a huge elephant, which she has colored black by application of musk paste, out of fear of the famous thief of Gokula.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *mohana ! atisuguptam etad dhanaṁ kadāpi bhavad-dṛṣṭa-caraṁ vṛttam asti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said “O enchanter! When did you see this highly concealed treasure?”**

***tato’haṁ*** *sa-smitaṁ – nāndīmukhi ! vidyuc-camatkṛtim iva sakṛd īkṣitam asti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said with a smile, “O Nāndīmukhī! I saw the treasure once, which was more amazing than lightning.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *kadā ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “When was that?”**

***mayoktaṁ*** *– ekasminn avasare nija-sarovara-snānād uttīrya nija-madhurāṅgataś cīnāṁśukena rahasi tayā niḥsāryamāṇe payasi sumanoharaṇāya daivāt tatraivāgatena mayā manāg evāvalokitaṁ tat tataḥ sā sabhayam iva sahasaiva śyāmala- śāṭikāñcalenāvṛtavatī \| samprati mad-bhāgya-vaśād yadi kutrāpi yauvana-dhana-garvitā sāsmad-rājyābhilāṣiṇī mac-cakṣuṣor aparokṣībhavet tadā nakha-daśana-bhaṭa-sāmantān antareṇāpi chāyā-dvitīyena mayā kara-kamala-yugenaiva tat-kumbha-dvayam āloḍya tāruṇya-dhanāny ātma-sātkṛtya sādhvasa-labdha-kampa-pulakāyāṁ tasyāṁ yūtha-nāthāyāṁ nirvacanī-kṛtāyāṁ tad-amātya-ghaṭeyaṁ mahā-vaikalyena*  
*sva-sva-tāruṇya-dhanaṁ gṛhītvā palāyitum api suṣṭhu-sthānam api nāpsyati kiṁ vā tad-dhana-samarpaṇa-pūrvakaṁ śuddha-bhāvena mām eva paricariṣyati \| tad atikṣudrābhir ābhiḥ sva-mahimonnāha-glāni-kāriṇā vākovākena kim ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “One day, when she dropped her fine cloth from her sweet limbs into the water as she emerged from bathing alone in her pond, I saw her for a moment, having come there to pick flowers by chance. In fear, she quickly covered herself with the edge of her blue cloth. If now, by good fortune, that object, proud of the wealth of her youth, desiring my kingdom, would appear before my eyes, then instead of using my soldiers and general--my nails and teeth, using my lotus hands, a second shadow, I will press her breasts and bring the treasures of youth to me. When she becomes silent and trembles in fear with hairs standing on end, her ministers, grabbing their treasures, will not find a safe place to which to flee. Or offering their treasures, they will serve me with pure hearts. Why quarrel with these low cowherd girls, since it gives fatigue to my great glory?”**

*tataḥ kiñcit smitvā* ***nāndīmukhīṁ*** *prati rādhā anuccaiḥ prāha – ayi capala- brahmacāriṇi ! apehi apehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Smiling slightly, Rādhā spoke softly to Nāndīmukhī. “O fickle brahmacāriṇī! Go away! Go away!”**

***viśākhā*** *– aho uḍḍīya calitum aśakyasya bimbīmātraika-bhojino bubhukṣitasya lolubha-śukasya manasi durlabha-madhura-drākṣā-bhakṣaṇam etat \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “The greedy parrot, eager to eat, who usually eats only bimba fruits, is unable to fly away. He is now thinking of eating rare, sweet grapes.”**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *– priya-vayasya ! mad-abhīṣṭa-pāritoṣikaṁ dehi dhruvam adhunaiva bhavad-rājyābhilāṣiṇīṁ rādhāṁ tāṁ bhavat-kara-gatāṁ vidhāsye \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “My dear friend! Give me my desired reward and certainly right now I will put Rādhā, who desires your kingdom, in your hand.”**

***tato’ham*** *– sāyaṁ miṣṭānna-bhojanaṁ dāsye \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said “In the evening I will feed you sweets.”**

***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *– māthura-rāja-pārśve phutkṛtya tad-aśvārūḍhānāṁ śatam ānīya pṛṣṭha-deśe carma-rajjvā kaphoṇi-dvaya-bandha-pūrvakaṁ kaśābhis tat-patir abhimanyus tathā tāḍayitavyaḥ yathā sa eva tām ānīya dadāti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Madhumaṅgala said, “Calling to the king of Mathurā, I will bring a hundred horses and they will bind Abhimanyu’s elbows behind his back with leather ropes and flog him with whips. He will then bring her to you.”**

*tataḥ sarve smayayanti sma \|*

**Перевод:**

**Everyone smiled.**

***lalitā*** *– viśākhe ! kam api mahābhāgam uddiśya yat kiñcin nivedayāmi tat śṛṇu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O Viśākhā! I will now reveal something which is aimed at good fortune. Please listen.”**

***viśākhā*** *– kathyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Please speak.”**

*tataḥ kadamba-bhūruhāgram abhivīkṣya* ***lalitā*** *– bho vṛndāvana-cara-tapasvi-vara ! tvaṁ tāvat phalāhārī khyātas tat-sva-maryādhām ullaṅghya bhavad-ayogya-parama- durlabha-sādhvī-hṛdayaṅgama-mahā-dhane lālasām udvahan kathaṁ kaluṣito bhavann asi ?*

**Перевод:**

**Looking towards the kadamba tree, Lalitā said, “O best of ascetics travelling in Vṛndāvana! You are known for eating only fruits. Why do you pollute yourself by harboring the desire for the great wealth of the heart of the chaste girl, most difficult to obtain and unsuitable for you, transgressing your own rules?”**

***viśākhā*** *– lalite ! bubhukṣito’yaṁ tapasvī phalam anālabhya etad ayogyam api kartuṁ pravṛtto’sti \| tad uttama-phalam asmai samuddiśyatām \| tathāpi dharma-vṛddhir bhaviṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “O Lalitā! Desiring to eat, the ascetic did not obtain fruit. Therefore he is trying for something unsuitable. You should show him where to obtain the best fruit. By that you increase your dharma.”**

***lalitā*** *smitam apavarya – viśākhe ! kim asau mānasa-jāhnavīṁ na jānāti ?*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said with a smile privately, “O Viśākhā! Does he know about Mānasa-gaṅgā?”**

***viśākhā*** *– sarvatra bhramaṇa-śīlo’yam atiprasiddhāṁ tāṁ kathaṁ na jñāsyati ?*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Since he is inclined to wander everywhere, why would he not know about the famous place?”**

***lalitā*** *– tad vāyavya-tīre sarvataś-calā kācid apūrva-padmā sphuṭam ekā vallarī vartate \| tasyā madhya-bhāge parama-manoharaṁ ahrasvaṁ tumbī-phala-dvandvaṁ tathāgre ca akṣīṇam akaṭu-bimbīphala-yugalam asti \| (199)*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “On its northwest bank there is an amazing lotus creeper moving about. At the creeper’s central part are two long, attractive tumbi fruits. Above that are two full, sweet bimba fruits.”**

***viśākhā*** *– gosvāmin ! tvaritam eva tatra gatvā tva-yogya-tad-anuttama- phalopabhogena sādhvī-dhana-lobham apahāya sva-dharmaṁ pālayatā bhavatā parama-sukhinā bhūyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “O master of the senses! Quickly go there and relieve yourself of greed for the wealth of a chaste girl by enjoying the incomparable fruit suitable for you. You will become most happy by preserving your dharma.”**

*tataḥ sarvāsu hasantīṣu* ***mayoktaṁ*** *– lalite ! tapasy asāv ayācita-vṛttiḥ kara-pātrī bhakṣaṇe niṣiddhaṁ tumbīphalaṁ nopabhuṅkte \| kintu atraiva nirbhara-nija-pradyota-bhareṇa yā dṛśyamānāpi na dṛśyate \| tasyāḥ kāñcanavallyāḥ kroḍe madhura-rasa-maya-śrī-phala-dvandvaṁ yad vidyotate tat svayam eva saivāgatya praṇaya-pūrvakaṁ madhura-vacanenainaṁ nimantryāsya karo yadi pariveśayati, tadā tad-upabhogena sukhī bhavann asau tathāśiṣayati yathā tat-phalonnatir mahatī syāt \|*

**Перевод:**

**When everyone laughed, I said, “O Lalitā! The ascetic who vows to eat only a handful without begging cannot eat the forbidden tumbi fruit. However, if I can attain that person who has abundant effulgence but is not seen here, who has two śrī fruits filled with sweet juice on the lap of a golden vine, who comes when invited with sweet words, I can be happy enjoying that fruit. I will bless her so that her fruits will ripen abundantly.”**

*tatas tan niśamya* ***rādhā*** *anuccaiḥ – narma-lampaṭo’yaṁ dhūrtaḥ \| śaṅke vaidagdhyenātratyāṁ mad-avasthitim avadhārya mām eva kadarthayitum imāṁ vacana-bhaṅgīṁ vitanoti \| tad itaḥ kuñjāntaram āsādya sva-gopanam eva nūnam ucitam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Rādhā said softy, “The lusty joker is a cheater. I fear that detecting my hiding place here by his quick wit, he will use his clever words to torment me. I will go further within the bower to hide myself.”**

*iti manasi bhāvayantyāṁ tasyāṁ punar* ***mayoktaṁ*** *– priya-sakhi lalite ! ayaṁ vṛndāvana-cārī mahā-vinodī kṣuṇṇa-yūtheśāṁ tvāṁ paṭṭa-rājñīṁ vidhāya tvayā sahaika-siṁhāsa upaviśya viśākhā-prabhṛti-vṛndāvana-carīṇāṁ lāsya-vilāsam ālokayitum abhilaṣati tad ājñāpyatām etā nṛtyantu \|*

**Перевод:**

**As she thought thus, I again said, “O my dear friend Lalitā! I, the wanderer in Vṛndāvana, the great enjoyer, make you the queen of that defeated group leader. I desire to see the dancing of Viśākhā and others, sitting on my throne with you. Give the order and have them dance.”**

***lalitā*** *sa-krodhaṁ – nāndīmukhi ! asman-muktā-kedārikā-kara-parivartanena kim asya vidūṣakasya narma-dhanam eva dāpayituṁ vayam atra vyāghoṭyānītāḥ smaḥ ? tad etāṁ narmādi-gati-kriyām apahāya yathāsau subalena saha yathārthaṁ lekham ācarya kedārikā-karaṁ dadāti tathā vidhāyāsmān drutaṁ gṛhāya prasthāpaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said angrily, “O Nandīmukhī! The joker has brought us back here to pay a wealth of jokes, instead of giving the tax to us for our fields in which he planted his pearls. Give up the joking. With Subala he can write the correct amount he owes and then give the tax for the use of the fields. Then bidding us farewell, go home quickly.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– bhavatīnāṁ kiyān karaḥ saṁmataḥ syāt tat tāvat kathaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “What tax did you agree on? Please tell us.”**

***lalitā*** *– śyāmākādi-kṣetra-karato dhānyasyādhikaḥ karo lagati \| tato’pi kārpāsasya \| tato’pi vāstu-bhūmeḥ pracurataraḥ \| apūrvāmūlya-muktā-kedārasya tato’pi parārdha- guṇaḥ \| tad-alaukika-muktā-kedārikām imāṁ dharma-śāstrokta-vṛndāvana-rājya- vihitālaukika-māna-daṇḍena parimāya lekhaṁ vidhāya ca subala eva kathayatu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “The tax should be more on a grain field than the tax for a field of śyāmaka. The tax on a cotton field is more. The tax on land with buildings is more. The tax on fields of priceless pearls is a million times more. Subala should measure the extent of the astounding field of pearls using an astonishing measuring stick prescribed for measuring the kingdom of Vṛndāvana as mentioned in scriptures. Write that down and tell us.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *kiyat pramāṇaṁ sa khalu māna-daṇḍaḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “How long should the measuring stick be?”**

***lalitā –*** *mayā kathyamānaḥ sa khalu kena pratīyatāṁ \| tad etat kṣetra-pālikayā sarva- śāstrābhijñayā vṛndayaiva sa nirūpayitavyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Who will believe what I say? Vṛndā who protects the fields and is knowledgeable of all scriptures should say.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *bhadraṁ vṛnde ! madhyasthayā tvayaiva muktā-māna-daṇḍaḥ kṛtvā dīyatām\|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Yes, Vṛndā! Since you are neutral, make a stick for measuring pearl fields and give it to us.”**

***vṛndā devī*** *–*  
*anyatra ca –*  
*vāstu-dhānyādi-kaṅkvādi-kārpāsa-mauktika-kṣamāḥ \|*  
*mīyante’ṅguṣṭham ārabhya kramād aṅguli-pañcakaiḥ \|\| iti \|*  
*apūrva-muktā-kṣetrāṇām anarghya-karato budhaiḥ \|*  
*anāmikāṅguli-prāyo māna-daṇḍaḥ prakīrtitaḥ \|\| iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “Land with buildings, grains, grass, cotton and pearls are measured using respectively the thumb, and then the other fingers. It is also said that since pearl fields have immense value they are measured with the ring finger as the measuring stick.”**

***Lalitā –*** *kṣura-pramātra-khānita-bhūmer anāyāsenaivānargha-śasyotpādakatvāt kaniṣṭhaiva*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Since priceless grains are harvested from a field which has been ploughed, the little finger is used as measurement.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *lalite ! yady api tad eva yuktaṁ bhavati tathāpi vrajendra-kumāra- mukham ālokya bhagavatī-paricārikāṁ māṁ ca dṛṣṭvā anāmikaiva sthāpyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “O Lalitā! If that is correct, looking at Kṛṣṇa’s face and seeing me, the servant of Paurṇamāsī, establish the ring finger only.”**

***lalitā –*** *vṛnde ! tvaṁ tāval lekha-kriyāyāṁ māna-viṣaye ca nipuṇyāsi tat sarvābhiḥ sambhūya gatvā nāndīmukhī-subala-saṁmati-pūrvakaṁ kedārikāṁ parimīyāgamyatām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O Vṛndā! You are expert in writing and measuring. Taking everyone, let us go to the field with the consent of Nāndīmukhī and Subala.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *kṣuṇṇa-yūtheśvari ! ekaṁ prārthayitum icchāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndimukhī said, “Defeated leader! I have one request.”**

***lalitā –*** *kāmaṁ kathyatāṁ yogyaṁ cet kartavyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “Please speak. If it is reasonable, it shall be done.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *ayaṁ tāvat sva-deśaṁ vihāyātrāgato navīna-prāghunaḥ tatrāpi vṛndāvana-rājñīm āśrito’sti ihāpi bahv-āyāsenojjaṭa-bhūmiṁ kṛṣṭvā vaḥ prakāma-dhana-lābhaṁ vardhayann asti \| tasmān māne kṛte bhavatīnāṁ bhoga-rāgādi-vyayena bahu-hānyā punaḥ kṛṣi-karaṇo vimanaska iva bhaviṣyati \| kṛte’pi māne karaṁ dātum api na śakyate \| ato māna-karaṇaṁ vihāya nija-bhāgam ādāya tat-samucitāṁśam api pradāyāsya prakāmam utsāhaṁ vivardhayantu bhavatyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “This person has given up his land and come here. He is a new guest and has taken shelter of the queen of Vṛndāvana. With great labor he has ploughed the unkempt land and increased our wealth considerably. If you measure the land, because you must pay for food and condiments, the profit will be much less. If he measures the field, his cultivation will be disturbed. Thus, give up measuring the field and, taking your share, give him a fair portion. You will increase his enthusiasm considerably.”**

***vṛndā –*** *kīdṛśaṁ sa bhāgaḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “How much is his portion?”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *tvayā kiṁ na jñāyate ? yad asya paramojjvala-kṣetrasya samāna eva dvayor bhāgaḥ \| tathāyaṁ para-grāmād āgatya kṛṣi-vṛttiṁ kurvann āste \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “Do you know?” The most excellent field’s result is divided into two equal portions. Coming from another village, he has cultivated the field with that understanding.”**

***raṅgaṇamālā*** *anuccaiḥ – asau para-grāma-vāsī bhaumiko na syāt \| sāmprataṁ etasmin vane vāsam ācarya śrī-vṛndāvaneśākṛṣim ācarann asti \| tad asya ṣaṣṭho bhāga eva prāptavyo bhavati \| kathaṁ samānaṁ labhatām ?*

**Перевод:**

**Raṅgaṇamalā said softly, “He is not a farmer from another village. He now lives in this forest and farms for the queen of Vṛndāvana. He should take only one sixth of the total. Why should he take an equal share?”**

***viśākhā –*** *ayi mugdhe ! bhāga-nirṇaya-cālanayā kim asmākaṁ yato māna-pūrvakaṁ sarvadā kara-dānaṁ kartum eva mahā-rājñīnām ājñā-lekhaḥ samāgato’sti tat katham asmābhiḥ svātantryeṇaivaṁ kartuṁ śakyate ?*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “O fool! Since the written order of the queen has come to take the tax after measuring our land, why should we act independently?”**

*tato lalitā-viśākhayor mukham avalokya sva-karṇāvataṁsaṁ nāndīmukhyai netra- kūṇanena vṛndā darśitavatī \| tato’lpaṁ smitvā* ***nāndīmukhī*** *kiñcid upasṛtya vṛndayā saha utkoca-dāna-kathan-mudrābhinayena tayor īṣad iṅgitam evādhigamya mad- antikam āgatya sānucca-bhāṣaṁ – mohana ! vṛndāvana-rājñyāḥ sarvādhyakṣa-sacive khalu lalitā-viśākhe \| tad anayoḥ paramottamam utkocaṁ kam api dehi \| tadaiva tavābhimatam eva drutaṁ sampādayitavyam etābhyām \|*

**Перевод:**

**Looking at the faces of Lalitā and Viśākhā, and glancing at Nāndīmukhī, Vṛndā showed her earring to her. Smiling, Nāndīmukhī moved away slightly and, with Vṛndā, understanding the hint given by the two by a gesture, which indicated talk of giving bribes, she approached me. She said softly, “O enchanter! Lalitā and Viśākhā are the chief minsters of the queen of Vṛndāvana. Please give them some priceless bribe. Then they will fulfill your wish immediately.”**

***tadāhaṁ*** *sānandaṁ – sakhi nāndīmukhi ! yathā anyā na jānanti tathā ime eva nirjana- kuñje mat-samīpam ānīyetāṁ yathā etad abhīṣṭam utkocaṁ dattvā prīṇayāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said in bliss, “My friend Nāndīmukhī! Bring these two to a private bower without anyone knowing so that I can please them with their desired bribe.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– sundara ! etāḥ khalv anayor abhinnā eva tad asaṅkocaṁ prakaṭam atraiva dehi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “O beautiful Kṛṣṇa! The other gopīs are non-different from the two of them. Without reserve, give the bribe in public, right here.”**

***tato’haṁ*** *– svābhimatārtha-lābhena vinā pūrvam eva kathaṁ dātavyam \| yadi mayy apratītiḥ syāt tarhi tvayy eva sthāpayāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Without attaining my desired object, why should I give anything? If you do not trust me, I will keep my bribe with you.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *sa-śiraś-cālanaṁ – nahi nahi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī shaking her head said, “No, no!”**

***tato’haṁ*** *– āṁ brahmacāriṇyās tu viṣaya-sparśe kālūṣyam iti cet tadā mat-pratīti- pātryāṁ sādhvī-pravarāyāṁ raṅgaṇamālikāyām eva tad arpayāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “If brahamcāriṇīs become contaminated by touching material objects, then I will offer it to Raṅgaṇamallikā, the best of chaste women, whom I trust.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– rasika-śekhara ! prathamaṁ tāvat śrāvaya kiṁ vā kiyad vā dātavyam \| tena tāvataiva vānayoḥ santoṣo bhaven na veti nirdhārayāmaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “King of enjoyers! First, say how much is to be given. We will decide if that is acceptable to Lalitā and Viśākhā.”**

*tadā* ***mayoktaṁ*** *– bhavatu \| śrūyatām \| vṛndāvana-rājasya mama vana-pālanam apahāya dhana-lobhena mad-rājñīṁ rādhikām anusṛteyaṁ vṛndā \| tat prathamam utkoca-dānena kāyasthām enām ātmano vaśāṁ vidadhāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Let that be. Please listen. Giving up protecting the forests on my behalf, this Vṛndā has followed Rādhā, my queen, out of greed for wealth, though I am king of the forest. First, by giving a bribe, I will bring this mercantile person Vṛndā under my control.”**

***nāndīmukhī –*** *bhadram etat \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “That is fine.”**

***tato’haṁ –*** *carama-śarvaryāṁ paramākalpa-kalpanācāryayā gāḍhānurāga-vihvalayā gāndharvyā yena mad-vakṣo-gaganam ākalpitaṁ tenaiva niravadyārdha-candra- padaka-rājena sva-hastenaināṁ bhūṣayāmi \| tataḥ kaustubhād apy adhika- dedīpyamānenātula-tat-sahodara-mac-cumbaka-maṇinā yad gāndharvayā praguṇa- paraspara-praṇayoddhura-keli-kautukena bakula-rāja-tale parivartitaṁ apūrva-rasa- samudra-saṁloḍanāt samudbhūta-ghanībhūta-tat-sārāṁśa-rūpaṁ man-maṇito’py anarghyaṁ sva-cumbaka-mahā-ratnaṁ tat-tad-advaita-dayitam api lalitāyai dāsyāmi \| yathāsphuṭam asau tena sva-karṇābhyarṇam alaṁkaromi \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “With my hand I will decorate Lalitā with the king of half-moon faultless pendants, which was placed on the sky of my chest by Rādhā, the topmost woman, the ācārya of supreme ornamentation, agitated by deep anurāga. I will give to Lalitā Rādhā’s great, cumbaka gem, most adored by Rādhā, more valuable than all my other gems. It is the concentrated essence arising from the churning of the ocean of astonishing rasa, and was exchanged at the base of the king of bakula trees by Rādhā for my unequalled cumbaka gem, its brother, which shining brighter than the Kaustubha during unrestrained, amusing exchange of affection.”**

*tataḥ punaḥ punar viśākhā-vadanam īṣad avaloky****āhaṁ*** *sa-smitam avadam – anukṣaṇa-paramānurāga-bhareṇa sva-karābhyāṁ suṣṭhu viracayya sva-kuṇḍa- kuḍaṅgāṅgane etat priya-sakhī gāndharvā suvaidagdhyena yaṁ mahyam arpitavatī \| tayaiva vicitrāṅka-mālayā mad-dhṛdayākāśa-viśākhāṁ viśākhām apy alaṅkṛtya paritoṣayāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Looking at Viśākhā’s face again and again, I said with a smile, “I will satisfy and decorate Viśākhā, the constellation in the sky of my heart, with a colorful garland of marks, which Rādhā offered to me with great skill in the yard of a bower at Rādhā-kuṇḍa, after making it with her two hands because of continual anurāga.”**

*tac chrutvā – alīka-rājendra ! tiṣṭha tiṣṭheti vyāharantī rādhā manasaiva līlā-kamalena māṁ tāḍayati sma \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Rādhā said mentally, “O king of lying! Stop! Stop!” She mentally struck me with a play lotus.**

***lalitā*** *– padmādhara-rasāhiphenāśana-pramatta ! apehi apehi !*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “You have become mad because of drinking the snake poison from the lips of Padmā. Go away! Go away!”**

***viśākhā*** *– ārya vidūṣaka-pravara madhumaṅgala ! bhavad-vayasyaḥ kiṁ bhavato’pi guruḥ kiṁ vā tasya bhavān gurur ity avagantuṁ sarvāḥ samabhilaṣanti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “O best of jesters, Madhumaṅgala! Is your friend your guru or are you his guru? Everyone wants to know.”**

***lalitā*** *– viśākhe ! śrūyatām \| kapaṭa-nāṭakasya yaḥ kusumaśara-nāmā naṭas tasya suprasiddho yaḥ śuci-nāmā vidūṣakas tenāyaṁ dhūrtaḥ śiṣyatvena kṛpayānugṛhīto’sti \| madhumaṅgalas tu bhinna-sampradāyi-bhojana-lampaṭa-nāmno vidūṣakācāryasya prathīyān śiṣyaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “O Viśākhā! Please listen. The dancer called Kusuma-śara (flower arrow, Cupid), in the drama of deceit, has a jester called Śuci (love) who is famous. This jester has made this rascal his disciple by his mercy. Madhumaṅgala is the disciple of the ācārya of jesters called Bhojana-lampata (lusty eater) in a different sampradāya.”**

***viśākhā*** *– lalite ! tad enaṁ brāhmaṇaṁ miṣṭānnaṁ bhojayitum icchāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “O Lalitā! I want to feed sweets to this brāhmaṇa.”**

***lalitā*** *– viśākhe ! asau karma-sūcakaḥ paramānūcāno mahā-brāhmaṇaḥ \| tat katham asmākaṁ brāhmaṇetara-gopa-jātīnāṁ rāddham annam aśnātu \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā said, “He is a great brāhmaṇa, teacher of rituals, well versed in the Vedas. How can he eat food cooked by cowherds, not brāhmaṇas?”**

***viśākhā*** *– paramottama-dvijābhyāṁ mallī-bhṛṅgābhyāṁ sva-hastenānnaṁ saṁskṛtya paramādareṇa yathāsau bhojyate tathaiva sampādanīyam \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “Mallī and Bhṛṅgī, the highest twice born (or with the most beautiful teeth) can purify it with their hands so that the food can be eaten by him with great respect. This is how it should be done.”**

*tatas tac chrutvā krodhena kampamāna iva* ***madhumaṅgalaḥ*** *prāha – aye avadyavādini garvitābhīrike ! etad duḥśravaṇa-narmotkaṭa-kaṭu-lavaṇa-digdha-dagdhārdha- paryuṣita-vāg-godhūma-roṭikāḥ saṁbhojya mama madhura-karṇāv eva suṣṭhu kaṭūkṛtau, kiṁ punar mukha-vivaram \| tad vidūṣaka-gopa-vadhūnāṁ yuṣmākaṁ*  
*chāyā-nikaṭa-bhūmim api kadāpi na spṛśāmi \| kintu prātar evāsmaj-jāti-yājñika-vadhū- vargam upasarpiṣyāmi \| tatas tena paramādareṇa mac-caraṇa-kṣālana-pūrvakaṁ kauśeya-dukūle paridhāpya sakarpūra-vāsitopalāpānaka -madhura-paramānna- sasaindhavārdraka-limpāka-nānā-vidha-vyañjana-veṣṭita-saghṛta-sugandhi-śālyanna- phāṇita-śaṣkulī-kuṇḍalikā-laḍḍuka-ghanāvartita-dugdha-rasālā-saśarkara-baddha- dadhi-vividha-baṭakottama-marīcādibhis tathāhaṁ bhojayiṣye \| yathā vartmani succhāya-taru-mūleṣu svapan sva-gṛham āgatya saṅgavāvadhi svapann eva tiṣṭhāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, Madhumaṅgala as if trembling in anger said, “Shameful speaker! Proud cowherd woman! Eating the burned wheat chappatis of your half rotten words, smeared with bitter salt of insane jokes painful to hear, my sweet ears have become completely bitter. What to speak of my mouth! I will never touch the earth near the shadow of you clownish cowherd women. Tomorrow I will go to the wives of the brāhmaṇas of my caste. After they wash my feet with great reverence and dress me in a silk shawl, I will be fed drinks scented with camphor, sweet rice, aromatic rice served with the best ghee, surrounded by various vegetables, accompanied by sea salt, ginger and lemon slices, sugar cane juice, śaṣkulī, kuṇḍalikā, laḍdus, condensed milk, grapes, yogurt mixed with sugar candy, various deep fried balls, and black pepper. After napping under a tree on the path, I will return home and sleep till late afternoon.”**

*tatas tac-chravaṇena hāsa-kolāhale vṛtte* ***mayoktaṁ*** *– nāndīmukhi ! kṣudra-gāminair eva sva-sva-grāma-sīmārthaṁ madhyastham ālambya nyāyaḥ kriyate \| rājānas tu rājyaṁ sva-dordaṇḍa-balenaiva labhante \| tad etad-rājya-hetor nyāyena kim ? mayā sahaitāḥ samaram ācarantu \| tatra yasya jayo bhavet tasyaiva rājyaṁ sphuṭaṁ setsyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this, when the laughter dissipated, I said, “O Nāndīmukhī! Small villagers seek justice by using the legal system in order to determine the boundaries of their village. Kings however obtain their land by the strength of their arms. What is the use of this legal system for determining this kingdom? They should fight with me. Whoever gains victory will clearly gain the kingdom.”**

*ity uktvā tad-artham iva sāṭopaṁ kiñcid upasarpati mayi tāsu ca itas tataḥ sa-bhaya- sahelanam apasarpantīṣu* ***nāndīmukhī*** *– vīra ! sampraty asmat-samakṣam etāsām ujjvala-kula-vilasita-ballava-vadhūnāṁ dharṣaṇam etad bhavataḥ param asāmpratam eva \|*  
*ity avadhārayann ārād eva tiṣṭheti mām ābhāṣya lalitāṁ praty uvāca – lalite \| sāmpratam asya vana-vīrasya nirjana-vane’smin pragalbhatā-mahatī bhavatīnāṁ tu śirīṣa-kusuma-mṛdulāni śarīrāṇi, tad deśa-kāla-balādikaṁ vīkṣya vivādaṁ tyaja \|*

**Перевод:**

**Saying this, I advanced a little for that purpose with pride. They fled here and there in fear and contempt. Nāndīmukhī said, “O hero! It is very improper of you to violate these cowherd women born from pure families.”**  
**“Stay in the distance,” she said and then spoke to Lalitā. “O Lalitā! Now, the hero of the forest has great strength in this lonely forest. You have tender śiriṣa flower bodies. Seeing the place, time and strength, give up quarrelling.”**

*tac chrutvā* ***candramukhī*** *– bho mugdhāḥ ! nāndīmukhī satyaṁ kathayati \| tac chṛṇudhvam \| vayam abalāḥ komalāṅgyaḥ \| ayaṁ tu nirjana-vana-puruṣo’ticapalaḥ \| yasyā darśanād evāyaṁ sādhvasena vihvalo bhavati \| sāpy asmac-cakravartinī nāntika- vartinī \| sudurmukhābhimanyu-prabhṛti-vāhinī-patayo’pi sampraty etad-vṛttānabhijñā dūre vartante \| tad-bhayānaka-deśe’sminn ātma-madhye dvandve patite sarvataḥ sarve luṇṭāka-duṣṭa-gamakāḥ samutthāya samasta-mauktikāni viluṇṭhya neṣyanti \| tadāsmākam eva mahatī hānir bhaviṣyati \| asya tu ṣaṣṭhāṁśa eva tato’lpa-hāniḥ \| tad yadi sarvābhyo rocate tarhi vayaṁ saumyā iva bhavatyaḥ samprati rājya-vārtām apahāya samucitād apy adhika-mūlyena mauktikāny ādāya asya cillātakasya sparśa-mātreṇaiva janiṣyamāṇa-duṣkīrti-bharād ātmānam api saṁrakṣya sva-gṛham āsādayāmaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Candramukhī said, “O innocent girls! Nāndīmukhī speaks correctly. You should listen to her. We are young, with tender bodies. He is a fickle male, alone in the forest. Seeing the queen, he becomes agitated with fear, but your queen is not close now. The husbands like ugly-faced Abhimanyu are far away, unaware of this situation. While we quarrel among ourselves in this fearsome forest, some wicked thieves may come, steal all the pearls and go away. We will incur a great loss. There is little loss for him, only a sixth. If it pleases everyone, we should give up talk of the kingdom now, take the pearls at a higher, fair price, and return to our homes, protecting ourselves from the being defamed seriously by the touch of that thief.**

*paścād etad-vṛttānta-śravaṇād eva vṛndāvana-mahārājñī tan-muktā-pradānena drutam eva guru-prabhṛtīn santoṣya mahārāgeṇātivegena svayam atrāgatya dūrata eva kapaṭāṭopam avaṣṭabhya svayaṁ samaram akurvāṇā cañcala-nayanāñcala-pravaṇayā nīta-tīkṣṇādhāra-viṣama-śarāstra-saṁvardhita-bhrūkuṭi-dhanurdhara-sumukha-kañjana-bhīṣaṇābhimanyu-prabhṛti-vāhinī-pati-dvāraiva tathā vigraha-bhaṅgīm āṭopayiṣyati \| yathāyaṁ vana-madhya eva vīraṁ manyas tvaritam eva sādhvasena kampamānaḥ san bṛṁhita-hāheti-kamala-rāga-maṇi-prakara-khacita-cāru-cāṭu-cintā-ratna-nikara-racita-mañjula-mālāṁ nija-kaṇṭhād uttāryopaḍhaukitīkṛtya tac-caraṇa-parisaraṁ śaraṇam upagacchann etat-samudita-gadgada-gadita-khara-dyuti-lavena tat-kāla-druta-citta-navanītayā tayā kāruṇyena prasādī-kṛtenaiva tad-yāvakāruṇa-maṇi-draveṇābhinava-śikhaṇḍāvataṁsaṁ śoṇam iva racayann etad-rājyaṁ tad-utpanna-nikhila-mauktikādikam api samarpya svayam api tām evānucariṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**“Hearing about this later, the queen of Vṛndāvana, after quickly satisfying the elders by giving the pearls, will come here personally at great speed in great anger. Situating herself at a distance with feigned pride, she will engage in battle with him. She will make war using her generals--terrifying Abhimanyu and beautiful Cupid--having frowning brows for bows, with a harsh reservoir of rough arrows fired by the indications from the edges of her fickle eyes. Though thinking himself a hero, in that forest he will suddenly tremble in fear, crying out “Ha! Ha!” Taking from his neck his attractive necklace made of pleasing, beautiful cintāmaṇi gems and inlaid with various rubies, he will gift it to her and surrender to her lotus feet. He will turn his peacock feather ornament red from the liquefied ruby lac from her feet, made of her mercy, since her heart will melt like butter at that time, because of a small number of burning rays from his choked voice. Offering her the kingdom and all the pearls, he will serve her.”**

*tac-chravaṇena sānandollāsa-sasmitaṁ rādhā-kuñjaṁ tiryag avalokayati mayi*  
***nāndīmukhī*** *smitvā – gokula-pravīra ! tvad-anurūpa-viṣama-śara-samara-pravīṇayā vṛndāvana-cakravartinyā samam eva bhavat-samara-paripāṭī paraṁ śobhate \| etāḥ punar atīva komalās tad-abhyarṇam antareṇa tvayā saha tādṛśa-vigraha-vilāsaṁ katham ācarantu tad alīka-virodhaṁ parityajya sāmpratam anyāsām api mauktikam amūlyaṁ nirṇīyatāṁ paścād bhagavaty eva rājya-nyāyaṁ vicārayiṣyati \|*

**Перевод:**

**Smiling in bliss, I glanced at Rādhā’s bower. Nāndīmukhī said with smile, “O hero of Gokula! Your skill in battle becomes most glorious in fighting with the queen of Vṛndāvana, who is as skillful at war with rough arrows as you are. How can the very soft gopīs perform such pastimes of battle with you without her presence? Give up this false fighting and decide now the price of the pearls of the others also. Later Paurṇamāsī will decide the judgment concerning the kingdom.”**

*tatas tāsāṁ sākūta-vacanam avadhāryaiva garvita-gopyo jitā jitā iti vadan sva-mukhe vāma-muṣṭiṁ bherīkṛtya vādayan madhumaṅgalaḥ sānandaṁ tāṇḍavam ātanoti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing their meaningful words, Madhumaṅgala said, “The proud gopīs have been defeated!” Making sounds like a drum with his left fist, he danced in bliss.**

***vṛndā*** *– bho naṭa-pravara madhumaṅgala ! asmac-cakravartinyām atrāyātāyām api tad-āhlādanārtham ātma-priya-sakhasya hāheti-ḍhakkā-vādyena śikṣā-vaiśiṣṭyena ṣaṭ- pado bhavan uḍḍīyoḍḍīya bhramarikām ādadānaḥ sphuṭam ito raṅgād asmād adhikam uddaṇḍaḥ tāṇḍavayann anena dvipada-gopena sārdhaṁ sugahanaṁ nandīśvara- goṣṭham āpsyasi \| tadānīṁ tad-avalokanād vayam api cakṣuḥ-sāphalyam utsphārayiṣyāmaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Vṛndā said, “Best of dancers, Madhumaṅgala! In the presence of our queen you will learn a lesson and shout “Oh no!” for her pleasure. Assuming the form of a bee, flying here and there, dancing and keeping time with a louder beat, you will leave our place and arrive at a thicket in Nandīśvara with your two-footed cowherd. Seeing that, our eyes would expand in pleasure.”**

***tato’haṁ*** *vihasya – nāndīmukhi ! seyaṁ candramukhī sāmañjasya-ratā lalitādivad dvandva-pātinī na bhavati \| ato vinā mūlyenāpy asyai santuṣṭena mayā mauktikāni deyāni \| kintv iyaṁ mantra-vidāṁ mūrdhanyā tataḥ śvaḥ paraśvo vā parama-śuciḥ satī rahaḥ sthānam āgatya snānādinā parama-śucaye kāntadarpābhidhācārya-nirukta- mantra-paṭalaṁ mahyam upadiśatu \| yatheha vṛndāvane gopenaiva mayā surādhikā- śrī-drutam eva labhyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**I laughed and said, “O Nāndīmukhī! This Candramukhī, engaged in conciliation, does not create dissension, unlike Lalitā. I will give her pearls for free. Since she is the most knowledgeable of mantras and purest, she should go to a solitary place, and having me bathe, should teach me many mantras declared by the ācārya Cupid, so that I, the cowherd, can attain beauty greater than a devatā. (so I can attain the beauty of Rādhā quickly).**

*tataś* ***candramukhī*** *kuṭilaṁ mām avalokayanty āha – aho mām api hitopadeśinīṁ kṛtānabhijñas tvaṁ kadarthayitum udyato’si ? nāhaṁ tava mantrābhijñā tan-mantra- caturā kāñcana-latair ācāryā kriyatāṁ bhavatā \|*

**Перевод:**

**Candramukhī said, looking at me with a frown, “Being ungrateful, you harass me, though I am giving you good instructions. I am not your knower of mantras. Kañcana-latā, skillful at mantras, should act as your ācārya.”**

***tato’haṁ*** *– kāñcanalate ! bhavad-vaidagdhyāvalokanena man-mano-bhramaras tvayi prakāmam anurajyann atīvotkaṇṭhate tat-parama-sundaratārādhikā bhavad- upakaṇṭha-vartinī mañjulataraikāvalir ekā sasmitam ālokayantīnām āsāṁ samakṣam eva paramotkaṇṭhite mad-urasi sneha-bhareṇa svayam eva tvayā yady adhīyate tarhi mūlyam antareṇāpi bhavad-abhīṣṭa-mauktikāny avaśyaṁ vitariṣyāmi \| api ca mat- pariṣvaṅga-lalita-ratna-hāra-trayeṇa bhavat-kaṇṭha-madhya-nābhi-pradeśān alaṁ prasādhayiṣyāmīti tām anusarati mayi sā māṁ tiryag-avalokayantī huṁ kurvatī kiñcic apasasāra \| rādhā ca sasmitaṁ kurvatī ātmani śaṅkām ādhāya sambhramam avāpa \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said “O Kañcanalatā! Seeing your cleverness, the bee of my mind is very attracted to you. If you place the very beautiful single-stranded necklace which I greatly covet that is situated on your neck upon my chest with affection while others watch, I will give you the pearls you desire without a price. I will give you my three attractive jeweled necklaces which embrace me, and then decorate places from your navel to your neck.” I moved towards her. She glanced at me obliquely and, saying “Hum,” drew away. Rādhā smiled, but, worrying about herself, became confused.**

*tato* ***viśākhā*** *tarjany-aṅguṣṭha-choṭikayā nāndīmukhīm abhimukhīkṛtya netra- kūṇanena raṅgaṇamālikā-tulasike darśitavatī \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā, snapping her forefinger, faced Nāndīmukhī and indicated Raṅganamālikā and Tulasī with her glance.**

***nāndīmukhī*** *vihasya – mohana ! ime rādhikā-priya-caraṇa-tat-pare tad-atīva-priye raṅgaṇamālikā-tulasike tāṁ vinā kṣaṇam api kutrāpy avasthātuṁ na śaknuvatas tad anayor muktā-mūlyaṁ nirṇīya tūrṇam ete tad-antike prasthāpaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī laughed and said, “O bewilderer! Raṅgaṇamālikā and Tulasī, dedicated to the lotus feet of Rādhā and dearest to her, cannot remain without her for a moment. Decide the price of their pearls and quickly send them off to her.”**

***tato’haṁ*** *sāntarānandaṁ vihasya – nāndīmukhi ! tulasyā adṛṣṭacara-cañcala- nayanāñcalāvaloka-lava-maricika-kṣoda-miśrita-susmita-nava-ghanasāra-lava-*  
*parimilitāśruta-cara-nava-nava-bhāṣita-makaranda-pāyanena vihvalīkṛtam iva māṁ sneha-vihvalā raṅgaṇamālikā mad-urasi nija-kuca-kuṭmalābhyām avaṣṭabhya maj- jīvātu-rūpa-svādhara-pīyūṣa-pāyanena drutaṁ sandhukṣayatv iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**With internal bliss I smiled and said, “O Nāndīmukhī! I have been agitated by drinking the fragrant honey of Tulasī’s unique, fresh speech mixed with a particle of camphor of her smile and a particle of pepper of her unique, fickle glance. Raṅgaṇamālikā, agitated with great affection for me, should establish herself on my chest with her two bud-like breasts and revive me quickly by letting me drink the nectar of her lips which give me life.”**

*tataḥ sarvāsu hasantīṣv avanata-mukhyau te viśākhā-pṛṣṭha-lagne babhūvatuḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**When everyone laughed, with bowed heads the two withdrew behind Viśākhā.**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– muktāphala-vāṇijya-vilāsin ! yūtheśayor api rādhā-viśākhayor mauktika-mūlya-nirṇaye yan mano na dhatse \| tatra kiṁ kāraṇam ?*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “You are addicted to selling pearls. Why do you not think of deciding the price of the pearls of the leader of our group and Viśākhā?”**

***tato’haṁ*** *– sā yūtheśā mat-savidham āgatya cet svayaṁ nirṇayati \| tadaiva nirṇayanīyam \| tad-agocare kena nirṇīyatām ?*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “If the leader comes to me and bargains herself, I will decide. Without seeing her how can I decide?**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– vīra! mūlyaṁ tāvat prakāśaya \| yathā tac-chravaṇena tan-mūlya- dravyāṇāṁ samāhṛtis tayā kriyate \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “O hero! Say the price so that by hearing that, she can gather the correct amount.”**

***mayoktaṁ*** *– rādhā-viśākhayor advaitād dvayor api mad-atīva-priyayor yat kiñcin mūlyaṁ kathyate tac chrūyatām \| mat-pṛṣṭha-mañjulatara-tamāla-saṁvalita- masṛṇatara—dakṣiṇa-savya-bhujā-svarṇa-latayor gāndharvikā-viśākhayor lalita- natāṁsa-nihita-vilāsollasita-parimilita-supracaṇḍa-dor-daṇḍa-yugalasya surabhi- kusuma-kula-parivāsita-vana-vihāra-mādhurīm anusṛtasya parasparam aviṣama- nirupama-prema-kalāpāvalokana-kautukena mama gaṇḍa-raṅga-sthale tan-madhura- vadana-sudhākara-pravīṇa-naṭa-pravarau yugapat pṛthag vā mṛdu mṛdu tāṇḍavaṁ samullāsayantau mad-ānanda-kandaṁ kandalayatām \|*  
*kiṁ ca, rādhā-kuṇḍa-taṭī-kuḍaṅga-bhavanāṅgane bhramad-bhramara-jhaṅkṛti- parimilita-surabhi-kusuma-vṛnda-syandamāna-makaranda-mañjula-bakula-talāmala- kanaka-vedikāyāṁ mañjula-malli-kusuma-dala-kula-kalpita-praśasta-sukumāra- talpopari svarṇa-yūthikā-kusuma-kṛta-candropadhāne vāma-kaphoṇim avaṣṭabhya saṅkucita-jānu-dvayaṁ sukhopaviṣṭasya man-mano-madhupāśraya-vāsantyā viśākhayā svarṇa-sampuṭa-sthita-kuṅkuma-rasaṁ kiñcit ślatham ivālokayantyā sva-sakhya- praṇaya-madhūnmādena nistala-nija-vakṣoja-yugalād ākṛṣṭa-dara-kaṭhinībhūta- surabhi-kuṅkuma-paṅka-milanād īṣad ghanīkṛtya tena mṛdu mṛdu rūṣite mama pulakita-vakṣasi mat-prāṇa-pañjara-śārikā rādhikā kadācit svasya kadācit viśākhāyāḥ kuca-sampuṭa-vikṛṣṭa-nava-mṛgamada-draveṇa saromāñcaṁ campaka-kalikāgreṇa vallari-makarāṅkura-patrāṇi praguṇākalpa-kalpanayā kalpayantī nirbhara- parasparāsamānordhva-sauhārda-saurabhya-bhareṇa camatkṛti-valita-mad-deha- mano-vākyāni paraṁ parivāsayatv iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “Hear the small price that I declare for Rādhā and Viśākhā who are one entity, and extremely dear to me. May the two expert dancers in the form of their sweet moonlike faces, together or separately, dance softly with enthusiasm on the arena of my cheek and increase my abundant bliss with mutual joyful glances of abundant, incomparable, pure prema, as I seek out the sweetness of pastimes in a forest perfumed with fragrant flowers, while my two broad arms, eager for pastimes, rest on the delicate, bowed shoulders of Rādhā and Viśākhā, with the golden creepers of their tender right and left arms on my beautiful tāmala tree back.**  
**“In the yard of the house in the bower on the bank of Rādhā-kuṇḍa, on a spotless golden dais at the foot of an attractive bakula tree streaming with honey from countless fragrant flowers buzzing with wandering bees, on a soft, excellent bed made of delicate jasmine petals, placing my left elbow on a moon-shaped pillow made of golden yūthika flowers, I sit comfortably with my two knees bent. Viśākhā, who is the abode and shelter for the bee of my mind, seeing that the kuṅkuma on Rādhā’s golden chest is somewhat thin, makes it a little thicker by mixing the fragrant kuṅkuma paste made slightly hard, taken from her own round breasts, having become mad for the sweetness of her friend’ love. She spreads that mixture gently on my chest, while my hairs stand on end. Making patterns of makara and creepers with the tip of a campaka bud, with fresh musk taken from the casket of her own or Viśākhā’s breasts, with unprecedented imagination, Rādhā, the śārikā bird in the cage of my life airs, should scent my body, mind and words filled with astonishment, with the strong fragrance of strong, unequalled, mutual friendship.”**

*ato rādhā-viśākhe sa-snehaṁ yugapat supulaka-paraspara-cātur-akṣika-vīkṣaṇena lajjite babhūvatuḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā and Viśākhā simultaneously felt shame, while affectionately glancing at each other with four eyes with hairs standing on end.**

***viśākhā*** *– lalite ! lampaṭatā-nāṭye tal-lampaṭa-naṭābhyām asambhava-manoratha-nāma-nāṭakasya vidhīyamānābhinaya-vīkṣaṇāya nirjana-vana-raṅge’smin yathārtha- nāmnī nāndīmukhīyaṁ mauktika-pradāna-nimittaka-vitathā-vāk-prastutyāsmān sabhāsadaḥ kartuṁ saṁrakṣya prakāreṇa viḍambayitum udyuktāsti \| tad atra raṅge yasyā raṅgo vidyate sā kila kulavatī sādhvī sphuṭam asya catuḥṣaṣṭhī-kalā-vidagdhasya*  
*naṭasya lāsyam atropaviśya paśyantī bhadreṇa kula-dvayam utkīrtayatu ahaṁ tu gṛhaṁ gacchāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**Viśākhā said, “O Lalitā! Nāndīmukhī, using false words about giving pearls, had simply fooled us, keeping us here at this meeting in this solitary forest in order to see the gestures of the actor called Possessor of Impossible Desires in a lusty play along with his two lusty actors. This chaste woman of good family, who is amused in this arena, sits there looking at the dancing of the actor skillful in the sixty-four arts. Let her glorify the two sides of her family with this auspicious act. I am going home.”**

*tato* ***nāndīmukhī*** *–sakhi viśākhe ! asya narma-karmaṭha-śīlasya durlīlasya vacana- vilāsa-mātreṇaiva kathaṁ nirvidya khidyase ? kṣaṇaṁ tiṣṭha \| niḥsandeham adhunaiva muktā dāpayiṣyanty asmi iti tām ābhāsya nivartya ca mat-savidham āsādya māṁ prāha*  
*– durlīla gopa-yuvarāja ! bhavad-visadṛśa-narmālāpa-śravaṇena viśākhādayo mad- upari śaśvat khidyante \| tat kṛpayā samprati narma-karma-pañjikā-prapañcaṁ saṁrakṣya kraya-vikraya-pañjikām udghāṭya draviṇādi-mūlyena muktāḥ pradāya dhruvam amūr bhavat-snigdha-gāndharvā-camūs tvaritam anurañjaya \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī said, “My friend Viśākhā! Why are you so distressed and pained by the playful words of this deceitful person who likes joking? Wait a moment. Without doubt, soon I will arrange that you receive the pearls.”**  
**Speaking to Viśākhā, she made her return and, approaching me, Nāndīmukhī said, “Mischievous prince of the cowherds! Because of hearing your inappropriate jokes, Viśākhā and others continually harass me. Now you should put aside the compendium of jokes and pulling out the compendium on buying and selling, give the pearls for a price, and quickly delight the army of Rādhā, whom you love.”**

***tato’haṁ*** *– nāndīmukhi ! yadyapi rādhā mayi kāṭhinyam eva santatam ātanoti, tathāpi sahaja-snigdhasya mama manas tu tan-nāma-mātra-śravaṇād eva nāṅgam eva samastaṁ drutam anusandhatte tadānu tat-sahacarīṣu kāṭhinyena kim ? tato dina- dvayābhyantare yāvan nirṇīta-mūlyam upasthāpayanti \| tāvad eva tat- suvarṇālaṅkāraṇādi-raupyādi-rasādi-priya-gavādikaṁ dhanaṁ mayi sthāpyatayā mayi saṁrakṣya etad-anurūpa-kiyan-mauktikāni gṛhṇantu \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O Nāndīmukhī! Though Rādhā is always hard on me, since I have natural affection, my mind, on just hearing her name, immediately seeks out everything about her. Why should I be harsh with her followers? After the price is decided, within two days, for whatever wealth--gold, ornaments, silver, butter and dear cows—is brought and kept with me, they may take the appropriate amount of pearls.”**

*ity ābhāṣya punaḥ kṣaṇaṁ maunena vimṛśyāha – nāndīmukhi ! santata-dayita- gocāraṇa-līlayā vane vane bhramatā mayaitat sarvaṁ kutra rakṣitavyam \| pratīti- pātram api ko’pi na dṛśyate \| rakṣite ca para-ramaṇī-dravye lajjāpakīrtito mahad bhayaṁ labhyate \| tat satyam ucyate prastuta-mūlyaṁ vinā etat kim api na sampadyata eva \|*

**Перевод:**

**After saying this I thought for a moment silently and then said aloud, “O Nāndīmukhī! Since I wander from forest to forest to engage in my cherished pastime of herding cows, where will I keep all this wealth? I do not see a trustworthy person who can keep the wealth for me. If I keep possessions belonging to other’ wives, I have great fear of defamation and embarrassment. But in truth, I cannot give the pearls without the above mentioned price.”**

***nāndīmukhī*** *– mohana ! etad apūrva-mūlyaṁ kutrāpi na dṛṣṭaṁ na vā śrutam asti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndimukhī said, “O bewilderer! Such an unprecedented price has never been seen or heard of at any place.”**

***tato’haṁ*** *– vidagdhe nāndīmukhi ! īdṛśam etad bhūmi-jātāpūrva-mauktikaṁ tvayā brahmāṇḍe kutrāpi dṛṣṭacaraṁ śrutacaraṁ vā asti ? tad-apūrva-padārthasya mūlyam apy apūrvam \| tatrāpi na vayaṁ muktā-vyāpāriṇaḥ kintu kevalaṁ bhagavatī-pādānām ādeśena bhavad-āgraheṇa cātra pravartitāḥ smaḥ \| tasmāc ced icchā syāt tarhi mitho nirdhārita-mūlyaṁ prastutaṁ dattvā parama-caturā etā mauktikāni gṛhṇantu \| no ced gṛhaṁ gacchantu \| paśya madhyāhna-prāyo divaso jātas tad vayam api godhana- sambhālanāya govardhanaṁ gacchāmaḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**I said, “O clever Nāndīmukhī! Have you ever seen or heard of such astonishing pearls arising from the earth anywhere? The price of such astonishing goods is also astonishing. I am not a pearl merchant but have started this endeavor on the order of Paurṇamāsī and on your insistence. Therefore, if they desire, we can mutually decide on the price and, giving that amount, the intelligent gopīs can take the pearls. If not, they may go home. Look! It is almost noon. I must go to Govardhana to herd the cows.”**

*tato* ***nāndīmukhī*** *sanirvedam iva lalitāntikam upetya anuccaiḥ – sakhi lalite ! sakhi viśākhe ! bhoḥ sarvāḥ priyasakhyaḥ ! nijābhiṣṭaitan mūlya-labdhiṁ vinā haṭhī nāgaro ‘yaṁ muktā na dāsyaty eveti mayoparodhatvena nirdhāritam asti. Mauktikāny avaśayam eva grāhyāṇi ato mayaitan mantryate yad asminn eva mūlye kenāpi vyājena kiñcin mātra sammati-pradānenāmum santoṣya mauktikaṇy ādāya gṛhe gate ko nāma kasya mūlyaṁ gṛhnātu ko vā dadātu*

**Перевод:**

**Nāndīmukhī, with apparent regret, approached Lalitā and said softly, “O my friend Lalitā! My friend Viśākhā! O all dear friends! Without giving the desired price, the bold lecher will not give the pearls. I have concluded this by the trouble he is causing. We must get the pearls. Therefore I suggest that satisfying him by just agreeing to his price, while using some trickery, we should take the pearls and go home. Then you may decide on a price and give something.”**

*tataḥ adbhuta-tapasvini ! tiṣṭha tiṣṭha iti nāndīmukhīm ākṣipya māṁ prati* ***lalitā***  
*smitvā – dhīra-lalita yuvarāja ! apūrvā vṛndāvana-kedārikā apūrvam etan mauktika-bījam apūrvāṇi mauktikāni apūrvo ‘yaṁ mauktika-vikrayī vaṇik apūrvam etan mūlyaṁ na vayam apūrvā bhavāmas tat katham asmākam etad apūrva-mūlya-pradāne sāmarthyaṁ bhavatu kintu nāndīmukhīyaṁ tāvad asmāsv atīva snigdhā asmad abhinnā ca tvaṁ ca tāvad apūrva-brahmacārī tasmād apūrva-brahmacāriṇīyam apūrva-tapasyā-balāt bhavad-abhimmatāpūrva-mūlya-pradānena bhavataṁ santoṣya mautikāny ādāsyate vayam tu gṛhāya calitāḥ sma iti sapraṇayerṣyam iva vividha-sāttvikollāsa-vilasitāh kiñcit tiryag-grīvābhirāmam smita-śabalita-nayanāpāṅgena kṣaṇe kṣaṇe mām avolokayantyas tathāvidhām vividha-sāttvika-bhāvollaṣa vilasiyām rādhām puraskṛtya rādhā-kuṇḍa-tīropānta-mañjula-bakula-kuṇja-mandiram tāḥ sarvā jagmuh*

**Перевод:**

**Lalitā scolded Nāndīmukhī. “O amazing renunciate! Stop! Stop!”**  
**Smiling she said to me, “O compliant prince! The field of Vṛndāvana is extraordinary. The seed are extraordinary. The pearls are extraordinary. The merchant selling the pearls is extraordinary. The price is extraordinary. But we are not extraordinary. How can we pay the extraordinary price? But this Nāndīmukhī is affectionate to us and non-different from us. You are an extraordinary brahmacārī. She is an extraordinary brahmacāriṇī. By the strength of her extraordinary austerity she will satisfy you by giving an extraordinary price agreeable to you and take the pearls. But we are going home!”**  
**Saying this with a mixture of love and hatred, showing various ecstatic symptoms, they looked at me from moment to moment from the edges of their smiling eyes while tilting their heads. Putting in front Rādhā, who also displayed various ecstatic symptoms, they went to a bower of attractive bakula trees on the bank of Rādhā-kuṇḍa.**

*tataḥ paramānanda-sandohena mayā vicitya vicitya paramottama-mauktika-saṅcayaiḥ sva-hastena vicitra-śilpa-kalpanayā rādhāṅga-pratyaṅgābharaṇāni viracayya suvarṇa- sampuṭe nidhāya tan-nāma-mudrayā cihnitīkṛtya lalitā-viśākhādi-sakhī-maṇḍalīnāṁ ca bhūṣaṇāni tathaiva nirmāya pṛthak pṛthak sampuṭe nyasya tat-tan-nāma-mudrayā cihnitīkṛtya tathaivāgrathitāny apy uttama-mauktikāni bahūni ca nāndīmukhyā saha*  
*madhumaṅgala-subala-tat-kālāgatojjvala-vasanta-kokilādi-hastena rādhā-kuṇḍa- nikuñja-mandire prahitāni \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then, in complete bliss, thinking and thinking, after making ornaments by my own hand for all of Rādhā’s limbs with amazing artistic skill, using piles of the best pearls, I placed them in a golden box and marked it with her name. Making ornaments for Lalitā, Viśākhā and others, I placed them in separate boxes and wrote their names on the boxes. I took the unstrung excellent pearls and, with Nāndīmukhī, using the assistance of Madhumaṅgala, Subala and the recent arrivals Ujjvala, Vasanta and Kokila, placed them in a pavilion in a bower at Rādhā-kuṇḍa.**

*rādhayā smita-lalita-lalitā-viśākhādi-sakhībhiḥ samaṁ smita-śavalita-harṣeṇādāya sādara-madhura-pracuratara-pakvānna-tāmbūla-vīṭikābhir madhumaṅgalaṁ praṇaya- rūpa-gandha-candanair vara-tāmbūlaiś ca subalādikaṁ ca santoṣya tad-dhastena sva- hasta-sampādita-surabhi-sukumārāruṇa-kusuma-vicitrita-kāñcana-yūthikā-mālya- karpūra-vāsita-tāmbūlopaḍhaukanena nirbharam ānandito’haṁ praṇayādhikyena tan- mālya-bhūṣitas tat-tāmbūlam upayuñjānaḥ sakhibhiḥ saha gosambhālanāya govardhanam āgataḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Rādhā along with her smiling friends Lalitā and Viśākhā received these boxes with joy and smiles. Madhumaṅgala was satisfied with a great quantity of delightful cooked rice and betel as a sign of respect. Subala and others were satisfied with scents, sandalwood and betel, as a sign of affection. I became blissful by gifts of a garland of fragrant, young, pink golden yūthika flowers made by Rādhā, as well as camphor scented betel, presented by her. Affectionately wearing the garland and chewing the betel, I went along with my friends to herd the cows at Govardhana.**

*tataś ca svarṇa-sampuṭaṁ samudhghāṭya lalitā tad-alaṅkaraṇena sānandam ānanditāṁ rādhāṁ prasādhayāmāsa \|*

**Перевод:**

**Opening the golden box, Lalitā blissfully dressed Rādhā with the ornaments.**

*tad anu lalitā-viśākhādayaḥ sakhyo’pi parasparaṁ tat-tat-sampuṭābharaṇenātmānaṁ bhūṣayāmāsuḥ \| tatas tāḥ sva-sva-gṛhe gatvā tat-pracuratarādbhuta-mauktika- pradānena sva-sva-patiṁ sva-sva-gurūṁś ca paraṁ santoṣya punar gāndharvā-saras- tīra-gāndharvā-sthānīm avāpya mitho man-madhura-madhura-narma-vārtā-vinodena sukhaṁ vijahruḥ \|*

**Перевод:**

**Then Lalitā, Viśākhā and other friends dressed each other with the ornaments from the other boxes. They went home and satisfied their elders and husbands sufficiently by giving great quantities of astonishing pearls. They then went to Rādhā-kuṇḍa and amused themselves with talks about my sweet jokes.**

***satyā*** *– gokula-vilāsārāma-matta-kokila ! tatas tataḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Satyabhāmā said, “O cuckoo mad with enjoying the pastimes of Gokula! Then what happened?”**

***kṛṣṇaḥ*** *– priye ! śrutaṁ śrotavyam \| tad alaṁ tad-ati-vārtayeti bruvann eva tan- madhura-rahasya-keli-vṛttāntodghāṭana-vaikalyātiśayenādhairyaṁ –*  
*mat-kaṇṭhasya suvarṇa-bandhura-maṇi-vrātollasan-mālikā*  
*mac-chabda-grahayor alaṁ parilasat-svarṇāvataṁsa-dvayī \|*  
*mat-kāyasya sugandhi-kuṅkuma-lasac-carcā parā sā kadā*  
*hāhā yāsyati dṛk-pathaṁ mama punaḥ puṇyair agaṇyair iha \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Kṛṣṇa said, “O beloved! You have heard what should be heard. That is enough.”**  
**He became extremely unsteady because of confusion arising from the manifestation of the sweet, confidential pastimes.**  
**“She is a garland of shining gems and gold on my neck. She is two glittering gold earrings on my ears. She is the shining unguent of scented kuṅkuma on my body. When will I see the supreme Rādhā by some inestimable good fortune?”**

*iti kṣaṇaṁ maunam ālambya punaḥ sautsukyaṁ –*  
*mad-vakṣaḥ-sthala-campakāvalir iyaṁ man-netra-padma-dvayī*  
*saudhāsiktir iyaṁ mad-eka-vilasat-sarvāṅga-lakṣmīr iyam \|*  
*mat-prāṇoru-vihaṅga-vallarir iyaṁ mat-kāmita-śrīr iyaṁ*  
*maj-jīvātur iyaṁ mayā punar aho hā hā kadā lapsyate \|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Remaining silent for a moment, he again spoke, with eagerness. “She is a garland of campaka flowers on my chest. She is a sprinkling of nectar on my lotus eyes. She is my one Lakṣmī with shining limbs. She is the creeper for the excellent bird of my life air. She is my cherished wealth. She is my life sustaining medicine. When will I attain her again?”**

*iti vilapan sāśru-dhāras tal-līlā-smaraṇa-vihvalaṁ bhūmau nipatya sa-śabdaṁ rudantaṁ madhumaṅgalam āliṅgya tad anu – prāṇa-vallabhe ! tvam eva jīvātu-rūpā rādhāsīti sa-kampaṁ sa-gadgadaṁ lapan tāṁ satyāṁ pariṣvajya muhur muhur dīrgham uṣṇaṁ ca uccair niśasann āsīt \|*

**Перевод:**

**Lamenting in this way, he began weeping. He fell to the ground, agitated by remembering the pastime. He cried loudly and embraced Madhumaṅgala. He then said, “O friend dear to my life! O Rādhā, you are my life-sustaining medicine.” Trembling, with choked voice, he lamented. He embraced Satyabhāmā and breathed hot air loudly, again and again.**

***satyabhāmā*** *ca – sambhrameṇa sāśru-romāñcā nija-śāṭikāñcalena taṁ vījayantī tūṣṇīm āsīt\|*

**Перевод:**

**Satyabhāmā, with tears and hairs standing on end, while fanning him reverently with the edge of her cloth, remained silent.**

*ity akhila-vṛttāntaṁ paurṇamāsī-śiṣyā samañjasā-mukhād ākarṇya saromāñcaṁ sakautukaṁ savyathaṁ* ***lakṣmaṇā*** *prāha – sakhi samañjase ! tatas tataḥ ?*

**Перевод:**

**Hearing this story from the mouth of Samañjasā, the student of Paurṇamāsī named Lakṣmaṇā, with hairs standing on end, said with curiosity and pain, “O my friend Samañjasā! What happened next?”**

***samañjasā*** *-- tataḥ prathamam atisambhrameṇa kṣaṇaṁ tūṣṇīṁ sthitvā tad anu – prāṇanātha ! nikhila-vraja-janaika-jīvana ! jaya jaya dhairyam avalambasva dhairyam avalambasva \| samāśvāsīhi samāśvāsīhīti vaiyagrya-viśaṅkaṭa-dhvaniṁ bhāṣamāṇayā muhur muhur vijanena mṛdu mṛdu madhurāṅga-mārjanena gokula-gamanārtha- prārthanādibhir eva nija-jīvita-nāthaṁ śanaiḥ śanaiḥ sandhukṣitīkṛtya*

**Перевод:**

**Samañjasā said, “At first Satyabhāmā remained silent in great respect. Then she said, “O lord of my life! O sole life of all Vraja! Victory! Victory! Remain steady! Remain steady! Please recover! Please recover!”**  
**Speaking with pain and worry, she constantly fanned him and wiped his sweet limbs gently, requesting that he return to Gokula. Gradually by these means, she brought the lord her life to consciousness.**

*nijānanta-sukha-sudhā-sindhūn nijānanta-prāṇa-parasparām api tṛṇavad dhruvam anapekṣya sadā cikīrṣita-nija-paramābhīṣṭa-tat-sukhābhāsa-lava-leśayā tac-caraṇa-paṅkajaika-gatyā satyayā tadānīm eva sakhī-dvārā tatrānīta-śrīmad-uddhava-mantri-rāja sannirūpite samāgāmini paraśvo’hani madhura-dadhyannādi-bhojanānantaraṁ guru-dina-sita-daśamī-dhaniṣṭhābha-śubha-yoga-sambhāvita-vividha-guṇābhirājitābhijin-nāma-san-muhūrta-vare sarvato nairvighnyena jhaṭiti samasta-praśasta-śastotpādana- puraḥsara-gokula-pura-praveśa-sampādayitrīṁ parama-maṅgala-kulojjvalita-yātrāṁ vidhāya*

**Перевод:**

**Disregarding her own infinite oceans of sweet happiness and the infinite series of her life airs, regarding them like grass, Satyabhāmā had made her only goal Kṛṣṇa’s lotus feet. Desiring to give the supreme object of her life a small speck of happiness, she had her friend bring the chief minister Uddhava. They decided to make a most auspicious journey to Gokula, bearing the most auspicious gifts, a few days later-- on Thursday, during the waxing moon tenth day, with the moon in Dhaniṣṭhā constellation, during an auspicious yoga, during the hour of Abhijit (noon), possessing various good qualities, to avoid all obstacles.**

*savinaya-nirbandhena pūjya-caraṇa-śrīmad-agraja-mahānubhāvaṁ gokula- gamanārtham atyutkaṇṭhitayam api dvārakā-purābhibhāvanārtham abhisaṁrakṣya tatrabhavatī śrī-bhagavatī-pāda-padmān puro nidhāya sārdham uddhava- rohiṇīśvarībhyāṁ sakala-maṅgalāliṅgito madhumaṅgalālaṅkṛtaḥ śrī-śrīmad-vraja- nava-yuvarājas tat-kṣaṇād eva drutam itaś calitvā śrīmatā nandīghoṣa-rathena śrī- gokulopaśalyam āsādya*

**Перевод:**

**Though Balarāma also wanted to go to Gokula, he stayed to protect Dvārakā by their polite insistence. Placing Paurṇamāsī in front, with Uddhava, queen Rohinī and Madhumaṅgala, the prince of Vraja departed quickly from Dvārakā at that time and arrived at the outskirts of Gokula on his chariot Nandīghoṣa.**

*pramada-sambhṛta-cintābhilaṣita-nijābhilaṣita-nijābhīra-śṛṅgāra-nikareṇāti-bhrājamānaḥ san śrīmati nija-vraja-pure śubha-praveśam avaśyaṁ kariṣyatīti sudṛḍhaṁ sarva-sammatyā nirṇītam astīti tat-pariveśita-madhura-samācāra- sudhāsāram ānandāsāra-samplutā śravaṇa-caṣakaiḥ samācamya parama- saubhāgyavatī-śiromaṇi-mañjarī-satyabhāmāsamakakṣa-paṭṭa-mahiṣī sakala- sallakṣaṇa-guṇa-lakṣojjvalita-lakṣmaṇā sautsukyaṁ sālalāpa -- sakhi samañjase ! etan- madhura-rasa-vārtā-śravaṇād atīva utkaṇṭhitāhaṁ śrī-yādavendreṇa sārdhaṁ goṣṭhendra-goṣṭham avāpya rādhā-sakhya-puṣpa-saurabhyenātmānaṁ vāsayitum abhilaṣāmi \|*

**Перевод:**

**They all agreed that they would make an auspicious entrance into Vraja. They were was shining with decorations befitting cowherds, their preferred identity for which they were anxious, which gave them joy.**  
**Filled with the essence of bliss, Lakṣmaṇā, a queen of Dvārakā in the category of Satyabhāmā, a bud from the crest jewel of auspiciousness, sipped with the cups of her ears the intense sweetness of the sweet news of their entry into Gokula. Lakṣmaṇā, shining with hundreds of thousands of good qualities, said with eagerness, “O my friend Samañjasā! From hearing this story filled with mādhurya-rasa I have developed intense longing. I desire to go to that cowherd village with Kṛṣṇa and perfume myself with the fragrance of the flower of friendship with Rādhā.”**

***samañjasā –*** *sakhi tathaiva sarvathā bhavatu bhavatyā iti \|*

**Перевод:**

**Samañjasā said, “You will certinaly attain this.”**

*ādadānas tṛṇaṁ dantair idaṁ yāce punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*śrīmad-rūpa-padāmbhoja-dhūliḥ syāṁ janma-janmani \|\|1\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Putting grass between my teeth I beg again and again that I will attain the dust from the lotus feet of Śrī Rūpa birth after birth.**

*yasyājñā-sudhayā prabodhita-dhiyā muktā-caritrair mayā*  
*gucchaḥ puṣpa-bharair vyadhāyi ya iha śrī-rūpa-saṁśikṣayā \|*  
*jīvākhyasya mad-eka-jīvita-tanos tasyaiva dṛk-ṣaṭpadī*  
*ghrāṇais taṁ paribhūṣitaṁ na tanutāṁ tat-keli-śīdhūtka-dhīḥ \|\|2\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**By the nectar of his order, my intelligence has been awakened. I have arranged this bunch of flowers composed of the story of the pearls following the teachings of Śrī Rūpa. Jīva, who is the life of my body, whose eyes are like bees, uses his intelligence to find the nectar of the pastimes. May he make this work superior by smelling it!**

*muktā-caritra-puṣpaughair gucchaṁ gumphitam adbhutam\|*  
*vataṁsatu mat-snehāt śrīmad-rūpa-gaṇo rahaḥ \|\|3\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**A pearl necklace has been amazingly strung from a flood of flowers in the form of the story of the pearls. Out of affection for me, may the followers of Rūpa quickly decorate their ears with it!**

*yasya saṅga-balato’dbhutā mayā*  
*mauktikottama-kathā pracāritā \|*  
*tasya kṛṣṇa-kavi-bhūpater vraje*  
*saṅgatir bhavatu me bhave bhave \|\|4\|\|*

**Перевод:**

**Life after life may I have the association of Kṛṣṇa das Kavirāja in Vraja. It is by the strength of his association that I have made the story of the pearls.**
